        win.StartCapture("broadside"); Key(global::Godot.Key.H); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.H && Settings.Keys.ContainsKey("Battleship.broadside"), "broadside rebound to H and saved");
        win.StartCapture("broadside"); Key(global::Godot.Key.G); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.G && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "firemode") == global::Godot.Key.H,
              "binding a taken key (G, fire mode's) swaps the two");
        win.StartCapture("broadside"); Key(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.G, "a reserved key (W) is refused");
        win.StartCapture("broadside"); Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.G, "V is warp, a fixed key: refused too");
        win.StartCapture("pd"); Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 1 && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "pd") == global::Godot.Key.Q, "Esc cancels a capture without closing the window");
        Nodes<Button>(win).First(b => b.Name == "ResetKeys").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.F && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "firemode") == global::Godot.Key.G, "reset restores defaults (fire mode on G)");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 0, "K closes it");
        // KEYS FROM BEFORE THE BROADSIDE, in this machine's settings file: fire mode moved onto F (so
        // the missile went to G), and the reload kept on T. The broadside takes the missile's key --
        // never F beside fire mode -- and the battleship's reload, which is gone, keeps none.
        Settings.Keys.Clear();
        Settings.Keys["Battleship.firemode"] = (int)global::Godot.Key.F; Settings.Keys["Battleship.missile"] = (int)global::Godot.Key.G;
        Settings.Keys["Battleship.reload"] = (int)global::Godot.Key.T;
        Settings.Save(); Settings.Load();
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.G && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "firemode") == global::Godot.Key.F
              && !Settings.Keys.ContainsKey("Battleship.missile") && !Settings.Keys.ContainsKey("Battleship.reload"),
              $"an old settings file: the missile's key (G) is the broadside's, fire mode keeps F alone, the reload's key is dropped ({Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside")})");
        Settings.Keys.Clear(); Settings.Save();

        // ── dummies ──
        var d1 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
        var d2 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("PracticeFighter1");            // where dummy 2 stood: two practice fighters
        var pf2 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("PracticeFighter2");
        var d3 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy3");
        Check(Count<TargetDummy>() == 4 && new[] { d1.NetId, d2.NetId, pf2.NetId, d3.NetId }.Distinct().Count() == 4
              && d2.Fighter && pf2.Fighter && d2.HitRadius < 20f && !d1.Fighter && !d3.Fighter,
              "two dummies and two practice light fighters (small craft), all with distinct ids");

        // ── battleship main guns: cursor aim, hold Space, salvo == staggered ──
        var dummy = d1;
        await Park(dummy.Position + new Vector2(0, 600));
        AimWorld(dummy.Position); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.GetGlobalMousePosition().DistanceTo(dummy.Position) < 3f, "test cursor lands on the dummy");
        await Wait(2.5);
        var mainT = Nodes<Turret>(ship).First(x => !x.PointDefense);
        float aimErr = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(mainT.GlobalRotation, (dummy.Position - mainT.GlobalPosition).Angle()));
        Check(aimErr < 0.02f, $"main guns point at the cursor (off {aimErr:0.000} rad)");
        Check(Mathf.Abs(ship.Rotation) < 0.001f, "the hull does not turn toward the cursor");
        dummy.ResetMeter(); await Wait(1.0);
        Check(dummy.Total == 0, "no fire without Space");

        ship.Staggered = false; dummy.ResetMeter();
        // shells take ~1 s to arrive: wait for the last ones before reading the total
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(20.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(1.5);
        double salvo = dummy.Total; await Wait(1.2);
        ship.Staggered = true; dummy.ResetMeter();
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(20.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(1.5);
        double stag = dummy.Total;
        GD.Print($"  salvo {salvo:0.0} dmg / 20s;  staggered {stag:0.0} / 20s;  sheet {ship.Stats.MainDps:0.000} DPS");
        double want20 = ship.Stats.MainDps * 20, oneShot = ship.Stats["main_damage"];
        Check(Math.Abs(salvo - want20) <= 4 * oneShot + 0.01, $"salvo delivers the sheet's {ship.Stats.MainDps:0.0} DPS (±1 salvo)");
        Check(Math.Abs(stag - want20) <= oneShot + 0.01, $"staggered delivers the sheet's {ship.Stats.MainDps:0.0} DPS (±1 shot)");
        bool before = ship.Staggered; Key(global::Godot.Key.G); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.Staggered != before, "G toggles salvo / staggered");

        // ── THE BROADSIDE (F): the main turrets swing onto the cursor through a 0.5 s wind-up, then
        //    every main gun fires three volleys 0.25 s apart; a 14 s cooldown; the ship steers throughout ──
        Check(Abilities.For(ShipClass.Battleship).Where(a => !a.Open).Select(a => a.Id).SequenceEqual(new[] { "guns", "firemode", "broadside", "pd" })
              && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.F,
              "the battleship's bar: guns, fire mode, the BROADSIDE on F, point defence -- no missile, no reload");
        Check(ship.Stats["broadside_volleys"] == 3 && ship.Stats["broadside_mult"] == 1.0 && ship.Stats["broadside_windup"] == 0.5
              && ship.Stats["broadside_gap"] == 0.25 && ship.Stats["broadside_cooldown"] == 14 && ship.Stats["missile_mag"] == 0,
              "the broadside: 3 volleys at a shell's own damage, a 0.5 s wind-up, 0.25 s between volleys, a 14 s cooldown");
        {
            // Aimed well AWAY first (south-east, ~135 degrees off the dummy): at their own 90 deg/s the
            // turrets would still be 90 degrees off when a 0.5 s wind-up ends, so only a wind-up that
            // really swings them onto the cursor can put every shell on the dummy.
            var mainsB = Nodes<Turret>(ship).Where(x => !x.PointDefense).ToList();
            float Off(Turret t) => Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(t.GlobalRotation, (d1.Position - t.GlobalPosition).Angle()));
            AimWorld(ship.Position + new Vector2(450, 450)); await Wait(2.5);
            float off0 = mainsB.Min(Off);
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); d1.ResetMeter();
            var seen = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Shot>(Shells());
            var volleys = new System.Collections.Generic.List<(double t, int n)>();
            double bc0 = ship.Clock, cdAtLast = -1, speedAtFire = -1; float offAtFire = -1; string barAiming = null, barFiring = null;
            AimWorld(d1.Position);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F);
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W);                                  // under way through the wind-up
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2; i++)
            {
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                AimWorld(d1.Position);                                     // the camera follows the ship: keep the cursor on the dummy
                if (barAiming == null && ship.BroadsideWindupLeft > 0) barAiming = AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "broadside", H.Selected).Line;
                int fresh = Shells().Count(s => seen.Add(s));
                if (fresh == 0) continue;
                if (volleys.Count == 0) { offAtFire = mainsB.Max(Off); speedAtFire = ship.SpeedAhead; }
                volleys.Add((ship.Clock - bc0, fresh));
                if (volleys.Count == 1) barFiring = AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "broadside", H.Selected).Line;
                if (volleys.Count == 3) cdAtLast = ship.BroadsideCooldownLeft;
            }
            KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            GD.Print($"  broadside: turrets {off0:0.00} rad off at the press, {offAtFire:0.000} at the first volley; volleys at {string.Join(", ", volleys.Select(v => $"{v.t:0.000}s x{v.n}"))}");
            Check(barAiming == "AIMING" && barFiring == "FIRING", $"the slot reads AIMING through the wind-up, FIRING through the volleys ({barAiming}, {barFiring})");
            Check(off0 > 2.0 && offAtFire >= 0 && offAtFire < 0.03,
                  $"the wind-up swings every main turret onto the cursor from ~135 degrees away -- far faster than their own 90 deg/s ({off0:0.00} -> {offAtFire:0.000} rad)");
            Check(volleys.Count == 3 && volleys.All(v => v.n == 4) && volleys[0].t > 0.45 && volleys[0].t < 0.6
                  && Math.Abs(volleys[1].t - volleys[0].t - 0.25) < 0.035 && Math.Abs(volleys[2].t - volleys[1].t - 0.25) < 0.035,
                  $"three volleys of all four guns: the first at the end of the 0.5 s wind-up, then 0.25 s apart ({volleys.Count} volleys)");
            Check(speedAtFire > 10, $"the ship steers throughout: under way through the wind-up ({speedAtFire:0} u/s ahead at the first volley)");
            await Wait(0.6);
            Check(Math.Abs(d1.Total - 268.5) < 0.01, $"every shell lands: 3 volleys x 4 shells x 22.375 (17.9 x1.25 by the chips) = 268.5 ({d1.Total:0.000})");
            Check(cdAtLast > 13.9 && cdAtLast <= 14 && AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "broadside", H.Selected).Busy > 0.8f,
                  $"the 14 s cooldown starts with the last volley, and the slot shows it ({cdAtLast:0.00} s)");
            int shellsBefore = Shells().Count(s => seen.Add(s));
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.8);
            Check(ship.BroadsideWindupLeft == 0 && ship.BroadsideVolleysLeft == 0 && Shells().Count(s => seen.Add(s)) == 0,
                  "F during the cooldown does nothing");
            ship.Sl("broadside").Cool = 0.02;                       // the slot by name, not a private field
            await Wait(0.1); Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(ship.BroadsideWindupLeft > 0.4, $"once the cooldown is over, F winds up again ({ship.BroadsideWindupLeft:0.00} s)");
            await Wait(1.5);
            Check(Missiles().All(t => t.HostileFire), "a battleship launches no missile of its own");
        }

        // ── the main guns fire SHELLS: straight, at 650 u/s (the battleship's), damage on arrival ──
        {
            var gs = Me; var gd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            var gKeep = gs.Position; gs.Position = gd.Position + new Vector2(0, 520); gs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; gs.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.6);
            AimWorld(gd.Position); await Wait(1.2);                                   // turrets swing onto it
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); gd.ResetMeter();
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, Pressed = true });
            Shot sh1 = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 90 && sh1 == null; i++) { AimWorld(gd.Position); sh1 = Shells().FirstOrDefault(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, Pressed = false });
            double atFire = gd.Total;
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var sp0 = sh1.Position; var sd0 = sh1.Dir;
            for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float shSpd = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(sh1) ? (sh1.Position - sp0).Length() / (10f / 60f) : 0f;
            Check(Math.Abs(shSpd - 650) < 6f && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(sh1) && sh1.Dir == sd0 && atFire == 0,
                  $"the guns fire shells at 650 u/s, straight, and nothing lands at once ({shSpd:0} u/s)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 90 && gd.Total == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(gd.Total > 0 && Math.Abs(gd.Total % gs.Stats["main_damage"]) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(gs.Stats["main_damage"] - 22.375) < 1e-9, $"a shell lands for 17.9 x1.25 by the chips = 22.375 ({gd.Total:0.000})");
            // the balance: guns 4 x 17.9 every 2 s = 35.8 DPS, and a broadside of 3 x 4 x 17.9 = 214.8 every
            // 15 s (the wind-up, two gaps, the cooldown): 50.12 DPS in all. The carrier reaches 1.5x as far.
            var bsS = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Battleship); var caS = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier);
            double bsTotal = bsS.MainDps + bsS.BroadsideDps + bsS.MissileDps;
            Check(Math.Abs(bsS.MainDps - 35.8) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(bsS.BroadsideDamage - 214.8) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(bsS.BroadsideCycle - 15) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(bsTotal - 50.12) < 0.001,
                  $"the battleship: guns 35.8 DPS and a 214.8-damage broadside every 15 s -- 50.12 DPS in all, no missile ({bsTotal:0.000})");
            Check(Math.Abs(caS["control_range"] - 1500) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(bsS["main_range"] - 1000) < 1e-6,
                  "the carrier's fighters reach 1500 u, 1.5x the battleship's 1000 u guns");
            gs.Position = gKeep; gs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.5);
        }

        // ── warp (V): every capital ship; a hull cooldown, not a slot ──
        {
            var me = Me; var warpHome = me.Position; var d2w = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy3");
            void Ready() { typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_warpCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(me, 0.0); me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.Rotation = 0f; }
            void Deselect() => H.ClearSelection();     // the selection is a SET now, and the Hub owns clearing it
            Check(Abilities.For(me.Class).All(a => a.Id != "warp"), "warp takes no slot on the ability bar");
            Ready(); Deselect(); var start = me.Position;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(me.Warping && me.Position.DistanceTo(start) < 20f, "V starts a 3 s warm-up; the ship holds until it jumps");
            await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup);
            var moved = me.Position - start;
            Check(!me.Warping && Math.Abs(moved.Length() - 1200f) < 40f && Mathf.Abs(Vector2.Up.AngleTo(moved)) < 0.05f,
                  $"nothing selected ahead: 1200 u straight ahead ({moved.Length():0} u)");
            Check(Math.Abs(me.WarpCooldownLeft - PlayerShip.WarpCooldown) < 0.6 && PlayerShip.WarpCooldown == 30, "then a 30 s cooldown");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!me.Warping, "V does nothing while it cools down");
            // a target within 45 degrees of the bow: arrive just short of it
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, 900); await Wait(0.3);
            Click(Scr(d2w.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup + 0.2);
            float want = d2w.HitRadius + Classes.Art(me.Class).Length * 0.5f + PlayerShip.WarpStandoff;
            double noseGap = me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position) - d2w.HitRadius - Classes.Art(me.Class).Length * 0.5f;
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position) - want) < 20f && noseGap > 120,
                  $"a target ahead: it warps to short of it, and a hull most of 400 u long stops with its NOSE {noseGap:0} u clear of the target's circle, not through it ({me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position):0} u, wanted {want:0})");
            // FURTHER OFF THAN THE DRIVE REACHES: the heading is the target's, the distance is the
            // drive's. An aimed jump used to arrive whatever the distance, so anything selected was
            // one press away however far off it stood.
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, 3000); var far0 = me.Position; await Wait(0.3);
            Click(Scr(d2w.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup + 0.2);
            var farMoved = me.Position - far0;
            Check(Math.Abs(farMoved.Length() - 1200f) < 40f && farMoved.Y < 0 && me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position) > 1700,
                  $"a target 3000 u ahead: 1200 u toward it, not onto it ({farMoved.Length():0} u moved, {me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position):0} u still to go)");
            // a target behind: outside the cone, so straight ahead instead
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, -900); var s3 = me.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup + 0.2);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Position - s3).Y + 1200f) < 40f && H.Selected == d2w, "a target behind (outside 45 degrees): straight ahead instead");
            // aimed when it JUMPS, by the heading then: swing onto a target during the charge...
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, -900); await Wait(0.2);          // facing north, d2 behind
            Click(Scr(d2w.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(1.0);
            me.Rotation = Mathf.Pi; await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup - 1.0 + 0.2);                // now facing it
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position) - want) < 20f, "V pressed facing away, then turned onto the target: it warps to the target");
            // ...or turn away from it, and it goes straight on along the new heading
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, 1400); await Wait(0.2);           // facing north, d2 ahead
            var s4 = me.Position;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(1.0);
            me.Rotation = Mathf.Pi / 2f; await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup - 1.0 + 0.2);            // now facing east
            var mv = me.Position - s4;
            Check(Math.Abs(mv.X - 1200f) < 40f && Math.Abs(mv.Y) < 40f, $"V pressed facing the target, then turned east: 1200 u east ({mv.X:0}, {mv.Y:0})");
            // the radar: click a marker to select it -- an enemy as the target, a landmark as a waypoint
            // beside the base: every landmark it clicks below is within the radar's 3600 u reach from here
            Ready(); me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 300); me.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.6);
            var radar = Nodes<Radar>().First(); var rr = radar.GetGlobalRect(); float rad = rr.Size.X / 2f;
            Vector2 OnRadar(Vector2 w) => rr.Position + new Vector2(rad, rad) + (w - me.ViewPosition) * ((rad - 6f) / Radar.Range);
            H.ClearSelection();
            Click(OnRadar(d2w.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == d2w, "clicking an enemy on the radar makes it the target");
            Click(OnRadar(Hub.BasePos)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == null && H.Waypoint == Hub.BasePos && H.WaypointName == "BASE", "clicking the base on the radar makes it a waypoint (and drops the target)");
            var ne = new Vector2(2121.3f, -2121.3f);
            Click(OnRadar(ne)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.WaypointName == "OUTPOST NE" && H.Waypoint is { } wp && wp.DistanceTo(ne) < 1f, $"an outpost is on the radar: clicking it makes OUTPOST NE the waypoint ({H.WaypointName})");
            // ONE TABLE BEHIND THE SCOPE (Hub.ScopeMarks): what can be picked is what is drawn.
            // There were two lists, and they had drifted -- MISSION PORTAL was pickable with no
            // glyph of its own, so the only way to that waypoint was a click on empty scope.
            var scopeAtHome = H.ScopeMarks().ToList();
            var wantMarks = new[] { "BASE", "THREAT INTELLIGENCE", "RECYCLER", "PORTAL", "SALVAGE FIELD", "MINING BELT",
                                    "OUTPOST SE", "OUTPOST NE", "OUTPOST NW", "OUTPOST SW" };
            Check(scopeAtHome.Count == 10 && wantMarks.All(n => scopeAtHome.Count(smk => smk.Name == n) == 1),
                  $"the scope's marks at home are the 10 landmarks, once each ({scopeAtHome.Count}: {string.Join(", ", scopeAtHome.Select(smk => smk.Name))})");
            Check(scopeAtHome.All(smk => smk.Name.Length > 0 && smk.Pick > 0f && smk.Size.X > 0f && smk.Size.Y > 0f && smk.Colour.A > 0f),
                  $"every one of the {scopeAtHome.Count} marks that can be picked carries a glyph -- a size and a colour to draw it with");
            foreach (var smk in scopeAtHome)
            {
                Click(OnRadar(smk.At)); await Wait(0.05);
                Check(H.WaypointName == smk.Name && H.Waypoint is { } mkAt && mkAt.DistanceTo(smk.At) < 1f,
                      $"{smk.Name} is drawn on the scope at ({smk.At.X:0}, {smk.At.Y:0}) and picks there as itself ({H.WaypointName})");
            }
            Click(OnRadar(Hub.BasePos)); await Wait(0.05);
            me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 1500); me.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.2);    // the base dead ahead
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup + 0.2);
            float wantB = 260f + Classes.Art(me.Class).Length * 0.5f + PlayerShip.WarpStandoff;
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - wantB) < 20f, $"a warp to the waypoint lands just short of the base ({me.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos):0} u)");
            H.ClearSelection();
            Ready(); me.Position = warpHome;
        }

        // ── battleship PD: idle until Q; slow independent turrets ──
        await Park(new Vector2(780, 700), 0);                      // among all three dummies
        foreach (var d in Nodes<TargetDummy>()) d.ResetMeter();
        await Wait(2.0);
        Check(Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total) == 0 && !ship.PdActive, "PD does nothing until activated");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(4.0);
        var pds = ship.PdTurrets;
        Check(ship.PdActive && pds.All(x => x.Target != null) && pds.Select(x => x.Target).Distinct().Count() == pds.Count,
              $"Q: each battleship PD turret picks its own target ({string.Join(",", pds.Select(x => (x.Target as TargetDummy)?.Number))})");
        Check(Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total) > 0, "PD fires while active");
        Check(ship.Stats["pd_turn"] < Mathf.Tau / 2.9f, "battleship PD turns slowly (τ/3)");
        await Wait(11.5);
        Check(!ship.PdActive && ship.PdRechargeLeft > 14, "the window closes after 15 s and recharge starts");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(!ship.PdActive, "Q does nothing while recharging");
        double mid = Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total); await Wait(2.0);
        Check(Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total) == mid, "no PD fire while recharging");
        // A readout goes to the dummy with that NUMBER, not that position. The dummies are 1, 3,
        // 4 and 5 (2's spot holds the two practice fighters), so indexing the list by number-1
        // sent 3's figures to 4, 4's to 5, and dropped 5's entirely because 5 > Count: guests
        // read the wrong numbers off the wrong hulls.
        var dummy5 = Nodes<TargetDummy>().First(x => x.Number == 5);
        var dummy4 = Nodes<TargetDummy>().First(x => x.Number == 4);
        double l5 = dummy5.LastSecond, a5 = dummy5.Average10, t5 = dummy5.Total;
        double l4 = dummy4.LastSecond, a4 = dummy4.Average10, t4 = dummy4.Total;
        Priv(H, "NetDummy", 5, 7.0, 8.0, 9.0);
        double got5 = dummy5.Total, got4 = dummy4.Total;                   // read BEFORE restoring
        bool routed = Math.Abs(got5 - 9.0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(got4 - t4) < 1e-9;
        dummy5.SetReadout(l5, a5, t5); dummy4.SetReadout(l4, a4, t4);      // put them back
        Check(routed, $"a dummy's readout reaches the dummy with that NUMBER, not that position (5 got {got5:0.#} of 9, 4 sat at {got4:0.#})");
        // Combat.Clear drops the hooks the world installed on its way out. OnShell was left
        // behind: a lambda holding the Hub, so the next shot called into a freed node, and a Hub
        // that could never be collected once you were back at the menu. Snapshot and restore --
        // this all happens inside one frame, so nothing ever sees the cleared state.
        // The hooks go back through the world's OWN installer (Hub.Hooks), not a list kept here:
        // this check used to restore four of them by hand, so every hook added after it was
        // written -- the slug hook, and then Fx.On -- was silently dropped for the rest of the run,
        // and the effects raised after this point went up on nobody's screen.
        var keepHostiles = Combat.Hostiles.ToList(); var keepPlayers = Combat.Players.ToList();
        Combat.Clear();
        bool hooksDropped = Combat.OnFlash == null && Combat.World == null && Combat.ShotFired == null && Fx.On == null;
        Priv(H, "Hooks");
        Combat.Hostiles.AddRange(keepHostiles); Combat.Players.AddRange(keepPlayers);
        Check(hooksDropped && Combat.OnFlash != null && Combat.World != null && Combat.ShotFired != null && Fx.On != null,
              "Combat.Clear lets go of the world and every hook it installed, and the world puts every one of them back");

        // ── A REFIT, on the battleship: the hull keeps its fraction (keeping the damage taken let a
        //    pilot heal by swapping parts) ──
        {
            var bl = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Battleship);
            double max0 = ship.MaxHp;
            ship.Hp = 75;
            bl[2] = "sh_bulwark_3"; H.PilotChanged();
            double bulMax = ship.MaxHp, bulHp = ship.Hp;
            bl[2] = "sh_glass_3"; H.PilotChanged();
            double glMax = ship.MaxHp, glHp = ship.Hp;
            bl[2] = "basic_deflector"; H.PilotChanged();
            Check(Math.Abs(max0 - 375) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(bulMax - 615) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(bulHp - 123) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(glMax - 300) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(glHp - 60) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(ship.MaxHp - 375) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(ship.Hp - 75) < 1e-6,
                  $"a refit keeps the hull's fraction: 75/375, Bulwark III {bulHp:0}/{bulMax:0}, Glass III {glHp:0}/{glMax:0}, back {ship.Hp:0}/{ship.MaxHp:0} -- no heal by swapping");
            ship.Hp = ship.MaxHp;
        }

        // ── THE DESTROYER: the owner's hull, the smallest of the three; two main guns, missile bursts, the fastest ──
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Destroyer); ship = Me;
        {
            var dspr = ship.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First();
            var dts = Nodes<Turret>(ship).ToList();
            Check(ship.Class == ShipClass.Destroyer && dspr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("destroyer_hull.png") && Math.Abs(dspr.Texture.GetHeight() * dspr.Scale.Y - 212.625f) < 0.5f
                  && Math.Abs(Classes.Art(ShipClass.Destroyer).HalfWidth - 27.8f) < 1e-4 && dts.Count(t => !t.PointDefense) == 2 && dts.Count(t => t.PointDefense) == 2
                  && Math.Abs(Classes.Art(ShipClass.Carrier).Length - 283.5f) < 1e-4 && Math.Abs(Classes.Art(ShipClass.Battleship).Length - 378f) < 1e-4,
                  "the destroyer 25% smaller than the carrier (212.625 u), the carrier 25% smaller than the battleship (283.5 of 378); a 27.8 u half-beam; two main turrets, two PD");
            Check(help.Text.StartsWith("DESTROYER") && help.Text.Contains("mouse aims the main guns"), "controls line is destroyer-specific");
            Check(Abilities.For(ShipClass.Destroyer).Where(a => !a.Open).Select(a => a.Id).SequenceEqual(new[] { "guns", "firemode", "missile", "reload", "pd" })
                  && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Destroyer, "missile") == global::Godot.Key.F && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Destroyer, "reload") == global::Godot.Key.R,
                  "the destroyer's bar: guns, fire mode, the missile burst on F, reload on R, point defence");
            var ddS = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Destroyer); var bsS0 = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Battleship); var cvS0 = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier);
            bool N9(double a, double b) => Math.Abs(a - b) < 1e-9;
            Check(N9(ddS["hull"], 250) && N9(ship.MaxHp, 312.5) && N9(ddS["main_count"], 2) && N9(ddS["pd_count"], 2) && ddS["fighter_count"] == 0 && ddS["broadside_volleys"] == 0,
                  $"250 hull (312.5 with the five chips), two main guns, two PD; no wing, no broadside ({ship.MaxHp:0.0})");
            Check(N9(bsS0["max_speed"], 104) && N9(ddS["max_speed"], 130) && N9(cvS0["max_speed"], 116.48)
                  && N9(ddS["thrust"], 70) && N9(ddS["reverse_thrust"], 30) && N9(ddS["reverse_speed"], 45)
                  && N9(cvS0["thrust"], 48 * 116.48 / 96) && N9(cvS0["reverse_thrust"], 20 * 116.48 / 96) && N9(cvS0["reverse_speed"], 32 * 116.48 / 96)
                  && N9(ddS["turn_radius"], 107) && N9(ddS["turn_rate"], 1.08) && N9(cvS0["turn_radius"], 127) && N9(cvS0["turn_rate"], 0.9),
                  "speeds: the destroyer 130 u/s (the battleship's 104 +25%), the carrier 116.48 (+12%); each class's accelerations and astern speed scale with it; turning unchanged");
            Check(N9(ddS.MainDps, 15.0) && Math.Abs(ddS.MissileDps - 441.0 / 9.8) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(ddS.MainDps + ddS.MissileDps - 60.0) < 0.001,
                  $"the destroyer's damage: guns 11.8 DPS, bursts 2 x 3 x 5 every 16.6 s (1.81) -- 13.61 DPS ({ddS.MainDps + ddS.MissileDps:0.000})");
            // the fastest: flat out for 4 s it is past anything a battleship can reach
            await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 600), Mathf.Pi);
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(4.0);
            double ddAhead = ship.SpeedAhead;
            KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            Check(ddAhead > 0.95 * 130, $"W drives the destroyer to its 130 u/s -- past a battleship's 104 ({ddAhead:0} u/s)");
            await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 600));
            ship.Staggered = false;
            // its two guns: Space fires both (salvo)
            AimWorld(d1.Position); await Wait(1.5);
            var seenD = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Shot>(Shells());
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.15); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Space);
            int ddShells = Shells().Count(s => seenD.Add(s));
            Check(ddShells == 2, $"Space fires the destroyer's two main guns ({ddShells} shells)");
            await Wait(2.0);

            // ── the missile BURST: a magazine of three bursts; a selected target in range, or it refuses ──
            System.Collections.Generic.List<Shot> Burst() => Missiles().Where(x => x.Heavy && !x.HostileFire && Smoke(x).Count < 40).ToList();
            string SlotMsg() => AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "missile", H.Selected) is var st && st.Fail ? st.Line : null;
            Check(ship.Stats["missile_mag"] == 3 && ship.MissilesLoaded == 3, "the destroyer starts with a magazine of three bursts");
            // Esc only if something is selected: with nothing selected, Esc opens the Esc menu
            if (H.Selected != null) { Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.05); }
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Burst().Count == 0 && ship.MissilesLoaded == 3 && SlotMsg() == "NO TARGET", "no target: nothing fires, and the slot says NO TARGET");
            await Wait(PlayerShip.FailShow + 0.1);
            Check(SlotMsg() == null, "the message clears after a moment");
            Click(Scr(d1.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            var keep = ship.Position; ship.Position = d1.Position + new Vector2(0, (float)ship.Stats["missile_range"] + 150f); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Burst().Count == 0 && ship.MissilesLoaded == 3 && SlotMsg() == "OUT OF RANGE", "target out of range: nothing fires, and the slot says OUT OF RANGE");
            ship.Position = keep; ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.1);
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); d1.ResetMeter();
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var burst = Burst();
            var toTarget = (d1.Position - ship.ToGlobal(new Vector2(0, -Classes.Art(ShipClass.Destroyer).Length * 0.5f))).Angle();
            var offs = burst.Select(m => Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(toTarget, m.Dir.Angle()))).OrderBy(a => a).ToList();
            GD.Print($"  burst: {burst.Count} missiles at {string.Join(", ", offs.Select(a => a.ToString("0.0")))} degrees off the line to the target");
            Check(burst.Count == 3 && burst.All(m => m.TargetId == d1.NetId && m.TurnRate == 1.5f) && ship.MissilesLoaded == 2 && d1.Total == 0
                  && Math.Abs(offs[1]) < 3 && offs[0] < -64 && offs[0] > -71 && offs[2] > 64 && offs[2] < 71,
                  "F launches a burst of three guided missiles: one at the target, two launched 70 degrees either side (magazine 2/3); nothing lands at once");
            float mspd = 0;
            if (burst.Count > 0)
            {
                var mp1 = burst[0].GlobalPosition; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                mspd = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(burst[0]) ? (burst[0].GlobalPosition - mp1).Length() * 60f : 0;
            }
            Check(Math.Abs(mspd - 200) < 0.5f, $"they fly at 200 u/s ({mspd:0.0})");
            for (int i = 0; i < 900 && d1.Total < 183.74; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(d1.Total - 183.75) < 0.01, $"all three land -- the outer two curve in: 3 x 49 x1.25 by the chips = 183.75 ({d1.Total:0.00})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.5);        // the 0.4 s between bursts
            int before3 = Burst().Count;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(ship.MissilesLoaded == 0 && Burst().Count - before3 == 3, $"the third burst empties the magazine ({Burst().Count - before3} new missiles, {ship.MissilesLoaded} left)");
            await Wait(0.7);
            var flying = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Shot>(Missiles());
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Missiles().All(flying.Contains), "empty magazine: F does nothing");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(ship.Reloading && AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "reload", H.Selected).Busy > 0.9f, "R starts the reload; the bar shows it");
            await Wait(9.2);
            Check(!ship.Reloading && ship.MissilesLoaded == 3, "reload refills the magazine (3 bursts) after 9 s");
            // guided: move the target sideways and watch the middle missile follow, no faster than 1.5 rad/s
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
            var midM = Burst().OrderBy(m => Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(toTarget, m.Dir.Angle()))).FirstOrDefault(); var dhome = d1.Position;
            d1.Position = dhome + new Vector2(260, 0);
            float a0 = midM?.Dir.Angle() ?? 0; await Wait(1.0);
            float turned = midM != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(midM) ? Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(a0, midM.Dir.Angle())) : 0;
            Check(turned > 0.05 && turned <= 1.51, $"it steers toward the target, no faster than 1.5 rad/s ({turned:0.000} rad in 1 s)");
            await Wait(6.0); d1.Position = dhome;

            // A REFIT: the magazine never holds more than it has room for
            var dl = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Destroyer); var dlKeep = (string[])dl.Clone();
            for (int k = Equipment.CoreSlots; k < Equipment.Slots; k++) dl[k] = "";
            dl[4] = "dd_salvo_3"; H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;
            Check(Math.Abs(ship.Stats["missile_mag"] - 6) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["missile_reload"] - 12.0) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["missile_damage"] - 39.2) < 1e-9,
                  $"Salvo Rack III: 6 bursts a magazine, a 12.0 s reload, 39.2 a missile ({ship.Stats["missile_mag"]}, {ship.Stats["missile_reload"]:0.000}, {ship.Stats["missile_damage"]})");
            ship.Sl("missile").N = 6;
            dl[4] = "dd_missile_rack"; H.PilotChanged();
            Check(ship.MissilesLoaded == 3, $"back to the kit rack: a 3-burst magazine holds 3, not the 6 loaded before ({ship.MissilesLoaded})");
            for (int k = 0; k < Equipment.Slots; k++) dl[k] = dlKeep[k];
            H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;

            // CLOSE IN, the fan narrows so the outer two can still come round: at 70 degrees on a
            // ~107 u turning circle they would circle a target 150 u off the nose until their run ended
            ship.Position = d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 150f + Classes.Art(ShipClass.Destroyer).Length * 0.5f);
            ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; ship.Rotation = 0f;
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); d1.ResetMeter();
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var close = Burst();
            float closeSplay = close.Count == 3 ? close.Max(m => Mathf.Abs(Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(Vector2.Up.Angle(), m.Dir.Angle())))) : -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && d1.Total < 183.74; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(close.Count == 3 && closeSplay > 20 && closeSplay < 50 && Math.Abs(d1.Total - 183.75) < 0.01,
                  $"a target 150 u off the nose: the outer two launch narrower ({closeSplay:0} degrees) and all three land (18.75; {d1.Total:0.00})");
            await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 600));
        }

        // ── carrier ──
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier); ship = Me;
        Check(ship.PdTurrets.Count == 3, "carrier has three PD turrets");

        // bombers park in bays on the deck, half each side of the runway, and stay put as the carrier moves
        Wing[] Bombers() => Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Def.Parks).ToArray();
        async Task CheckBays(int n)
        {
            await Wait(0.3);
            var bs = Bombers();
            int port = bs.Count(w => ship.ToLocal(w.GlobalPosition).X < 0), stbd = bs.Count(w => ship.ToLocal(w.GlobalPosition).X > 0);
            Check(bs.Length == n && bs.All(w => w.Docked && w.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.ToGlobal(ship.Bay(w))) < 0.5f) && port == n / 2 + n % 2 && stbd == n / 2,
                  $"{n} bombers parked in their bays: {port} port, {stbd} starboard");
        }
        await CheckBays(2);
        Character.Bonuses["bomber_count"] = 2.0; ship.SetClass(ship.Class); ship = Me;   // 2 x (1 + 2) = 6
        await CheckBays(6);
        Character.Bonuses.Remove("bomber_count"); ship.SetClass(ship.Class); ship = Me;
        // ── GEAR THROUGH THE SHIP'S OWN REFIT: the owner's hangars, the wing rebuilt to match, and
        //    nothing left behind when craft are taken away ──
        {
            var cl = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Carrier); var clKeep = (string[])cl.Clone();
            for (int k = Equipment.CoreSlots; k < Equipment.Slots; k++) cl[k] = "";
            async Task Hangar(string part) { cl[0] = part; H.PilotChanged(); await Wait(0.1); ship = Me; }
            bool Near(double a, double b) => Math.Abs(a - b) < 1e-6;
            int Craft() => Nodes<Wing>().Count(w => w.Carrier == ship);
            await Hangar("cv_elite_3");
            bool elite = Near(ship.Stats["fighter_count"], 2) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_speed"], 1056) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_damage"], 7.5) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_turn"], 5.25);
            await Hangar("cv_swarm_3");
            bool swarm = Near(ship.Stats["fighter_count"], 9) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_speed"], 274.56) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_damage"], 1.95);
            int swarmCraft = Craft();
            await Hangar("cv_line_3");
            bool line = Near(ship.Stats["fighter_count"], 4) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_damage"], 3.5) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_speed"], 422.4);
            Check(elite && swarm && line, $"the owner's hangars on the carrier itself: Elite III, Swarm III, Line III (elite {elite}, swarm {swarm}, line {line})");
            // a swarm launched, then Elite fitted mid-flight: the extra fighters are freed where they fly
            await Hangar("cv_swarm_3");
            await Park(d3.Position + new Vector2(0, 520), 0);
            H.SelectTarget(d3); Key(global::Godot.Key.Space);
            for (int i = 0; i < 180 && Nodes<Wing>().Count(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter && !w.Inside) < 2; i++)
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            int out0 = Nodes<Wing>().Count(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter && !w.Inside);
            await Hangar("cv_elite_3");
            int eliteCraft = Craft();
            await Hangar("cv_fighter_hangars");
            var wingOrder = (List<Wing>)typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_wings", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(ship);
            int firstBomber = wingOrder.FindIndex(w => w.Def.Parks);
            bool fightersFirst = firstBomber < 0 || wingOrder.Skip(firstBomber).All(w => w.Def.Parks);
            Check(swarmCraft == 11 && out0 >= 2 && eliteCraft == 4 && Craft() == 5 && ship.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter) == 3 && fightersFirst,
                  $"the wing follows the hangar: 9 + 2 craft, then Elite with fighters out -- 2 + 2, the rest freed -- then the kit's 3 + 2; fighters listed first ({swarmCraft}, {out0} out, {eliteCraft}, {Craft()})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(0.2);
            // bombers through gear: the bays re-split, and a strike counted on the old bombers is
            // called off (set up as one in flight, so the old code -- which never touched it -- differs)
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetProperty("StrikeTarget").SetValue(ship, d3);
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_strikesOut", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(ship, 2);
            int armed0 = ship.BombersReady;
            cl[4] = "cv_widebay_3"; H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;
            int armedAfter = ship.BombersReady;
            bool strikeOff = ship.StrikeTarget == null;
            await CheckBays(5);
            cl[4] = "cv_heavybay_1"; H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;
            await CheckBays(1);
            cl[4] = "cv_bomber_bay"; H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;
            await CheckBays(2);
            Check(strikeOff, "a change in the bombers calls off a strike counted on the old ones");
            Check(armed0 == 2 && armedAfter == 2, $"a bay fitted in service adds bombers that arrive rearming, not armed: still 2 ready of 5 ({armed0} -> {armedAfter})");
            for (int k = 0; k < Equipment.Slots; k++) cl[k] = clKeep[k];
            H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;
        }
        ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        Check(Math.Abs(ship.Stats["control_range"] - 1500) < 0.01 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["strike_range"] - 3000) < 0.01,
              "fighter control range 1500 (1.5x the battleship's 1000 u guns); bomber strike range 3000 (2x)");
        var spr = Nodes<Sprite2D>(ship).First();
        Check(spr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("carrier_player.png") && Math.Abs(spr.Texture.GetHeight() * spr.Scale.Y - 283.5f) < 0.5f,
              "carrier flies the owner's line-art sprite at 283.5 u, 25% smaller than the battleship");
        await Park(new Vector2(780, 700), 0);
        var pdLight = H.SpawnRaider(new Vector2(700, 860));            // a third light target (the practice fighters are two)
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(2.0);
        var cpds = ship.PdTurrets;
        var pdts = cpds.Select(x => x.Target).Where(x => x != null).ToList();
        Check(pdts.Distinct().Count() == 3 && pdts.All(t => t is Raider || (t is TargetDummy td && td.Fighter)),
              "carrier PD: three turrets on three different LIGHT targets -- never a plain dummy");
        // two light targets for three turrets: the spare must double up on a light one, never a dummy
        if (GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(pdLight)) pdLight.TakeDamage(9999);
        await Wait(1.5);
        var pdts2 = cpds.Select(x => x.Target).Where(x => x != null).ToList();
        Check(pdts2.Count > 0 && pdts2.All(t => t is Raider || (t is TargetDummy td2 && td2.Fighter)),
              "with more turrets than light targets, the spare turret still never takes a plain dummy");

        // clicks select only; Space attacks; R recalls
        await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(-300, 300));
        Click(Scr(d3.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d3, "carrier: left-click selects dummy 3");
        Click(Scr(d3.Position), MouseButton.Right); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.WingTarget == null && ship.StrikeTarget == null, "carrier: clicks never order an attack");
        Check(Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter).All(w => w.Inside && !w.Visible),
              "idle fighters wait docked inside the carrier, out of sight");
        // parked SMALL, as far below as the deck is: 65% of 28.125 u (the hauler's landed size), facing
        // the bow, the whole of it (18 u across) on the white between the runway's edge (15.2 u out) and
        // the hull's (37.9 u)
        foreach (var bw in Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Def.Parks && w.Docked))
        {
            var lp = ship.ToLocal(bw.Position); var bsp = Nodes<Sprite2D>(bw).First();
            float half = bsp.Texture.GetWidth() * bsp.Scale.X * 0.5f;
            Check(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(bw.Rotation, ship.Rotation)) < 0.01f && Math.Abs(bsp.Texture.GetHeight() * bsp.Scale.Y - 28.125f * 0.65f) < 0.05f
                  && Mathf.Abs(lp.X) - half > 14.7f && Mathf.Abs(lp.X) + half < 36f,
                  $"a parked bomber sits at 65% on the white deck beside the runway, facing the bow ({Mathf.Abs(lp.X) - half:0.0} to {Mathf.Abs(lp.X) + half:0.0} u out)");
        }
        int sig0 = ship.SignalsLit;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.WingTarget == d3 && ship.StrikeTarget == null, "Space sends the fighters only (bombers are their own ability)");
        // fighters leave one at a time, at least 0.83 s apart (a hard limit, not a stat)
        var fts = Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter).ToList();
        for (int fr = 0; fr < 200 && fts.Any(w => w.LaunchedAt < 0); fr++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var order = fts.Select(w => w.LaunchedAt).Where(t => t >= 0).OrderBy(t => t).ToList();
        double minGap = 99; for (int g = 1; g < order.Count; g++) minGap = Math.Min(minGap, order[g] - order[g - 1]);
        GD.Print($"  fighter launches at {string.Join(", ", order.Select(t => t.ToString("0.000")))} s; smallest gap {minGap:0.000} s");
        Check(fts.Count == 3 && ship.Stats["fighter_count"] == 3, "the carrier starts with 3 fighters");
        Check(order.Count == 3 && minGap >= Wings.Of(WingKind.Bomber).LaunchInterval - 1e-9, "fighters launch one at a time, at least 0.83 s apart");
        Check(ship.SignalsLit > sig0 || order.Count == 3, "launching fighters light the hangar signal");
        Check(ship.Stats["torpedo_speed"] == 300 && ship.Stats["launch_range"] == 1900 && ship.Stats["torpedo_interval"] == 0.5,
              "torpedoes: 300 u/s, launched from 1900 u, one every 0.5 s");
        var fx0 = H.GetChildren().OfType<FlashLayer>().FirstOrDefault();
        Check(fx0 != null && !fx0.ZAsRelative && fx0.ZIndex > ship.ZIndex && fx0.ZIndex < ship.ZIndex + Nodes<Turret>(ship).First().ZIndex,
              "hit flashes draw above the hulls and below the turrets");
        // strafing runs: 3 shots, straight through by 1.2x the target's diameter, then round again
        var fx = Nodes<Wing>().First(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter);
        int passes = 0; bool three = true, through = true; string lastPhase = fx.FighterPhase; float worst = 999f;
        for (int i = 0; i < 780; i++)                     // 13 s, inside the first 15 s of firing
        {
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            string ph = fx.FighterPhase;
            if (lastPhase == "Burst" && ph == "Overshoot") { passes++; three &= fx.ShotsThisPass == Wings.Of(WingKind.Fighter).Shots; }
            if (lastPhase == "Overshoot" && ph == "Turn") { through &= fx.LastOvershoot >= Math.Max(1.2 * 2 * d3.HitRadius, 2 * 352 / 3.5) - 0.5; worst = Mathf.Min(worst, fx.LastOvershoot); }
            lastPhase = ph;
        }
        GD.Print($"  fighter: {passes} passes in 13 s; shortest overshoot {worst:0} u (needs {Math.Max(1.2 * 2 * d3.HitRadius, 2 * 352 / 3.5):0}: 1.2 diameters, and the fighter's own 201 u turning circle)");
        Check(passes >= 2 && three, $"fighters strafe: every pass fires exactly 3 shots ({passes} passes)");
        Check(through, "each pass carries on through the target by at least 1.2x its diameter -- and a turning circle -- then turns");
        var fighters = Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && !w.Def.Parks).ToList();
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && !fighters.All(f => f.Resting); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(fighters.All(f => f.Resting), "after 15 s of firing, every fighter heads home to rest");
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && !fighters.All(f => f.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.GlobalPosition) < 1f); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(fighters.All(f => f.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.GlobalPosition) < 1f), "they rest at the centre of the carrier");
        d3.ResetMeter(); await Wait(2.0);
        Check(d3.Total == 0, "no fire while resting");
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && fighters.Any(f => f.Resting); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(fighters.All(f => !f.Resting), "after 3 s they rejoin the fight");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.WingTarget == null, "R recalls the fighters");
        await Wait(3.0); d3.ResetMeter(); await Wait(1.0);
        Check(d3.Total == 0, "recalled fighters stop firing");

        // A SMALL, SLOW TARGET -- the owner's report: fighters flew through an escort's light raiders
        // without firing. A practice light fighter drifting sideways at 12 u/s, a light riding a pinned
        // hauler's pace: a pass that ended 1.2 diameters past it left it inside the fighters' ~100 u
        // turning radius, and they flew laps round it, never firing again until they went home. Every
        // fighter must keep coming back for passes.
        {
            var pf = d2; var pfHome = pf.Position;
            await Park(pf.Position + new Vector2(0, 500), 0);
            var wing = Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && !w.Def.Parks).ToList();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 25 && !wing.All(f => f.Inside && !f.Resting); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Click(Scr(pf.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.05);
            bool onIt = ship.WingTarget == pf;
            var shots = wing.ToDictionary(f => f, f => new System.Collections.Generic.List<double>());
            var prev = wing.ToDictionary(f => f, f => f.ShotsThisPass);
            double clock = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 780; i++)
            {
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                double step = GetProcessDeltaTime(); clock += step;
                pf.Position += new Vector2(12f, 0) * (float)step;
                foreach (var f in wing)
                {
                    if (f.ShotsThisPass > prev[f]) shots[f].Add(clock);
                    prev[f] = f.ShotsThisPass;
                }
            }
            double Gap(System.Collections.Generic.List<double> t) =>
                t.Count == 0 ? 99 : Math.Max(t.Zip(t.Skip(1), (a, b) => b - a).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max(), clock - t[^1]);
            var fired = wing.Where(f => shots[f].Count > 0).ToList();
            GD.Print($"  a light drifting at 12 u/s: {string.Join("; ", wing.Select(f => $"{shots[f].Count} shots, longest gap {Gap(shots[f]):0.0} s"))}");
            Check(onIt && fired.Count == wing.Count && fired.All(f => shots[f].Count >= 9 && Gap(shots[f]) < 4.0),
                  $"fighters on a small target drifting at 12 u/s keep making passes: every one fires again and again, no gap of 4 s ({string.Join(", ", wing.Select(f => $"{shots[f].Count}/{Gap(shots[f]):0.0}s"))})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(0.05);
            pf.Position = pfHome;
            // AND A TARGET THAT LEAVES: a hunter withdrawn when its escort ends is freed with hull left,
            // so it reads "alive" -- the wing must let go of it, not go on reading a freed node
            var gone = H.SpawnRaider(ship.Position + new Vector2(400, 0));
            gone.Quarry = H.Yard.Hauler;
            await Wait(0.1);
            Click(Scr(gone.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.3);
            bool attacked = ship.WingTarget == gone;
            // point defence on it too: a turret holding it must let go as well
            ship.Sl("pd").Left = 15.0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 90 && !ship.PdTurrets.Any(tu => tu.Target == gone); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            bool pdOn = ship.PdTurrets.Any(tu => tu.Target == gone);
            H.CallOff(H.Yard.Hauler); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(attacked && pdOn && ship.WingTarget == null && ship.PdTurrets.All(tu => tu.Target != gone),
                  $"a hunter called off while the fighters and point defence are on it: both let go of it ({attacked}, {pdOn}, {ship.WingTarget != null})");
            ship.Sl("pd").Left = 0.0;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5);
        }

        // ── bombers: an active ability; straight, steady torpedoes with smoke ──
        await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5);
        Click(Scr(d1.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        // beyond the strike range the order is refused
        ship.Position = d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 3200); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.3);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.StrikeTarget == null && AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line == "OUT OF RANGE",
              "a target beyond 2x control range: strike refused, slot says OUT OF RANGE");
        // inside the 3000 u strike range, outside the 1900 u launch range: the run in is real
        ship.Position = d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 2100); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.5);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.StrikeTarget == d1, "F sends the bombers at the target");
        // THEY TAKE OFF IN TURN, at the fighters' cadence: each rolls out onto the runway and up it to
        // the bow, growing from deck size to flight size -- lifting off, as the hauler does
        {
            var bws = Bombers();
            float liftMin = 9f, liftMax = 0f, bowY = 0f;
            for (int fr = 0; fr < 600 && bws.Any(w => w.LaunchedAt < 0 || w.BomberPhase == "Lifting"); fr++)
            {
                foreach (var w in bws.Where(w => w.BomberPhase == "Lifting"))
                {
                    liftMin = Mathf.Min(liftMin, w.VisualScale); liftMax = Mathf.Max(liftMax, w.VisualScale);
                    bowY = Mathf.Min(bowY, ship.ToLocal(w.Position).Y);
                }
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var lorder = bws.Select(w => w.LaunchedAt).Where(t => t >= 0).OrderBy(t => t).ToList();
            double lgap = 99; for (int g = 1; g < lorder.Count; g++) lgap = Math.Min(lgap, lorder[g] - lorder[g - 1]);
            Check(lorder.Count == 2 && lgap >= 0.83 - 1e-9, $"the bombers take off one at a time, at least 0.83 s apart, as the fighters launch ({lgap:0.000} s)");
            Check(liftMin < 0.66f && liftMax > 0.99f && bowY < -104f,
                  $"a bomber lifts off the deck: from 65% to full size, up the runway to its bow end, 110 u ahead ({liftMin:0.00} to {liftMax:0.00}; {-bowY:0} u ahead)");
        }
        Wing crawl = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 2400 && crawl == null; i++) { crawl = Nodes<Wing>().FirstOrDefault(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Launching); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(crawl != null && crawl.Velocity.Length() > 5f && crawl.Velocity.Dot(d1.Position - crawl.Position) > 0,
              $"bombers keep closing slowly while they launch ({crawl?.Velocity.Length():0} u/s)");
        float launchDist = crawl?.Position.DistanceTo(d1.Position) ?? -1;
        Check(launchDist > 1850 && launchDist <= 1901, $"the bombers close to 1900 u before they launch ({launchDist:0} u)");
        var fspr = Nodes<Sprite2D>(Nodes<Wing>().First(w => w.Carrier == ship && !w.Def.Parks)).First(); var bspr = Nodes<Sprite2D>(crawl).First();
        Check(Math.Abs(fspr.Texture.GetHeight() * fspr.Scale.Y - 17f) < 0.1f && Math.Abs(bspr.Texture.GetHeight() * bspr.Scale.Y - 28.125f) < 0.1f, "fighters 17 u, bombers 28.1 u (snout-nosed, 25% smaller)");
        Shot first = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && first == null; i++) { first = Missiles().FirstOrDefault(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(first != null, "bombers launch torpedoes");
        // measure over an exact frame count: timers round up to the next frame
        var dir0 = first.Dir; var q0 = first.GlobalPosition;
        for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        float spd = (first.GlobalPosition - q0).Length() / (30f / 60f);
        Check(first.Dir == dir0 && Math.Abs(spd - 300) < 2, $"torpedo runs straight at a steady 300 u/s (no tracking; {spd:0})");
        Check(Smoke(first).Count > 3, "torpedo leaves a smoke trail");
        // the landings begin inside the next 4 s: the deck is watched from here
        float landFar = 0f, landMin = 9f, landMax = 0f, taxiTurn = 0f; bool sawTaxi = false;
        void SampleDeck()
        {
            foreach (var w in Bombers())
            {
                if (w.BomberPhase == "Landing")
                { landFar = Mathf.Max(landFar, ship.ToLocal(w.Position).Length()); landMin = Mathf.Min(landMin, w.VisualScale); landMax = Mathf.Max(landMax, w.VisualScale); }
                if (w.BomberPhase == "Taxiing") { sawTaxi = true; taxiTurn = Mathf.Max(taxiTurn, Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(w.Rotation, ship.Rotation))); }
            }
        }
        for (int fr = 0; fr < 240; fr++) { SampleDeck(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(d1.Total >= 171.875 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["torpedo_damage"] - 171.875) < 1e-6,
              $"a torpedo lands for 171.875 (137.5 x1.25 by the chips), on top of the fighters' hits ({d1.Total:0})");
        Check(AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line.StartsWith("REARM") || AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line == "RETURNING",
              $"bomber slot shows the return / rearm ({AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line})");
        // HOME: each comes in over the carrier's centre and settles onto the runway there, shrinking
        // back to deck size and turning to face the bow, then taxis to its bay
        {
            for (int fr = 0; fr < 60 * 15; fr++) { SampleDeck(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(landMax > 0.95f && landMin < 0.7f && landFar <= 10.01f,
                  $"bombers land on the carrier's centre: set down within 10 u of it, shrinking from full size to deck size ({landFar:0.0} u; {landMax:0.00} to {landMin:0.00})");
            Check(sawTaxi && taxiTurn < 0.01f, "then taxi from the centre to their bays, facing the bow");
        }
        Check(ship.BombersReady == 2 && Bombers().All(w => w.Docked), "bombers return to their bays, rearm, and are ready again");
        // bombers land and re-arm even with the carrier at full speed: they must catch its centre,
        // and the landing and the taxi then run in its frame, on a clock
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && !Bombers().All(w => w.Ammo <= 0 || !w.Docked); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.W, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.W, Pressed = true });
        float yaw0 = ship.Rotation; bool allHome = false; float topSeen = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && !allHome; i++)
        {
            allHome = Bombers().All(w => w.Docked); topSeen = Mathf.Max(topSeen, ship.Velocity.Length());
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.W, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.W, Pressed = false });
        Check(allHome && topSeen > 90f && Bombers().All(w => w.RearmLeft > 0 || w.Armed),
              $"with the carrier at full speed ({topSeen:0} u/s), the bombers still land, park and re-arm");
        ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(15.0);
        // the balance's reference: what the carrier's sheet says (fighters + a strike's torpedoes); re-measured
        // here over 40 s -- lumpier (bomber strikes land in lumps), so within 25% -- to catch it drifting
        {
            var md = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            Click(Scr(md.Position)); await Wait(0.1);
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); md.ResetMeter();
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.05); Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 40; i++)
            {
                ship.Position = md.Position + new Vector2(0, 520); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                if (i % 30 == 0 && ship.BombersReady == (int)ship.Stats["bomber_count"] && ship.StrikeTarget == null) Key(global::Godot.Key.F);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            double carrierDps = md.Total / 40.0;
            // (unequipped the sheet gives fighters 3 x 2.5 / 0.35 s and 8 torpedoes of 137.5 a strike; the
            //  five default chips add 25%, so the expected figure with them is about 117 DPS)
            GD.Print($"  carrier measured: {carrierDps:0.00} DPS over 40 s (reference 117 with the chips)");
            Check(Math.Abs(carrierDps / 117.0 - 1) < 0.35, $"the carrier delivers about the 117 DPS its sheet says with the chips ({carrierDps:0.00})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(12.0);                       // recall the wing for what follows
        }

        // No tracking, tested on single torpedoes so bombers cannot re-aim: moving
        // the target during a whole strike is not a fair test, because a bomber still
        // lining up correctly aims at where the target now is.
        var home = d1.Position;
        var from = home + new Vector2(0, 500);
        await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5); d1.ResetMeter();
        Combat.LaunchTorpedo(from, Vector2.Up, 240f, 900f, 3.0);
        await Wait(3.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(d1.Total - 3) < 0.01, "control: a torpedo at a still target hits for 3");
        await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5); d1.ResetMeter();
        Combat.LaunchTorpedo(from, Vector2.Up, 240f, 900f, 3.0);
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        d1.Position = home + new Vector2(160, 0);                  // step aside just after launch
        await Wait(4.5);
        GD.Print($"  moved target took {d1.Total:0} damage");
        Check(d1.Total == 0, "a target that moves after launch is missed (the torpedo does not track)");
        d1.Position = home;

        // ── utility ships: hull, loss, and a rebuild for 10% of the category's investment ──
        {
            var Yd = H.Yard;
            var mn = Yd.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner);
            var sv = Yd.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager);
            Check(mn.MaxHull == 120 && sv.MaxHull == 120 && Yd.Hauler.MaxHull == 262.5, "miners and salvagers have 120 hull, the hauler 262.5 (150 +75%)");
            Yd.Credits = 100000; double inv0 = Yd.Invested("MINERS"); int lv0 = Yd.Level("miner_hold");
            double price = Economy.Cost(Economy.ById("miner_hold"), lv0);
            Check(Yd.TryBuy("miner_hold") && Math.Abs(Yd.Invested("MINERS") - inv0 - price) < 1e-6, $"buying an upgrade adds its price to its category's investment (+{price:0})");
            double cost = Yd.RebuildCost("MINERS");
            Check(cost > 0 && Math.Abs(cost - Math.Round(Yd.Invested("MINERS") * 0.10)) < 1e-6, $"a rebuild costs 10% of it ({cost:0} of {Yd.Invested("MINERS"):0})");
            mn.TakeDamage(119);
            Check(mn.State != Gatherer.St.Destroyed && Math.Abs(mn.Hull - 1) < 1e-6, "119 damage leaves a miner at 1 hull");
            mn.TakeDamage(5); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(mn.State == Gatherer.St.Destroyed && !mn.Visible && mn.Cargo == 0 && Math.Abs(mn.RebuildIn - 30) < 0.2 && Yd.ArmOf(mn) < 0 && Yd.QueueIndex(mn) < 0,
                  "destroyed: gone, its cargo lost, its arm and queue place given up, rebuilt in 30 s");
            Yd.Credits = cost - 1; mn.RebuildIn = 0.1; await Wait(0.3);
            Check(mn.State == Gatherer.St.Destroyed && mn.WaitingForCredits, "short of the rebuild cost, it waits");
            Yd.Credits = cost + 500; await Wait(0.2);
            Check(mn.State != Gatherer.St.Destroyed && mn.Hull == 120 && mn.Visible && Math.Abs(Yd.Credits - 500) < 1e-6, "paid for: rebuilt at the base at full hull, exactly 10% charged");
            // the hull upgrades: +10% a level, 125 cr to start (25% above the other +10% rows), x1.25 a level
            var mh = Economy.ById("miner_hull"); var sh2 = Economy.ById("salvager_hull");
            Check(mh != null && sh2 != null && mh.Tab == "MINERS" && sh2.Tab == "SALVAGERS"
                  && Economy.Cost(mh, 0) == 125 && Economy.Cost(mh, 1) == 156 && Economy.Cost(sh2, 0) == 125,
                  "Miner hull and Salvager hull: 125 cr to start, x1.25 a level (156 next)");
            var fullM = Yd.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner && g.State != Gatherer.St.Destroyed);
            fullM.Hull = fullM.MaxHull; Yd.Credits = 100000;
            Yd.TryBuy("miner_hull"); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(fullM.MaxHull == 132 && fullM.Hull == 132 && sv.MaxHull == 120, "one level: a miner's hull 132 (+10%, kept full); salvagers still 120");
            Yd.TryBuy("salvager_hull"); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(sv.MaxHull == 132, "and the salvagers' own upgrade: 132");
            // A ship that comes off the pad AFTER an upgrade -- a new hub, the trip home, one more
            // bought -- comes at the upgraded hull. It used to start at the base 120 whatever the
            // level, and fly under a damage bar for good.
            var fresh = new Gatherer { Yard = Yd, Kind = GatherKind.Miner, Index = 9, Name = "FreshMiner" };
            Yd.AddChild(fresh); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(fresh.Hull - 132) < 1e-6, $"a miner built after the upgrade comes off the pad at 132 hull ({fresh.Hull:0})");
            Yd.RemoveChild(fresh); fresh.QueueFree();
            double hcost = Yd.RebuildCost("HAULER");
            Yd.Hauler.ResetToPad(); Yd.Hauler.TakeDamage(262.4); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Yd.Hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && Math.Abs(Yd.Hauler.Hull - 0.1) < 1e-6, $"262.4 damage leaves the hauler standing ({Yd.Hauler.Hull:0.0} hull)");
            Yd.Hauler.TakeDamage(0.2); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Yd.Hauler.State == Hauler.St.Destroyed && Yd.Hauler.WarpedOut && Math.Abs(Yd.Hauler.RebuildIn - 30) < 0.2, "the hauler, destroyed: gone for 30 s");
            Yd.Hauler.RebuildIn = 0.1; Yd.Credits = hcost + 100; await Wait(0.3);
            Check(Yd.Hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && Yd.Hauler.Hull == 262.5 && Math.Abs(Yd.Credits - 100) < 1e-6,
                  $"rebuilt on its pad for 10% of the hauler's investment ({hcost:0} cr)");
        }

        // ── networking: silent offline; what friends elsewhere need; one press a second ──
        {
            var nf0 = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
            Check(Net.I.NetworkIdle, "single player: no socket, no web lookup, no router job running");
            var inet = Net.Describe("203.0.113.7", true, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var shared = Net.Describe("100.72.1.9", true, "", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var vpn = Net.Describe("198.51.100.4", true, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var manual = Net.Describe("", false, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var none = Net.Describe("", false, "", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            Check(inet.reach == Net.Reach.Internet && inet.address == "203.0.113.7:27015", "router opened the port and is the internet's edge: friends join public:port");
            Check(shared.reach == Net.Reach.LanOnly, "the router opened a port on a carrier-grade address: network only");
            Check(vpn.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && vpn.address == "198.51.100.4:27015" && vpn.message.Contains("VPN"),
                  $"the router's public address is not the one the internet sees: a VPN on this PC, named, with the router's address to try ({vpn.reach})");
            // Nothing opened, and the router's internet side is private: TWO forwards, both named.
            var twoHand = Net.Describe("192.168.4.40", false, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015", "the router refused (ActionFailed)");
            Check(twoHand.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && twoHand.message.Contains("to this PC (192.168.1.5)") && twoHand.message.Contains("UDP 27015 to 192.168.4.40"),
                  "two routers, neither opened: both forwards, each to its own address");
            Check(Net.IsPublic("81.2.3.4") && !Net.IsPublic("100.72.1.5") && !Net.IsPublic("0.0.0.0") && !Net.IsPublic("169.254.3.4")
                  && !Net.IsPublic("192.0.0.8") && !Net.IsPublic("192.168.4.40") && !Net.IsPublic("::1"),
                  "only a real internet address counts as public (a router reporting 0.0.0.0 is not an address to give friends)");
            // What a player pastes into JOIN.
            Check(Net.ParseAddress("1.2.3.4") == ("1.2.3.4", 27015) && Net.ParseAddress(" 1.2.3.4 : 27020 ") == ("1.2.3.4", 27020)
                  && Net.ParseAddress("http://1.2.3.4:27020/") == ("1.2.3.4", 27020) && Net.ParseAddress("[2001:db8::1]:27020") == ("2001:db8::1", 27020)
                  && Net.ParseAddress("2001:db8::1") == ("2001:db8::1", 27015) && Net.ParseAddress("game.example.com") == ("game.example.com", 27015)
                  && Net.ParseAddress("1.2.3.4:99999") == null && Net.ParseAddress("1.2.3.4:abc") == null && Net.ParseAddress("   ") == null,
                  "JOIN reads an address however it is pasted: a port or none, IPv6 bare or in brackets, a name, spaces, a scheme; and refuses an impossible port");
            Check(manual.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && manual.address == "203.0.113.7:27015" && manual.message.Contains("UDP 27015"),
                  "the router will not open it: the public address, and how to forward the port");

            // The branches that were WRONG answers until now, beside the ones that were right.
            // The first is what a player saw when their router HAD opened the port: Describe was
            // handed "mapped" inferred from whether the router named its outside address, so a
            // router that opened the port and said nothing else was reported as refusing, and the
            // player was sent to forward a port that was already forwarded.
            var opened = Net.Describe("", true, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015", "");
            Check(opened.reach == Net.Reach.Internet && opened.address == "203.0.113.7:27015",
                  $"port open but the router will not name its address: still hosting for the internet ({opened.reach})");
            var cgnat = Net.Describe("100.72.1.5", false, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015", "the router refused (ActionFailed)");
            Check(cgnat.reach == Net.Reach.LanOnly && !cgnat.message.Contains("UDP 27015"),
                  $"behind carrier-grade NAT: not sent to forward a port that cannot work ({cgnat.reach})");
            var refused = Net.Describe("", false, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015", "the router refused (ActionFailed)");
            Check(refused.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && refused.message.Contains("ActionFailed") && refused.message.Contains("firewall"),
                  "a refusal says WHAT the router said, and mentions the firewall");
            Check(none.reach == Net.Reach.LanOnly, "nothing learned: network only");
            Check(new[] { inet, shared, vpn, manual, none, twoHand, cgnat }.All(d => !d.message.Contains("203.0.113.7") && !d.message.Contains("198.51.100.4")),
                  "no status line ever prints the public address");
            var sm = Nodes<SessionMenu>().First();
            Unmeet("multiplayer");
            var toggle = Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == "NetToggle");
            bool beforeUnfold = Met("multiplayer");
            toggle.ButtonPressed = true; await Wait(0.05); toggle.ButtonPressed = false;
            Check(!beforeUnfold && Met("multiplayer"), "MULTIPLAYER: met when the panel is unfolded");
            var off = Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == "Offline");
            off.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(sm.Locked && new[] { "Host", "Join", "Offline", "Reconnect" }.All(n => Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == n).Disabled),
                  "one press a second: after a press, host, join, offline and reconnect wait");
            await Wait(SessionMenu.PressGap);
            Check(!sm.Locked && !Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == "Host").Disabled, "and are back after 1 s");
            Check(Net.I.NetworkIdle, "and offline stays silent");
            // offline, there is nothing to get back in to
            var rcBtn = Nodes<Button>(sm).FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Reconnect");
            Check(rcBtn != null && !rcBtn.Visible && !Net.I.CanReconnect && !Net.I.Reconnecting, "offline, there is no RECONNECT: nothing to get back in to");
            // A JOIN THAT NEVER CONNECTS, from offline: this pilot's world as it was -- the same ship,
            // not a fresh one at the spawn -- and nothing retried (it was never in)
            var ship0 = Me;
            Net.I.Join("no-such-host.invalid");
            await RealTime(() => !Net.I.Connecting, 15);
            Check(!Net.I.Connecting && !Net.IsOnline && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(ship0) && Me == ship0 && !Net.I.Reconnecting && !Net.I.CanReconnect,
                  "a JOIN that fails from offline leaves the pilot's own ship alone, and is not retried");
            // ...and one to an address that never answers (a literal: nothing to look up) is given up on in 12 s
            ulong jl0 = Time.GetTicksMsec();
            Net.I.Join("127.0.0.1:9");
            await RealTime(() => !Net.I.Connecting, 16);
            double joinGaveUp = (Time.GetTicksMsec() - jl0) / 1000.0;
            Check(!Net.I.Connecting && !Net.IsOnline && Me == ship0 && !Net.I.Reconnecting && !Net.I.CanReconnect && joinGaveUp > 11.8 && joinGaveUp < 12.6
                  && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("Could not reach"),
                  $"a JOIN to an address that never answers is given up on in 12 s, and not retried ({joinGaveUp:0.0} s; {Net.I.LastStatus})");
            // A LINK LOST BEFORE THE HOST LET THIS PILOT IN (Godot calls that "disconnected", not
            // "failed"): an attempt that did not happen -- not a session lost, so nothing is retried,
            // even with a host to go back to
            typeof(Net).GetProperty("LastHost").GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(Net.I, new object[] { "127.0.0.1:9" });
            Net.I.Join("127.0.0.1:9"); await Wait(0.2);
            bool midHandshake = Net.I.Connecting;
            Priv(Net.I, "OnHostGone"); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(midHandshake && !Net.I.Connecting && !Net.IsOnline && !Net.I.Reconnecting && !Net.I.CanReconnect && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("Could not get in"),
                  $"a link lost mid-handshake is a failed JOIN, not a dropped session: nothing retried ({Net.I.LastStatus})");
            typeof(Net).GetProperty("LastHost").GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(Net.I, new object[] { "" });
            // A RETURNING PILOT PUT BACK (what a host does with a held place): its hull, where it was,
            // or its stasis with the time it had left
            var backAt = Me.Position; float backRot = Me.Rotation;
            Me.Restore(100, true, 0); bool hullBack = Math.Abs(Me.Hp - 100) < 1e-6;
            Me.Place(new Vector2(-1500, 1500), 1.0f); bool placed = Me.Position == new Vector2(-1500, 1500) && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(Me.Rotation, 1f);
            Me.Restore(0, false, 50); bool heldStasis = !Me.Alive && Math.Abs(Me.StasisLeft - 50) < 0.1;
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_stasis", nf0).SetValue(Me, 0.0); Me.UseAbility("reboard", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(hullBack && placed && heldStasis && Me.Alive, $"a returning pilot is put back: its hull, its place, or its stasis with the time it had left ({hullBack}, {placed}, {heldStasis})");
            Me.Place(backAt, backRot); Me.Hp = Me.MaxHp;
            // review: a router answer for a session that is already over must never be kept --
            // its port mapping is closed at once (it used to be stored, leaking the forward)
            var nf = nf0;
            int genNow = (int)typeof(Net).GetField("_hostGen", nf).GetValue(Net.I);
            int closed0 = Net.I.StaleMappingsClosed;
            bool staleClosed = false;
            var stale = new Router.Report();
            stale.Opened.Add(new Router.Hop("UPnP", "test", 27015, () => staleClosed = true));
            typeof(Net).GetMethod("RouterResult", nf).Invoke(Net.I, new object[] { genNow - 1, stale });
            await RealTime(() => staleClosed && Net.I.NetworkIdle, 5);
            Check(((System.Collections.ICollection)typeof(Net).GetField("_hops", nf).GetValue(Net.I)).Count == 0 && Net.I.StaleMappingsClosed == closed0 + 1 && staleClosed,
                  "a router answer for a finished session is not kept, and the port it opened is closed");
            Check(Router.IsCarrierGrade("100.100.1.2") && !Router.IsCarrierGrade("100.63.0.1") && !Router.IsCarrierGrade("192.168.1.2"),
