                  "carrier-grade / Tailscale addresses (100.64.0.0/10) are recognised");
            await RouterScenarios();

            // ANOTHER PLAYER'S SHIP, as this peer draws it: owned by peer 2, so it only follows what
            // it is told. Three things went wrong over the internet. A warp's landing flash never
            // faded here (it only ever faded on the owner's own screen). With its packets stopped --
            // a friend's connection dying without a goodbye -- it coasted on in a straight line until
            // the timeout. And a held trigger went on firing all that time. A carrier, so the held
            // trigger fires nothing while it is tested.
            var ghost = new PlayerShip { Name = "Ghost" };
            H.AddChild(ghost);
            ghost.Init(2, Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2600, 2600));
            ghost.SetIdentity("Ghost", Colors.Gray, Colors.Gray, ShipClass.Carrier);
            var sf = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_netPos", sf).SetValue(ghost, ghost.Position + new Vector2(900, 0));   // a warp's jump away
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_netVel", sf).SetValue(ghost, new Vector2(100, 0));
            ghost.Trigger = true;
            await Wait(0.1);
            double Flash() => (double)typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_warpFlash", sf).GetValue(ghost);
            double flash0 = Flash(); var at0 = ghost.Position;
            await Wait(2.0);
            Check(!ghost.Mine && flash0 > 0 && Flash() == 0, $"another player's warp: the flash where it lands, and then it fades ({flash0:0.00} -> {Flash():0.00})");
            Check(ghost.Position.DistanceTo(at0) < 60f, $"its packets stopped, another player's ship coasts half a second, not on and on ({ghost.Position.DistanceTo(at0):0} u in 2 s)");
            Check(!ghost.Trigger, "and a trigger it was holding lets go");
            ghost.QueueFree();

            // A host-owned thing on a guest's screen (NetPose) is carried forward along its measured
            // velocity -- a quarter second at most -- instead of stopping dead at the last report,
            // where it was a round trip ago. 10 u in 0.1 s is 100 u/s, smoothed to 50 on a first
            // measurement: a second later it stands 50 x 0.25 = 12.5 u past the report, and no further.
            var pose = new NetPose(); var probe = new Node2D();
            pose.Set(Vector2.Zero, 0f); pose.Follow(probe, 0.1f);
            pose.Set(new Vector2(10, 0), 0f);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) pose.Follow(probe, 1f / 60);
            Check(probe.Position.X > 21f && probe.Position.X < 22.6f, $"a raider on a guest's screen runs a quarter second ahead of its last report, no more (x {probe.Position.X:0.0} of 22.5)");
            probe.Free();
        }

        // ── the stat sheet's arithmetic: gear leans hard, so a stack of downsides must shrink a
        //    number, never zero it, turn it negative or divide by zero ──
        {
            var rate = new Stat { Base = 1, Bonus = -1, Inverse = true };
            var dmg = new Stat { Base = 10, Bonus = -1.5 };
            Check(Math.Abs(rate.Value - 10) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(dmg.Value - 1) < 1e-9,
                  $"the stat multiplier never falls below 0.1: -100% rate on a 1 s interval is 10 s, -150% on 10 damage is 1 ({rate.Value}, {dmg.Value})");
            var sum = ShipStats.Sum(new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, double> { ["a"] = 1 },
                                    new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, double> { ["a"] = 2, ["b"] = 3 });
            var one = ShipStats.Sum(null, new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, double> { ["b"] = 1 });
            Check(sum.Count == 2 && sum["a"] == 3 && sum["b"] == 3 && one.Count == 1 && one["b"] == 1,
                  "ShipStats.Sum adds shares key by key ({a:1} + {a:2, b:3} = {a:3, b:3})");
        }

        // ── THE GEAR TABLES: 12 parts per slot type, 4 specialisations x 3 rarities ──
        {
            Check(Enum.GetNames(typeof(Rarity)).SequenceEqual(new[] { "Common", "Rare", "Epic" }), "three rarities: Common, Rare, Epic");
            var drops = Equipment.Drops.ToList();
            var bsC = ShipClass.Battleship; var cvC = ShipClass.Carrier; var ddC = ShipClass.Destroyer;
            // A PART ASKS WHAT THE HULL HAS, it does not name a hull. Every family is still four
            // lines at three rarities; what changed is who may wear them -- a battery needs
            // main_damage and main_interval, so the nine new classes can wear one too, where
            // before they could wear nothing but the parts that named no class at all.
            int Wear(ShipClass c, GearSlot s) => drops.Count(i => Equipment.Fits(i, s, c));
            bool famShape = new[] { GearSlot.Weapon, GearSlot.Utility, GearSlot.Engines, GearSlot.Shield, GearSlot.Hull, GearSlot.Chip }
                .All(s => new[] { Rarity.Common, Rarity.Rare, Rarity.Epic }
                    .All(r => drops.Count(i => i.Slot == s && i.Rarity == r) * 3 == drops.Count(i => i.Slot == s)));
            var kit = Equipment.All.Where(i => i.Kit).ToList();
            Check(drops.Count == 246 && famShape && kit.Count == 23 && kit.All(i => i.Rarity == Rarity.Common)
                  && kit.Count(i => i.Slot == GearSlot.Weapon) == 7 && kit.Count(i => i.Slot == GearSlot.Utility) == 12
                  && drops.Count(i => i.Slot == GearSlot.Utility) == 144,
                  $"the gear: 246 drops, a third of each slot at each rarity, and a 23-part kit -- seven weapon mounts and twelve signature systems across twelve classes, plus the drive, the deflector, the frame and the chip every ship has. The utility slot is 144 of the 246: twelve systems with a family of four, where nine of the twelve had none at all ({drops.Count} drops, {drops.Count(i => i.Slot == GearSlot.Utility)} utility, {kit.Count} kit)");
            Check(Wear(bsC, GearSlot.Weapon) == 24 && Wear(cvC, GearSlot.Weapon) == 12 && Wear(ddC, GearSlot.Weapon) == 24
                  && Wear(ShipClass.FreightHauler, GearSlot.Weapon) == 24 && Wear(ShipClass.LightDart, GearSlot.Weapon) == 24
                  // THE UTILITY SLOT: one family of four per SYSTEM at three rarities, and every
                  // class has exactly one system, so every class wears exactly twelve -- the
                  // bubble's, the overdrive's, the shockwave's, the railgun's, the rush's, the
                  // hunters', the roll's, the echo's and the veil's are the nine that are new.
                  && Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => Wear(c.Id, GearSlot.Utility) == 12)
                  && new[] { GearSlot.Engines, GearSlot.Shield, GearSlot.Chip }
                        .All(g => Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => Wear(c.Id, g) == 12))
                  // THE HULL SLOT, by what a hull actually is: the four point-defence frames (12
                  // parts) only where there is point defence, the three turret cradles (9) only on
                  // a hull that drops turrets, and the three hull-and-handling frames (9) on
                  // everything. So a sniper wears 9 where it used to wear 3, and a freighter 30.
                  && Wear(bsC, GearSlot.Hull) == 21 && Wear(ShipClass.HeavySniper, GearSlot.Hull) == 12
                  && Wear(ShipClass.FreightHauler, GearSlot.Hull) == 30 && Wear(ShipClass.LightWraith, GearSlot.Hull) == 12
                  && Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => Wear(c.Id, GearSlot.Hull)
                         == 12 + (c.Has(Fit.Pd) ? 9 : 0) + (c.Has(Fit.Deploy) ? 9 : 0)),
                  "a hull wears what it has the stats for: 24 weapon parts for anything with main guns -- a freighter and a dart included, which could wear none -- 12 for the carrier's hangars, 12 utility parts for every one of the twelve classes, every hull all 12 engines, shields and chips, and a hull slot that says what the hull IS: twelve hull-and-handling frames for everything (the armoured frame among them, which is about the hull as much as the window), nine that are point defence's alone, nine turret cradles where turrets are dropped -- a sniper 12 where it wore 3, a battleship 21, a freighter 30");
            bool OnlyFamily(ItemDef i, string fam) => i.Pct.Keys.Concat(i.Add.Keys).All(k => k.StartsWith(fam));
            Check(Equipment.All.Where(i => !i.Kit && OnlyFamily(i, "broadside_")).All(i => Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, bsC) && !Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, ddC))
                  && Equipment.All.Where(i => !i.Kit && OnlyFamily(i, "missile_")).All(i => Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, ddC) && !Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, bsC))
                  && Equipment.All.Where(i => !i.Kit && OnlyFamily(i, "fighter_")).All(i => Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, cvC) && !Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, bsC))
                  && Equipment.All.Count(i => !i.Kit && OnlyFamily(i, "broadside_")) == 12
                  && Equipment.All.Count(i => !i.Kit && OnlyFamily(i, "missile_")) == 12
                  && Equipment.All.Count(i => !i.Kit && OnlyFamily(i, "fighter_")) == 9,
                  "and a part whose every stat belongs to one system still fits only the hull that has it: twelve broadside parts on the battleship alone, twelve missile parts on the destroyer, nine pure fighter parts on the carrier");
            // EVERY CLASS, EVERY SLOT -- the Known broken item, as one assertion. Nine of the
            // twelve could put nothing in the utility slot but the part they were born with:
            // there was no rolled part in the game that fitted a bubble, an overdrive, a
            // shockwave, a railgun, a rush, a cell of hunters, a barrel roll, an echo or a veil.
            var slots = Equipment.Core.Concat(new[] { GearSlot.Chip }).ToArray();
            var bare = Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready)
                              .SelectMany(c => slots.Where(s => Wear(c.Id, s) == 0).Select(s => $"{c.Name}/{s}")).ToList();
            Check(bare.Count == 0 && slots.Length == 6 && Classes.All.Count(c => c.Ready) == 12
                  && Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => Wear(c.Id, GearSlot.Utility) == 12),
                  $"all twelve classes can fit a ROLLED part in every one of the six slots, the utility slot included -- twelve of them each ({bare.Count} empty slots: {string.Join(", ", bare)})");
            // A KIT PART FITS ONLY THE HULLS BORN WITH IT, by the SAME rule as every other part:
            // it asks for the signature row of those hulls (ItemDef.Needs). It used to take a
            // second table (Equipment.KitOwners) and a second branch inside Fits. The sets must
            // be EQUAL, class by class, not merely overlapping.
            var carries = Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).SelectMany(c => c.Kit.Select(k => (part: k.Id, cls: c.Id)))
                                 .ToLookup(x => x.part, x => x.cls);
            var wrongHull = kit.Where(i => carries.Contains(i.Id))
                               .SelectMany(i => Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready)
                                   .Where(c => Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, c.Id) != carries[i.Id].Contains(c.Id))
                                   .Select(c => $"{i.Id}/{c.Name}")).ToList();
            var anyHull = kit.Where(i => !carries.Contains(i.Id)).ToList();
            // and what a SAVED loadout does with one that no longer fits: the hull's own, as ever
            var saved = Equipment.Sanitize(ddC, new[] { "bs_main_battery", "std_drive", "basic_deflector", "reinforced_frame", "dd_missile_rack", "", "", "", "", "" });
            Check(wrongHull.Count == 0 && carries.Count == 19 && anyHull.Count == 4
                  && carries["freight_main_gun"].Count() == 3 && carries["light_main_gun"].Count() == 3 && carries["heavy_main_gun"].Count() == 2
                  && anyHull.All(i => Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, c.Id)))
                  && Equipment.Fits(Equipment.ById("freight_main_gun"), GearSlot.Weapon, ShipClass.FreightTender)
                  && !Equipment.Fits(Equipment.ById("freight_bubble"), GearSlot.Utility, ShipClass.FreightTender)
                  && !Equipment.Fits(Equipment.ById("bs_main_battery"), GearSlot.Weapon, ddC)
                  && saved[0] == "dd_main_battery" && saved[4] == "dd_missile_rack",
                  $"a kit part fits the hulls born with it and no others: nineteen named by a class row -- one cargo gun on the three freighters, one light cannon on the sniper and the warden, one dart cannon on the three lights -- and the drive, the deflector, the frame and the chip on any hull; a battleship's mount saved on a destroyer comes back as the destroyer's own ({wrongHull.Count} wrong: {string.Join(", ", wrongHull)})");
            // a sheet with only the named parts on the kit, chips off
            ShipStats Sheet(ShipClass c, string part)
            {
                var l = Equipment.Default(c); for (int k = Equipment.CoreSlots; k < Equipment.Slots; k++) l[k] = "";
                var it = Equipment.ById(part);
                if (it.Slot == GearSlot.Chip) l[Equipment.CoreSlots] = part; else l[Array.IndexOf(Equipment.Core, it.Slot)] = part;
                return new ShipStats(c, Equipment.Bonuses(c, l), Equipment.Adds(c, l));
            }
            bool Near(double a, double b) => Math.Abs(a - b) < 1e-6;
            var e1 = Sheet(cvC, "cv_elite_1"); var e2 = Sheet(cvC, "cv_elite_2");
            var h1 = Sheet(bsC, "bs_heavy_1"); var h2 = Sheet(bsC, "bs_heavy_2"); var h3 = Sheet(bsC, "bs_heavy_3");
            Check(Near(e1["fighter_speed"], 704) && Near(e1["fighter_damage"], 5.0) && Near(e2["fighter_speed"], 880) && Near(e2["fighter_damage"], 6.25)
                  && Near(h1["main_damage"], 32.22) && Near(h2["main_damage"], 39.38) && Near(h3["main_damage"], 46.54)
                  && new[] { h1, h2, h3 }.All(h => Near(h["main_interval"], 2 / 0.65)),
                  "a rarer copy leans harder, never costs more: Elite I / II speed 704 / 880; Heavy I / II / III shells 10.62 / 12.98 / 15.34, all reloading in 1.54 s");
            var barrage = Sheet(bsC, "bs_barrage_3"); var snap = Sheet(bsC, "bs_snap_2"); var heavyS = Sheet(bsC, "bs_heavyside_3"); var rapidS = Sheet(bsC, "bs_rapidside_1");
            Check(Near(barrage["broadside_volleys"], 6) && Near(barrage["broadside_windup"], 0.5 / 0.7) && Near(barrage["broadside_cooldown"], 17.5)
                  && Near(snap["broadside_volleys"], 2) && Near(snap["broadside_windup"], 0.2)
                  && Near(heavyS["broadside_mult"], 1.8) && Near(heavyS["broadside_cooldown"], 14 / 0.75)
                  && Near(rapidS["broadside_mult"], 0.75) && Near(rapidS["broadside_cooldown"], 14 / 1.5),
                  "the broadside's gear: Barrage III 6 volleys (0.71 s wind-up, 17.5 s cooldown); Snap II 2 volleys in a 0.2 s wind-up; Heavy III x1.8 shells (18.7 s); Rapid I back in 9.3 s at x0.75");
            var ddHeavy = Sheet(ddC, "dd_heavy_3"); var ddSalvo = Sheet(ddC, "dd_salvo_3");
            Check(Near(ddHeavy["main_damage"], 19.5) && Near(ddHeavy["main_count"], 2) && Near(ddSalvo["missile_mag"], 6) && Near(ddSalvo["missile_reload"], 12.0),
                  "the destroyer's own gear: Heavy Twins III shells 19.5 on its two guns; Salvo Rack III 6 bursts on a 12.0 s reload");
            var elite = Sheet(cvC, "cv_elite_3"); var swarm = Sheet(cvC, "cv_swarm_3"); var line = Sheet(cvC, "cv_line_3");
            Check(Near(elite["fighter_count"], 2) && Near(elite["fighter_speed"], 1056) && Near(elite["fighter_damage"], 7.5) && Near(elite["fighter_turn"], 5.25),
                  $"Elite Hangars III, the owner's example: 2 fighters, +200% speed and damage, +50% turning ({elite["fighter_count"]}, {elite["fighter_speed"]}, {elite["fighter_damage"]}, {elite["fighter_turn"]})");
            Check(Near(swarm["fighter_count"], 9) && Near(swarm["fighter_speed"], 274.56) && Near(swarm["fighter_damage"], 1.95),
                  $"Swarm Hangars III, the owner's example: 9 fighters, 22% slower and weaker ({swarm["fighter_count"]}, {swarm["fighter_speed"]}, {swarm["fighter_damage"]})");
            Check(Near(line["fighter_count"], 4) && Near(line["fighter_damage"], 3.5) && Near(line["fighter_speed"], 422.4),
                  $"Line Hangars III, between them: 4 fighters, +40% damage, +20% speed ({line["fighter_count"]}, {line["fighter_damage"]}, {line["fighter_speed"]})");
            // THE NINE NEW FAMILIES, in their own numbers: the lean scaled by rarity (x1, x1.5, x2),
            // the price flat, and every stat in a line belonging to that one system -- which is
            // what keeps a bubble part on the freighter that has a bubble and nowhere else.
            var bub3 = Sheet(ShipClass.FreightHauler, "bub_deep_3"); var bubS = Sheet(ShipClass.FreightHauler, "bub_snap_2");
            Check(Near(bub3["bubble_pool"], 800) && Near(bub3["bubble_radius"], 208)
                  && Near(bubS["bubble_cooldown"], 25 / 1.75) && Near(bubS["bubble_time"], 6) && Near(bubS["bubble_pool"], 340),
                  $"the bubble's gear: Deep III soaks 800 inside 208 u; Snap II is up again in 14.3 s, 340 over 6.0 s ({bub3["bubble_pool"]:0}, {bub3["bubble_radius"]:0}, {bubS["bubble_cooldown"]:0.0})");
            var hnt3 = Sheet(ShipClass.HeavyWarden, "hnt_heavy_3"); var hntS = Sheet(ShipClass.HeavyWarden, "hnt_swarm_3");
            Check(Near(hnt3["hunter_damage"], 99) && Near(hnt3["hunter_count"], 5)
                  && Near(hntS["hunter_count"], 9) && Near(hntS["hunter_damage"], 33.75),
                  $"the hunters' gear: Heavy Cells III is 5 missiles at 99; Swarm Cells III is 9 at 33.75 ({hnt3["hunter_count"]:0} x {hnt3["hunter_damage"]:0}, {hntS["hunter_count"]:0} x {hntS["hunter_damage"]:0.00})");
            var rlg3 = Sheet(ShipClass.HeavySniper, "rlg_quick_3"); var ovr3 = Sheet(ShipClass.FreightTender, "ovr_hard_3");
            Check(Near(rlg3["rail_charge"], 3 / 2.2) && Near(rlg3["rail_damage"], 112.5)
                  && Near(ovr3["overdrive_mult"], 3.2) && Near(ovr3["overdrive_time"], 5.6),
                  $"the railgun's and the overdrive's: Quick Charge III is a 1.36 s charge for a 112.5 slug; Hard Coils III is x3.2 for 5.6 s ({rlg3["rail_charge"]:0.00} s, {ovr3["overdrive_mult"]:0.0}x)");
            var vel3 = Sheet(ShipClass.LightWraith, "vel_deep_3"); var velA = Sheet(ShipClass.LightWraith, "vel_ambush_2");
            var bareVeil = new ShipStats(ShipClass.LightWraith);
            Check(Near(vel3["stealth_time"], 10) && Near(vel3["stealth_cooldown"], 20 / 0.75)
                  && Near(velA["stealth_rof"], 1.45) && Near(velA["stealth_speed"], 0.85)
                  && Near(bareVeil["stealth_speed"], 1) && Near(bareVeil["stealth_rof"], 1),
                  $"the veil's gear, on the two rows the veil gained so it could have a family at all: Deep III is unseen 10.0 s and back in 26.7 s; Ambush II fires at x1.45 and moves at x0.85 while unseen -- and a wraith with no veil part is x1.00 at both, the ship it always was ({vel3["stealth_time"]:0.0} s, {vel3["stealth_cooldown"]:0.0} s, {velA["stealth_rof"]:0.00}x, {bareVeil["stealth_speed"]:0.00}x)");
            // THE WORST CASE: the most negative share any fitting part gives each stat, in every core
            // slot at once and five times over in the chips, must leave the number above zero
            bool allPositive = true;
            double Worst(ShipClass c, string stat)
            {
                double w = 0;
                foreach (var slot in Equipment.Core)
                    w += Math.Min(0, Equipment.All.Where(i => Equipment.Fits(i, slot, c)).Select(i => i.Pct.GetValueOrDefault(stat)).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Min());
                return w + Equipment.ChipSlots * Math.Min(0, Equipment.All.Where(i => i.Slot == GearSlot.Chip).Select(i => i.Pct.GetValueOrDefault(stat)).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Min());
            }
            foreach (var c in Enum.GetValues<ShipClass>()) foreach (var st in new ShipStats(c).All) if (Worst(c, st.Id) <= -1) allPositive = false;
            Check(allPositive && Near(Worst(cvC, "fighter_damage"), -0.47) && Near(Worst(bsC, "main_damage"), -0.55) && Near(Worst(bsC, "hull"), -0.70) && Near(Worst(cvC, "hull"), -0.70),
                  $"no stack of gear takes a number to zero: worst fighter damage -47% (1.325), shell -55% (8.055), hull -70% since the spar frame joined the deepest stack (112.5 / 75) ({Worst(cvC, "fighter_damage"):0.00}, {Worst(bsC, "main_damage"):0.00}, {Worst(bsC, "hull"):0.00})");
            // every stat a part names is real on a class it fits, and none touches the turret counts
            string[] addable = { "fighter_count", "bomber_count", "missile_mag", "bomber_ammo", "broadside_volleys", "hunter_count" };
            var unknown = Equipment.All.SelectMany(i => i.Pct.Keys.Concat(i.Add.Keys).Where(k =>
                              !Enum.GetValues<ShipClass>().Any(c => new ShipStats(c).All.Any(s => s.Id == k)))
                              .Select(k => i.Id + ":" + k)).ToList();
            bool noCounts = Equipment.All.All(i => !i.Pct.Keys.Concat(i.Add.Keys).Any(k => k is "main_count" or "pd_count" or "strike_range"));
            bool addsOk = Equipment.All.All(i => i.Add.Keys.All(addable.Contains));
            Check(unknown.Count == 0 && noCounts && addsOk,
                  $"every stat a part names exists on a class it fits; no part changes a turret count; whole additions only to wings, magazines and volleys ({string.Join(",", unknown)})");
            // a part for the other class changes nothing, even named in the loadout
            var wrong = Equipment.Default(bsC); wrong[0] = "cv_elite_3";
            var kitB = Equipment.Bonuses(bsC, Equipment.Default(bsC)); var wrongB = Equipment.Bonuses(bsC, wrong);
            Check(wrongB.Count == kitB.Count && wrongB.All(kv => kitB.TryGetValue(kv.Key, out var v) && Math.Abs(v - kv.Value) < 1e-12) && Equipment.Adds(bsC, wrong).Count == 0,
                  "a carrier's hangar named on a battleship changes nothing on it: the sheet is the kit's");
            var words = Equipment.Describe(Equipment.ById("chip_basic"), cvC).ToList();
            Check(words.Any(w => w.StartsWith("Fighters")) && words.Any(w => w.StartsWith("Hull")) && !words.Any(w => w.StartsWith("Main guns")),
                  $"a part's effects are named for the ship it is on ({string.Join(" | ", words)})");
        }

        // ── LOOT's rules: crates by boss level, rarity unlocking every 5 levels, 70% own class ──
        {
            Check(new[] { 1, 5, 6, 10, 11, 20 }.Select(Loot.CratesFor).SequenceEqual(new[] { 2, 2, 3, 3, 4, 4 }),
                  "crates per pilot: 2, then 3 from boss level 6, 4 from level 11");
            (int c, int r, int e) Bands(int level)
            {
                var rng = new Random(12345); int c = 0, r = 0, e = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < 1000; i++)
                    foreach (var id in Loot.RollParty(new[] { (1, ShipClass.Battleship) }, level, rng)[1])
                        switch (Equipment.ById(id).Rarity) { case Rarity.Common: c++; break; case Rarity.Rare: r++; break; default: e++; break; }
                return (c, r, e);
            }
            var b5 = Bands(5); var b6 = Bands(6); var b10 = Bands(10); var b11 = Bands(11);
            double Share(int n, (int c, int r, int e) b) => n / (double)(b.c + b.r + b.e);
            Check(b5.r == 0 && b5.e == 0 && Math.Abs(Share(b6.r, b6) - 0.30) < 0.03 && b6.e == 0 && b10.e == 0 && Math.Abs(Share(b10.r, b10) - 0.30) < 0.03
                  && Math.Abs(Share(b11.c, b11) - 0.55) < 0.03 && Math.Abs(Share(b11.r, b11) - 0.30) < 0.03 && Math.Abs(Share(b11.e, b11) - 0.15) < 0.03,
                  $"rarity unlocks every 5 boss levels: L5 all Common; L6 30% Rare; L11 55 / 30 / 15 ({Share(b6.r, b6):0.00}; {Share(b11.c, b11):0.00} / {Share(b11.r, b11):0.00} / {Share(b11.e, b11):0.00})");
            // both pilots measured: each is the other's mirror, so a roll made for the wrong class shows on one of them
            var prng = new Random(777); int kit = 0;
            var share = new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<int, (int n, int bs, int cv)> { [1] = (0, 0, 0), [7] = (0, 0, 0) };
            for (int i = 0; i < 2000; i++)
            {
                var roll = Loot.RollParty(new[] { (1, ShipClass.Battleship), (7, ShipClass.Carrier) }, 1, prng);
                foreach (var peer in new[] { 1, 7 })
                    foreach (var id in roll[peer])
                    {
                        var it = Equipment.ById(id); var (n, b, c) = share[peer];
                        // "a battleship part" is one a battleship can wear and a carrier cannot, and
                        // the other way about: a part fits by what the hull has now, not by a name.
                        bool bsOnly = Equipment.Fits(it, it.Slot, ShipClass.Battleship) && !Equipment.Fits(it, it.Slot, ShipClass.Carrier);
                        bool cvOnly = Equipment.Fits(it, it.Slot, ShipClass.Carrier) && !Equipment.Fits(it, it.Slot, ShipClass.Battleship);
                        share[peer] = (n + 1, b + (bsOnly ? 1 : 0), c + (cvOnly ? 1 : 0));
                        if (it.Kit) kit++;
                    }
            }
            double Of(int peer, int k) => k / (double)share[peer].n;
            // THE RULE, not the pool: seven rolls in ten are drawn from what this pilot's own hull
            // can wear, three from everything. So a pilot sees parts only its own hull can wear at
            // 0.7 x (its own share of its pool) + 0.3 x (their share of all), and parts only the
            // other hull can wear at 0.3 x (their share of all). The pools come from the same
            // Fits the roll uses, so this cannot drift when a family is added.
            var pool = Equipment.Drops.ToList();
            int all = pool.Count;
            double MineOf(ShipClass mine, ShipClass theirs)
            {
                int fitsMine = pool.Count(i => Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, mine));
                int onlyMine = pool.Count(i => Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, mine) && !Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, theirs));
                return 0.7 * onlyMine / fitsMine + 0.3 * onlyMine / (double)all;
            }
            double TheirsOf(ShipClass mine, ShipClass theirs)
            {
                int onlyTheirs = pool.Count(i => Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, theirs) && !Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, mine));
                return 0.3 * onlyTheirs / all;
            }
            Check(kit == 0
                  && Math.Abs(Of(1, share[1].bs) - MineOf(ShipClass.Battleship, ShipClass.Carrier)) < 0.03
                  && Math.Abs(Of(1, share[1].cv) - TheirsOf(ShipClass.Battleship, ShipClass.Carrier)) < 0.02
                  && Math.Abs(Of(7, share[7].cv) - MineOf(ShipClass.Carrier, ShipClass.Battleship)) < 0.03
                  && Math.Abs(Of(7, share[7].bs) - TheirsOf(ShipClass.Carrier, ShipClass.Battleship)) < 0.02,
                  $"each pilot's own roll: seven in ten from what its own hull can wear, three from everything -- the battleship pilot sees {Of(1, share[1].bs):0.000} of its own against {MineOf(ShipClass.Battleship, ShipClass.Carrier):0.000} expected, and {Of(1, share[1].cv):0.000} of the carrier's against {TheirsOf(ShipClass.Battleship, ShipClass.Carrier):0.000}");
        }

        // ── equipment (I): per-class gear; common defaults and five chips (+5% damage, +5% hull each) ──
        {
            Check(Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Carrier)[0] == "cv_fighter_hangars" && Equipment.ById("cv_fighter_hangars").Name == "Mk I Fighter Hangars"
                  && Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Battleship).Take(5).All(id => Equipment.ById(id).Rarity == Rarity.Common)
                  // EVERY class comes out of the yard with its own two mounts, not the battleship's
                  && Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => Equipment.Default(c.Id).Take(5).All(id =>
                         Equipment.ById(id) is { } part && Equipment.Fits(part, part.Slot, c.Id)))
                  && Equipment.Default(ShipClass.HeavySniper)[0] == "heavy_main_gun"
                  && Equipment.Default(ShipClass.HeavySniper)[4] == "heavy_railgun"
                  && Equipment.Default(ShipClass.LightWraith)[4] == "light_stealth_veil",
                  "the defaults: common parts in five slots, and every one of the twelve classes starts with mounts it can actually wear -- a carrier's Fighter Hangars, a sniper's Railgun Mount, a wraith's Stealth Veil");
            var bsE = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Battleship, Equipment.Bonuses(ShipClass.Battleship, Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Battleship)));
            var cvE = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier, Equipment.Bonuses(ShipClass.Carrier, Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Carrier)));
            Check(Math.Abs(bsE["hull"] - 375) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(bsE["main_damage"] - 22.375) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(cvE["hull"] - 250) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(cvE["fighter_damage"] - 3.125) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(cvE["torpedo_damage"] - 171.875) < 1e-6,
                  "five default chips: +25% damage and hull (battleship 375 hull, 22.375 a shell; carrier 250 hull, fighters 3.125, torpedoes 171.875)");
            var cls0 = Me.Class;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2);
            var ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(ew != null && Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Slot_")) == 5
                  && Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Chip_")) == 5, "I opens the equipment: five parts and five chips");
            double full = Me.MaxHp;
            Nodes<Button>(ew).First(b => b.Name == "Unequip").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Math.Abs(Me.MaxHp - full * 1.20 / 1.25) < 1e-6 && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("chip_basic") == 1, $"a chip off: 5% less hull ({full:0} -> {Me.MaxHp:0}), the chip in the hold");
            var other = cls0 == ShipClass.Battleship ? ShipClass.Carrier : ShipClass.Battleship;
            Me.SetClass(other); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(other).Skip(5).Count(x => x == "chip_basic") == 5, "the other class keeps its own gear: five chips");
            Me.SetClass(cls0); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(cls0).Skip(5).Count(x => x == "chip_basic") == 4 && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("chip_basic") == 1 && Math.Abs(Me.MaxHp - full * 1.20 / 1.25) < 1e-6,
                  "back on the first class: its gear as it was left (four chips, one in the hold)");
            ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            if (ew == null) { Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2); ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().First(); }
            Nodes<Button>(ew).First(b => b.Name == "Equip").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Math.Abs(Me.MaxHp - full) < 1e-6 && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("chip_basic") == 0, "the chip back on from the hold: full hull again");
            // THE HOLD IS THE PILOT'S: a chip taken off one class goes on the other. The pilot really
            // changes class (Character.Class, as the refit does), and the window is opened afresh.
            Nodes<Button>(ew).First(b => b.Name == "Unequip").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.1);
            await SetClass(other);
            Character.LoadoutFor(other)[Equipment.Slots - 1] = "";
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2);
            var ew2 = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            var holdRow = ew2 == null ? null : Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew2).FirstOrDefault(r => r.Name == "Hold_chip_basic");
            holdRow?.GetChildren().OfType<Button>().FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Equip")?.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
            await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(other).Skip(Equipment.CoreSlots).Count(x => x == "chip_basic") == 5 && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("chip_basic") == 0,
                  $"the hold is the pilot's: a chip taken off the {cls0} goes on the {other} ({Character.LoadoutFor(other).Skip(Equipment.CoreSlots).Count(x => x == "chip_basic")} chips)");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.1);
            await SetClass(cls0);
            Character.LoadoutFor(cls0)[Array.IndexOf(Character.LoadoutFor(cls0), "", Equipment.CoreSlots)] = "chip_basic";
            H.PilotChanged(); Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2);
            ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().First();
            Character.Save(); var saved = (string[])Character.LoadoutFor(cls0).Clone();
            string eqId = Character.Id; Character.Loadout.Clear(); Character.Load(eqId);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(cls0).SequenceEqual(saved), "the gear is saved with the pilot");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().Count() == 0, "Esc closes it");

            // A DROP IN THE HOLD: a core part fits its slot and the part it replaces goes into the
            // hold; a part for the other class is listed but cannot be fitted here
            await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier);
            Character.GearHold["cv_line_1"] = 1; Character.GearHold["bs_rapid_1"] = 1;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2);
            var ew3 = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().First();
            var otherRow = Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew3).FirstOrDefault(r => r.Name == "Hold_bs_rapid_1");
            Check(otherRow != null && !Nodes<Button>(otherRow).Any(), "a battleship part in the carrier's hold is listed, with nothing to fit it by");
            // ...and it says what it wants in words a player has seen: the SYSTEM, any ONE of them.
            // It printed the raw stat ids it moves, joined by commas -- "needs fighter_speed,
            // fighter_damage, fighter_turn, fighter_count" -- which reads as four demands.
            Check(otherRow != null && Nodes<Label>(otherRow).Any(l => l.Text.Contains("needs Main guns"))
                  && !Nodes<Label>(otherRow).Any(l => l.Text.Contains("main_interval"))
                  && Equipment.NeedsSaid(Equipment.ById("cv_elite_1")) == "CARRIER"
                  && Equipment.NeedsSaid(Equipment.ById("sh_balanced_1")) == "Hull or Point defence"
                  && Equipment.NeedsSaid(Equipment.ById("bs_main_battery")) == "BATTLESHIP",
                  $"a part that does not fit names the system it wants, any one of them: a Rapid Battery needs Main guns and a Balanced Array Hull or Point defence, while a part only one hull in the game can wear names the HULL instead -- Elite Hangars and the battleship's own battery -- because one name reads better than the system it belongs to ({Equipment.NeedsSaid(Equipment.ById("sh_balanced_1"))}, {Equipment.NeedsSaid(Equipment.ById("bs_main_battery"))})");
            var lineRow = Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew3).FirstOrDefault(r => r.Name == "Hold_cv_line_1");
            lineRow?.GetChildren().OfType<Button>().FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Fit")?.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
            await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Carrier)[0] == "cv_line_1" && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("cv_fighter_hangars") == 1
                  && !Character.GearHold.ContainsKey("cv_line_1") && Me.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter) == 4,
                  $"a hangar from the hold fits the weapon slot, the kit's goes into the hold, and the carrier flies 4 fighters ({Me.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter)})");
            // THE K WINDOW shows what gear adds: a Swarm III's six extra fighters, and its downside in red
            Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Carrier)[0] = "cv_swarm_3"; H.PilotChanged();
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.1);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.2);
            var kw = Nodes<StatsWindow>().First(); kw.ShowPane(false); await Wait(0.2);
            var grid = (GridContainer)typeof(StatsWindow).GetField("_grid", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(kw);
            var cells = grid.GetChildren().OfType<Label>().ToList();
            int fh = cells.FindIndex(c => c.Text == "FIGHTERS");
            int craftRow = fh < 0 ? -1 : cells.FindIndex(fh, c => c.Text == "  Craft");
            int speed = fh < 0 ? -1 : cells.FindIndex(fh, c => c.Text == "  Top speed");
            var bonusCell = speed < 0 ? null : cells[speed + 3];
            bool redDown = bonusCell != null && bonusCell.Text == "−22%" && bonusCell.GetThemeColor("font_color") == Ui.Bad;
            bool added = craftRow >= 0 && cells[craftRow + 1].Text == "3" && cells[craftRow + 2].Text == "+6" && cells[craftRow + 4].Text == "9";
            bool rule = Nodes<Label>(kw).Any(l => l.Text.Contains("(base + added)"));
            Check(grid.Columns == 5 && added && redDown && rule,
                  $"K shows base, added, bonus and final: Swarm III's craft 3 +6 = 9, its top speed -22% in red (added {added}, red {redDown}, rule {rule})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
            var cl0 = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Carrier); cl0[0] = "cv_fighter_hangars";
            Character.GearHold.Remove("cv_fighter_hangars"); Character.GearHold.Remove("bs_rapid_1");
            H.PilotChanged(); await SetClass(cls0);
        }

        // ── pilot progression: EXP, levels, points, flat upgrades ──
        {
            Check(Progression.Cost(0) == 1 && Progression.Cost(1) == 2 && Progression.Cost(2) == 3, "upgrade costs 1, 2, 3, ... points");
            Check(Progression.ExpToNext == 1000 && Progression.ExpToNext == 1000, "every level needs 1000 EXP");
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; System.Array.Clear(Character.Bought);
            Unmeet("pilot");
            int up = Progression.AddExp(2600);
            Check(Met("pilot"), "A LEVEL UP: met on the first level gained");
            Check(up == 2 && Character.Level == 3 && Character.Exp == 600 && Character.Points == 2, "2600 EXP from level 1: level 3, 600 over, 2 points");
            // the approved formula, in literal numbers (so the checks can fail)
            // THE ONE PLACE THE LEVEL'S SCALE IS PROVED AGAINST A LITERAL. 2.5% a level, not the
            // 10% it was: measured against the owner's target of a pilot on par after four boss
            // kills at a level with gear at about 65% of the salvage ladder, a tenth a level meant
            // the boss grew forty-fold over forty levels against points worth +45%. Every other
            // check reads Missions.S rather than repeating a factor that can move again.
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.S(1) - 1) < 1e-12 && Math.Abs(Missions.S(5) - Math.Pow(1.025, 4)) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(Missions.LevelStep - 1.025) < 1e-12 && Math.Abs(Missions.S(41) - 2.685) < 0.001,
                  $"boss levels start at 1 and climb 2.5% each: S(5) = {Missions.S(5):0.0000}, and forty levels is x{Missions.S(41):0.00} where a tenth a level was x45");
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.HullMult(1, 3) - 2.2) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.DamageMult(1, 3) - 1.4) < 1e-9, "a party of 3: boss hull x2.2, damage x1.4");
            // 300 at the base now, and NOTHING at all for a boss under half this pilot's level:
            // a level-5 pilot is taught nothing by a level 1 or 2 boss, however many it kills.
            Check(Missions.KillExpFor(5, 10) == 150 && Missions.KillExpFor(2, 1) == 600 && Missions.KillExpFor(1, 1) == 300
                  && Missions.KillExpFor(2, 5) == 0 && Missions.KillExpFor(1, 5) == 0 && Missions.KillExpFor(3, 5) == 180
                  && Missions.WorthExp(3, 5) && !Missions.WorthExp(2, 5),
                  $"kill EXP 300 x boss level / pilot level (5 vs 10: {Missions.KillExpFor(5, 10)}; 2 vs 1: {Missions.KillExpFor(2, 1)}), and nothing at all under half the pilot's level (level 5 against a level 2 boss: {Missions.KillExpFor(2, 5)})");
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.BountyEach(Missions.Bounty, 1, 1) - 2000) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.BountyEach(Missions.Bounty, 1, 2) - 1500) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(Missions.BountyEach(Missions.Bounty, 1, 3) - 4000.0 / 3) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(Missions.BountyEach(Missions.Siege, 1, 1) - 2000) < 1e-9,
                  "the bounty, +50% per extra pilot, split: 2000 solo, 1500 each for two, 1333 each for three -- and a raid pays off the same base figure");
            Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1;
            int k1 = Progression.AwardClear(Missions.Bounty, 1), k2 = Progression.AwardClear(Missions.Bounty, 1);
            Check(k1 == 650 && k2 == 400, $"a level-1 boss: 300 + 250 first clear + 100 = 650; again: 400 ({k1}, {k2})");
            // ONE LADDER: a level counts as cleared whichever known boss held it -- the first-clear bonus is
            // paid once a level, and the next level opens; a clear under an id this build does not know
            // counts for nothing
            Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1;
            Character.BossCleared["ironhold"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1, 2 };
            bool unknownIgnored = !Missions.Cleared(Missions.Bounty, 1) && Missions.HighestBeaten(Missions.Bounty) == 0 && Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Bounty) == 1;
            Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1 };
            int again = Progression.AwardClear(Missions.Bounty, 1);
            Check(unknownIgnored && Missions.Cleared(Missions.Bounty, 1) && !Missions.Cleared(Missions.Bounty, 2) && Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Bounty) == 2 && again == 400
                  && Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"].SetEquals(new[] { 1 }) && Missions.ForLevel(1).Id == "silver_lancer",
                  $"ONE BOSS LADDER: a level cleared under a known boss counts, and pays no second first-clear bonus ({again}); an unknown boss's clears count for nothing");
            // ...WHICHEVER BOSS HELD IT: the bosses take the levels in turn, and a level cleared under the
            // other one counts all the same -- recorded under the Drake, level 1 is no first clear for the Lancer
            Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1;
            Character.BossCleared["drake_bastion"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1 };
            int crossed = Progression.AwardClear(Missions.Bounty, 1);
            Check(Missions.ForLevel(1).Id == "silver_lancer" && Missions.ForLevel(2).Id == "drake_bastion" && Missions.ForLevel(3).Id == "silver_lancer"
                  && crossed == 400 && Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"].Contains(1),
                  $"the bosses take the levels in turn (Lancer, Drake, Lancer), and a level cleared under either is cleared: no second first-clear bonus ({crossed})");
            Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 3;
            Check(!Nodes<QuarryBar>().First().Visible, "outside the arena there is no quarry bar");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.1);
            var pw = Nodes<PilotWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(pw != null, "L opens the PILOT window");
            Check(pw != null && Nodes<Label>(pw).Any(l => l.Text.Contains("300 x the boss's level") && l.Text.Contains("+250 the first time")
                                                       && l.Text.Contains("+100 for completing")),
                  "its EXP note reads the mission table's own 300 / 250 / 100 rather than restating them beside it");
            Button PBuy(string id) => Nodes<Button>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(pw).First(r => r.Name == "Pilot_" + id)).First(b => b.Name == "Buy");
            var me = Me; me.Hp = me.MaxHp; double hull0 = me.MaxHp, hp0 = me.Hp;
            Character.Points = 3;
            PBuy("hull").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            // (points are added BEFORE percentage bonuses: the five default chips' +25% makes +5 into +6.25)
            Check(Character.Bought[1] == 1 && Character.Points == 2 && Math.Abs(me.MaxHp - (hull0 + 6.25)) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(me.Hp - (hp0 + 6.25)) < 1e-6,
                  "Hull (1 pt): +5 hull (x1.25 by the default chips: +6.25) on the ship at once");
            PBuy("hull").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Character.Bought[1] == 2 && Character.Points == 0 && Math.Abs(me.MaxHp - (hull0 + 12.5)) < 1e-6, "Hull again (2 pts): +10 in all (+12.5 with the chips)");
            Check(PBuy("speed").Disabled, "with no points left, BUY is disabled");
            Character.Points = 6;
            PBuy("damage").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); PBuy("speed").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); PBuy("turn").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            var baseSheet = new ShipStats(me.Class, Equipment.Bonuses(me.Class, me.Loadout));            // the same gear, no points
            // Weapons moves whatever THIS hull calls its weapons, by the step its own row declares
            // (Classes.Damage); the figures behind that declaration are checked as literals where
            // the table is proved.
            // WEAPONS IS A SHARE NOW: three percent of each stat the hull declares, per point, so
            // a pilot compounds the way a boss does. Engines and Rudder are still amounts.
            var mine = Classes.Damage(me.Class).Where(kv => kv.Value != 0).ToList();
            Check(mine.Count > 0 && mine.All(kv => Math.Abs(me.Stats[kv.Key] - baseSheet[kv.Key] - new ShipStats(me.Class)[kv.Key] * 0.03) < 1e-6)
                  && Math.Abs(me.Stats["max_speed"] - baseSheet["max_speed"] - 1) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(me.Stats["turn_rate"] - baseSheet["turn_rate"] - Mathf.DegToRad(1f)) < 1e-6,
                  $"Weapons +3% on every weapon this hull declares ({string.Join(", ", mine.Select(kv => kv.Key))}), Engines +1 speed, Rudder +1 degree/s");
            // ...and the ROW says so, in the units the STATS tab prints. It showed the row's own
            // Per, with a `u.Id == "turn"` special case to read out degrees.
            Label PRow(string id) => Nodes<Label>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(pw).First(r => r.Name == "Pilot_" + id)).First();
            Check(PRow("turn").Text.Contains("Rudder limit") && PRow("turn").Text.Contains("+0.02 rad/s next")
                  && PRow("hull").Text.Contains("Hull points") && PRow("hull").Text.Contains("+5 next")
                  && PRow("speed").Text.Contains("Top speed ahead") && PRow("speed").Text.Contains("+1 u/s next"),
                  $"each pilot row names the stat it moves and what one point adds to it, in that stat's own units: 1 deg/s is +0.02 rad/s, +5 hull, +1 u/s ({PRow("turn").Text.Replace('\n', '|')})");
            string cid = Character.Id; int[] saved = (int[])Character.Bought.Clone();
            Character.Save(); System.Array.Clear(Character.Bought); Character.Load(cid);
            Check(Character.Bought.SequenceEqual(saved) && Character.Level == 3, "purchases and level are saved with the character");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Count<PilotWindow>() == 0, "L closes it");
            // leave the pilot as found for what follows
            System.Array.Clear(Character.Bought); Character.Points = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.Exp = 0; Character.Save();
            Priv(H, "ApplyLocalIdentity");
        }

        // ── damage rules: one hit per source per 0.35 s; regeneration; 12 s out of combat ──
        {
            var me = Me; double hp0 = me.Hp;
            me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:a"); me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:a");
            Check(Math.Abs(hp0 - me.Hp - 5) < 0.2, "the same source lands only once inside 0.35 s");
            me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:b");
            Check(Math.Abs(hp0 - me.Hp - 10) < 0.3, "a different source lands at once");
            await Wait(0.55); double hp1 = me.Hp;
            me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:a");
            Check(hp1 - me.Hp > 4.5, "after 0.52 s the first source lands again");
            // ...AND A VOLLEY IS NOT ONE SOURCE. The gap exists for an ONGOING source -- a
            // sweeping beam, a ram, an area tick -- not for three separate bodies that each strike
            // once and end. Three shots down one bearing in one frame, all naming one weapon, as
            // the Lancer's trident does: every fired shot carries its own identity now
            // (Shots.SourceKey), so all three land where a shared name left a hull feeling one.
            // Any bearing and any standoff; the hull is put at rest first (Place) so the shots
            // cannot chase it, and the damage is read off the tally, which regeneration does not
            // move. Nothing is healed afterwards: the line below puts the hull on 50% anyway.
            {
                var volAt = me.Position; float volBear = VaryAngle(), volStand = Vary(200f, 320f);
                me.Place(volAt, me.Rotation);
                var volFrom = volAt + Vector2.Right.Rotated(volBear) * volStand;
                var volAim = (volAt - volFrom).Normalized();
                // THE GAP COUNTS BODIES, THE TALLY COUNTS WEAPONS. Each shot carries its own
                // identity for the 0.52 s gap, so all three land; the tally is keyed by the WEAPON
                // that fired them, so it stays one line per gun instead of gaining one per round
                // ever fired at this hull.
                double Volley() => me.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("test:volley", out var v) ? v : 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
                    Combat.FireSlug(volFrom, volAim, 900f, volStand * 2f, 6f, 7.0, scrap: false, 0, "test:volley");
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && Volley() < 21.0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                int volLines = me.DamageBySource.Keys.Count(k => k.StartsWith("test:volley"));
                Check(Math.Abs(Volley() - 21.0) < 1e-9 && volLines == 1,
                      $"a volley of three from one weapon lands three times on one hull -- 3 x 7 = 21, where one shared name left a hull feeling 7 -- and the tally it lands in is still ONE line for that weapon ({Volley():0.000} over {volLines} line, fired from {volStand:0} u off at {Mathf.RadToDeg(volBear):0} deg)");
            }
            Check(PlayerShip.CombatHold == 12, "out of combat means 12 s without dealing or taking damage");
            me.TakeDamage(me.Hp - me.MaxHp * 0.5); me.NoteCombat(); double h0 = me.Hp;
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Hp - h0) / me.MaxHp - 0.005) < 0.0015, $"in combat it regenerates 0.5% of max hull a second ({(me.Hp - h0) / me.MaxHp * 100:0.00}%)");
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_combatT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(me, 0.0);
            double h1 = me.Hp; await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Hp - h1) / me.MaxHp - 0.03) < 0.004, $"out of combat, 3% a second ({(me.Hp - h1) / me.MaxHp * 100:0.00}%)");
            me.TakeDamage(-(me.MaxHp - me.Hp));
        }

        // ── raiders (6a): the light fighter -- cruise, boost, post, pin, laser ──
        {
            var me = Me; var keepPos = me.Position;
            me.Position = new Vector2(4000, 2500); me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            var r1 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(0, 2300));
            await Wait(0.5); var rp0 = r1.Position; await Wait(0.5);
            float cruise = (r1.Position - rp0).Length() / 0.5f;
            Check(r1.Target == me && Math.Abs(cruise - 100f) < 8f && !r1.Boosting, $"a light raider goes for the nearest ship at an unupgraded capital ship's pace ({cruise:0} u/s)");
            Check(Math.Abs(Raider.BoostAt - 1600f) < 1e-3, "it boosts from 1600 u (3 s at 500%, plus its 100 u reach)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && !r1.Boosting; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float atBoost = r1.Position.DistanceTo(me.Position); var b0 = r1.Position; await Wait(0.4);
            float rboost = (r1.Position - b0).Length() / 0.4f;
            Check(Math.Abs(atBoost - 1600) < 30 && Math.Abs(rboost - 500) < 30, $"at 1600 u ({atBoost:0}) it boosts to 500% ({rboost:0} u/s)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && !r1.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float rheld = Raider.Gap(r1.Position, me);                 // from the HULL: beside the ship, never on it
            Check(r1.Latched && rheld > 60 && rheld <= Raider.PinRange, $"it lands near its target, not on top: posted {rheld:0} u off the hull");
            var r2 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(300, 0)); var r3 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(-300, 0));
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && !(r2.Latched && r3.Latched); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var bow = Vector2.Up.Rotated(me.Rotation);
            var angs = new[] { r1, r2, r3 }.Select(r => Mathf.RadToDeg(bow.AngleTo(r.Position - me.Position))).OrderBy(a => a).ToList();
            Check(r2.Latched && r3.Latched && Math.Abs(angs[0] + 90) < 20 && Math.Abs(angs[1]) < 20 && Math.Abs(angs[2] - 90) < 20,
                  $"three hold posts ahead, left and right ({string.Join(", ", angs.Select(a => a.ToString("0")))} deg off the bow)");
            Check(me.Pinned, "rheld there, they pin it");
            float rot0 = me.Rotation;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.A, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.A, Pressed = true });
            await Wait(1.0);
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.A, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.A, Pressed = false });
            float rspd = me.Velocity.Length(), rtop = (float)me.Stats["max_speed"];
            Check(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(rot0, me.Rotation)) < 0.01f && rspd <= rtop * 0.2f + 1f && me.SpeedAhead > 5f,
                  $"pinned: A does not turn it, and forced thrust holds it to 20% of top speed ({rspd:0} of {rtop:0} u/s)");
            double Raided() => me.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key.StartsWith("raider:")).Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            double dm0 = Raided(); await Wait(5.0);
            double dps = (Raided() - dm0) / 5.0;
            Check(Math.Abs(dps - 3.0) < 0.7, $"three lights, 1 DPS each ({dps:0.00} DPS)");
            foreach (var r in new[] { r1, r2, r3 }) r.TakeDamage(999);
            await Wait(0.5);
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0 && !Combat.Hostiles.OfType<Raider>().Any() && !me.Pinned, "shot down, they are gone -- and the pin lets go");
            // A miner OUT IN THE BELT, beyond the base's own guns (600 u): one caught docking had its
            // raider shot down by them before it could latch. Where the fleet is at this point depends
            // on how long the router scenarios above took in real time, so it is waited for.
            Gatherer mnr = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 40 && mnr == null; i++)
            {
                mnr = H.Yard.Gatherers.FirstOrDefault(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner && g.State == Gatherer.St.Working && g.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) > 900);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            mnr ??= H.Yard.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner);
            // an EMPTY hold, so it goes on working (still) through the whole check: one picked nearly
            // full left for home at its full 110 u/s as the raider arrived, and a raider cruising at
            // 100 with its boost spent never caught it to latch
            mnr.Cargo = 0;
            var r4 = H.SpawnRaider(mnr.Position + new Vector2(120, 0));
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !r4.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float mr0 = mnr.Rotation; var mp0 = mnr.Position; await Wait(0.5);
            float rmspd = (mnr.Position - mp0).Length() / 0.5f;
            Check(r4.Target == mnr && mnr.Pinned && Math.Abs(rmspd - (float)mnr.Speed * 0.2f) < 3f && Math.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(mr0, mnr.Rotation)) < 0.01f,
                  $"a miner is pinned the same way: forward at 20%, no turning ({rmspd:0} u/s of {mnr.Speed:0}; target {(r4.Target as Node)?.Name}, pinned {mnr.Pinned}, turned {Mathf.AngleDifference(mr0, mnr.Rotation):0.000})");
            r4.TakeDamage(999); await Wait(0.3);
            me.Position = keepPos; me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.TakeDamage(-(me.MaxHp - me.Hp));   // hand over at full hull
        }

        // ── raiders (6b): the heavy -- waits at the map's edge; a predicted missile; boosts at 700% on a pinned target ──
        {
            var hm = Me; var keepH = hm.Position;
            hm.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2000, 0); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            var hv = H.SpawnRaider(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2600, 500), Enemies.Gunship);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(hv.Heavy && Math.Abs(hv.Length - 4 * Raider.LightLength) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(Raider.HeavyDps - 2 * Raider.RaiderDps) < 1e-9 && Raider.RaiderDps == 1,
                  "a heavy raider: four times a light's length, twice the raider damage (2 DPS)");
            double HvLaser() => hm.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key == $"raider:{hv.NetId}").Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            double HvMissile() => hm.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key == $"heavy:{hv.NetId}:missile").Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            var edge = Raider.EdgeSpot(hm.Position);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 22 && hv.Position.DistanceTo(edge) > 8f; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(0.5);
            float hvFacing = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hv.Rotation, (hm.Position - hv.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f)));
            Check(hv.Position.DistanceTo(edge) < 10f && Math.Abs(hv.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - 4200f) < 10f && hvFacing < 15f && HvLaser() == 0,
                  $"its target unpinned, it waits at the map's edge nearest it ({hv.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos):0} u from the base), facing it, laser silent");
            // the missile: within 500 u of the heavy, at where the target will be in TWELVE s
            hm.Position = edge + (Hub.BasePos - edge).Normalized() * 420f; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && H.BlastsPending == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var blastRing = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone && Math.Abs(t.Radius - Raider.BlastRadius) < 0.1f);
            Check(H.BlastsPending == 1 && blastRing != null && blastRing.Position.DistanceTo(hm.Position) < 6f
                  && Math.Abs(blastRing.Time - 12) < 1e-6 && blastRing.Warn && blastRing.Anchor == Fx.World,
                  "within 500 u it fires at where the target will be; a red circle -- a warning row of Fx.All, pinned to the ground -- marks it for 12 s, long enough to be somewhere else by then");
            double mm0 = HvMissile();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 15 && H.BlastsPending > 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(HvMissile() - mm0 - 42) < 1e-6, $"staying on the circle, the gunship row's 42-damage blast lands -- 40% harder than the 30 it was, for being 12 s in the air rather than 7 ({HvMissile() - mm0:0})");
            typeof(Raider).GetField("_missileCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hv, 0.0);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && H.BlastsPending == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            hm.Position += new Vector2(0, 300); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;                         // off the circle
            // ...and no SECOND missile while this one flies: the flight is 12 s and so is the
            // cadence, so a 15 s wait would otherwise end with a fresh one still in the air.
            typeof(Raider).GetField("_missileCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hv, 99.0);
            double mm1 = HvMissile();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 15 && H.BlastsPending > 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(H.BlastsPending == 0 && HvMissile() == mm1, "moving off the circle dodges it");
            // anything out of place -- a wild speed, a broken number -- and it aims right at the target
            var pz = new Vector2(100, 200);
            Check(Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(10, 0)) == pz + new Vector2(120, 0) && Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(5000, 0)) == pz
                  && Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(float.NaN, 0)) == pz && Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(float.PositiveInfinity, 1)) == pz,
                  "the missile leads a sane speed the whole 12 s of its flight -- 10 u/s carried 120 u -- and a wild speed, NaN or infinity aims at where the target is");
            // live: 2700 u/s in 45 u steps (wild, yet under the 50 u jump filter) -- the circle must sit on the ship
            hm.Position = edge + (Hub.BasePos - edge).Normalized() * 400f; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            typeof(Raider).GetField("_missileCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hv, 0.12);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            int rings0 = Nodes<FxNode>().Count(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone);
            Vector2 atFire = hm.Position;
            for (int i = 0; i < 20 && Nodes<FxNode>().Count(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone) == rings0; i++)
            {
                atFire = hm.Position; hm.Position += new Vector2(0, 45);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var wildRing = Nodes<FxNode>().LastOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone && Math.Abs(t.Radius - Raider.BlastRadius) < 0.1f);
            Check(wildRing != null && wildRing.Position.DistanceTo(atFire) < 100f,
                  $"a target moving at a wild 2700 u/s: the circle sits on it, not 7 s ahead ({(wildRing == null ? -1 : wildRing.Position.DistanceTo(atFire)):0} u off)");
            // pinned: it boosts at 700% until 300 u away, then closes at cruise and fires 2 DPS
            hm.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(1900, 0); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.Rotation = 0f;
            var lights = new[] { H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(0, -250)), H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(250, 0)), H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(-250, 0)) };
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !hm.Pinned; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(0.1); var hp1 = hv.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            float burn = (hv.Position - hp1).Length() / 0.2f;
            // the spec's numbers as literals: a check that reads the code's own constant cannot catch it being wrong
            Check(hm.Pinned && Math.Abs(burn - 700f) < 60f, $"its target pinned, it boosts at 700% ({burn:0} u/s)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && hv.Position.DistanceTo(hm.Position) > 300f - 20f; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var hp2 = hv.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            float slow = (hv.Position - hp2).Length() / 0.2f;
            // (over the ground: cruise plus the pinned target's own forced thrust, ~20 u/s)
            Check(slow <= 100f + 0.2f * (float)hm.Stats["max_speed"] + 5f, $"inside 300 u it closes at cruise ({slow:0} u/s over the ground)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !hv.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            double hl0 = HvLaser(); await Wait(3.0);
            double hdps = (HvLaser() - hl0) / 3.0;
            Check(hv.Latched && Raider.Gap(hv.Position, hm) <= Raider.HeavyReach && Math.Abs(hdps - 2) < 0.4,
                  $"from 135 u off the hull its laser does 2 DPS ({hdps:0.00})");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(9999);
            await Wait(0.3);
            hm.Position = keepH; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.TakeDamage(-(hm.MaxHp - hm.Hp));
        }

        // ── raiders (6c): patrols -- 3 lights and 1 heavy; nothing within 2000 u, no target ──
        {
            int pid = H.SpawnPatrol(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 6500));
            var pat = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == pid).ToList();
            Check(pat.Count == 4 && pat.Count(r => !r.Heavy) == 3 && pat.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 1, "a patrol: three lights and one heavy");
            await Wait(1.0);
            float pd0 = pat[0].Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos);
            Check(pat.All(r => r.Target == null) && pd0 < 6500 - 60 && pd0 > 6500 - 200,
                  $"nothing within 2000 u: they take no target, and head for the perimeter at a patrol's pace ({6500 - pd0:0} u in 1 s)");
            var pm = Me; var keepP = pm.Position;
            pm.Position = pat[0].Position + new Vector2(0, -1500); pm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            await Wait(0.5);
            var heavyP = pat.First(r => r.Heavy);
            Check(pat.Where(r => !r.Heavy).All(r => r.Target == pm) && heavyP.Target == pm,
                  "a ship within 2000 u: the lights go for it, and their heavy takes the same target");
            await RangeChecks();                // something real to select, at a real distance
            ZoomChecks();
            await TourChecks();
            // ── THREE AT ONCE, ON THE HULLS BIG ENOUGH TO HOLD THEM ──
            // How many a hull may hold is its class's row, so "capital ships only" is a number and
            // not a list of class names in an `if`. A light still holds one: picking a second
            // REPLACES the first rather than being refused, because a pilot who clicks a new
            // target means to take it.
            {
                var trio = H.Raiders.Take(4).Cast<IHittable>().ToList();
                if (trio.Count < 4) { Check(false, "multi-target: fewer than four raiders to pick"); }
                else
                {
                    H.ClearSelection();
                    foreach (var pick in trio) H.AddTarget(pick);
                    int capHeld = H.Targets.Count;
                    bool orderKept = H.Targets.SequenceEqual(trio.Take(3));
                    H.AddTarget(trio[1]);                          // picking one already held drops it
                    bool dropped = H.Targets.Count == 2 && !H.Targets.Contains(trio[1]);
                    var firstOf = H.Selected;
                    H.SelectTarget(trio[3]);                        // a plain click starts over
                    bool restart = H.Targets.Count == 1 && H.Selected == trio[3];
                    Check(H.TargetCap == 3 && capHeld == 3 && orderKept && dropped && restart
                          && ReferenceEquals(firstOf, trio[0]),
                          $"a capital holds trio targets at once and no more, in the order it picked them; picking one it holds drops that one, and a plain click starts over ({capHeld} of {H.TargetCap} held)");

                    // ...and a light fighter holds exactly one, from the same row.
                    var keepClass = Character.Class;
                    await SetClass(ShipClass.LightDart);
                    H.ClearSelection();
                    foreach (var pick in trio) H.AddTarget(pick);
                    Check(H.TargetCap == 1 && H.Targets.Count == 1 && H.Selected == trio[3]
                          && Classes.Of(ShipClass.Battleship).Targets == 3 && Classes.Of(ShipClass.LightDart).Targets == 1,
                          $"a light holds one, and taking another replaces it -- the number is the class's row, not a list of names ({H.Targets.Count} of {H.TargetCap})");
                    await SetClass(keepClass);
                    H.ClearSelection();
                }
            }
            foreach (var r in pat) if (GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(r)) r.TakeDamage(9999);
            await Wait(0.3);
            pm.Position = keepP; pm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; pm.TakeDamage(-(pm.MaxHp - pm.Hp));
        }

        // ── the base's own weapons: a 5 DPS laser at 300 u, a tracking 25-damage missile at 600 u -- raiders only ──
        {
            var bd = Nodes<BaseDefense>().First();
            Check(BaseDefense.LaserRange == 300 && BaseDefense.MissileRange == 600 && BaseDefense.LaserDps == 5 && BaseDefense.MissileDamage == 25
                  && Math.Abs(BaseDefense.MissileSpeed - 1.2f * 100f) < 1e-3, "the base: a 5 DPS laser at 300 u; a 25-damage missile at 600 u, 120 u/s");
            var dd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1"); var dHome = dd.Position;
            dd.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 250); dd.ResetMeter();
            double l0 = bd.LaserDealt; int m0 = bd.MissilesFired; await Wait(2.0);
            Check(bd.LaserDealt == l0 && bd.MissilesFired == m0 && dd.Total == 0, "it never fires at the practice dummies");
            dd.Position = dHome;
            var spot1 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 280);
            var rb = H.SpawnRaider(spot1); rb.Hp = 1000;
            double lz = bd.LaserDealt;
            for (int i = 0; i < 120; i++) { rb.Position = spot1; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            double laser2 = bd.LaserDealt - lz;
            Check(Math.Abs(laser2 - 10) < 2.6, $"a raider inside 300 u: the laser burns it at 5 DPS ({laser2:0.0} in 2 s)");
            rb.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            var spot2 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -500);        // north of the base: clear of the hauler pad's own gun
            var rc = H.SpawnRaider(spot2); rc.Hp = 1000;
            int mz = bd.MissilesFired; double lz2 = bd.LaserDealt;
            Shot bm = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && bm == null; i++)
            {
                rc.Position = spot2;
                bm = Missiles().FirstOrDefault(t => !t.HostileFire && !t.Heavy && t.TargetId == rc.NetId);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var bp1 = bm?.GlobalPosition ?? Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) { rc.Position = spot2; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            float bspd = bm != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(bm) ? (bm.GlobalPosition - bp1).Length() / 0.25f : 0f;
            double hpc = rc.Hp;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && rc.Hp == hpc; i++) { rc.Position = spot2; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(bm != null && bd.MissilesFired > mz && bd.LaserDealt == lz2 && Math.Abs(bspd - 120f) < 6f && Math.Abs(hpc - rc.Hp - 25) < 1e-6,
                  $"a raider at 500 u: no laser, but a tracking missile at {bspd:0} u/s lands for {hpc - rc.Hp:0}");
            rc.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            var spot3 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -700);
            var rd = H.SpawnRaider(spot3); rd.Hp = 1000;
            int mw = bd.MissilesFired; double lw = bd.LaserDealt;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6; i++) { rd.Position = spot3; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(bd.MissilesFired == mw && bd.LaserDealt == lw, "a raider at 700 u: out of reach, nothing fires");
            rd.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
        }

        // ── enemy fire, shields, stasis, the escape pod, re-boarding ──
        {
            var d3x = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy3");
            Check(d3x.Armed && !H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1").Armed, "only the bottom-right dummy is armed");
            var sh = Me;
            double Dummy() => sh.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("?", out var v) ? v : 0;    // (hull regenerates: count the hit itself)
            double hit0 = Dummy();
            sh.Position = d3x.Position + new Vector2(0, -120); sh.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; sh.Rotation = 0f;
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && Dummy() == hit0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(Dummy() - hit0 - 50) < 0.01, $"the armed dummy's missile hits a ship within 150 u for 50 ({Dummy() - hit0:0})");
            var shield = Nodes<ShieldFlash>(sh).First();
            Check(shield.Glow(2) > 0 && shield.Glow(0) == 0, "the shield lights the side facing the dummy (astern), not the bow");
            sh.Position = d3x.Position + new Vector2(0, -1200); await Wait(6.0);
            double taken1 = sh.DamageBySource.Values.Sum(); await Wait(6.0);        // (hull regenerates: count damage instead)
            Check(sh.DamageBySource.Values.Sum() == taken1, "outside 150 u it holds fire");
            Check(sh.Covers(sh.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * (Classes.Art(sh.Class).Length * 0.45f), 0f)
                  && !sh.Covers(sh.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * (Classes.Art(sh.Class).HalfWidth + 12f), 0f),
                  "the collider is a capsule: the bow is inside, a point just off the beam is not");
            // death
            sh.TakeDamage(sh.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.2);
            var pod = Nodes<EscapePod>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(!sh.Alive && sh.StasisLeft > 119 && pod != null && !Combat.Hostiles.Contains(pod as IHittable) && !Combat.Players.Any(p => (object)p == pod),
                  "destroyed: stasis (2:00) and an escape pod that nothing can target");
            Check(pod != null && Math.Abs(pod.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First().Texture.GetHeight() * pod.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First().Scale.Y - 24f) < 0.5f,
                  "the pod is 24 u long (60% of a salvager's 40)");
            var podStart = pod.Position;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W);
            Check(pod.Position.DistanceTo(podStart) > 20f && sh.Velocity == Vector2.Zero, "W flies the pod; the ship stays put in stasis");
            Check(H.GetType().GetField("_cam", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance) != null && sh.ViewPosition == pod.Position, "the camera follows the pod");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!sh.Alive, "F does nothing before stasis ends");
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_stasis", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(sh, 0.5);
            await Wait(0.7);
            Check(sh.CanReboard, "after the timer the ship is ready");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(sh.Alive && sh.Hp >= sh.MaxHp * 0.33 - 1e-6 && sh.Hp < sh.MaxHp * 0.34 && Nodes<EscapePod>().Count() == 0, "F re-boards at 33% hull (regenerating from there); the pod is gone");
            sh.TakeDamage(-(sh.MaxHp - sh.Hp));  // (test only) back to full for what follows
        }

        // a double-click on empty space clears the target; a single click there does not
        {
            var dd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            Click(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            var empty = Me.Position + new Vector2(-260, -260);
            Click(Scr(empty)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == dd, "a single click on empty space keeps the target");
            DoubleClick(Scr(empty)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == null, "a double-click on empty space clears it");
            Click(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            DoubleClick(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == dd, "a double-click on a target keeps it selected");
        }

        // selection rules still hold
        await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(-300, 300));
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Tab); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d1, "Tab selects the enemy nearest the ship");
        Click(Scr(d2.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d2, "left-click selects another");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == null && H != null, "Esc clears the target before leaving");

        // placement hook
        Vector2? placedAt = null;
        H.BeginPlacement("TEST", p => placedAt = p);
        var spot = d1.Position + new Vector2(-150, -150);
        Click(Scr(spot)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(placedAt.HasValue && placedAt.Value.DistanceTo(spot) < 3f && !H.Placing, "left-click confirms a placement at the cursor");

        // wings: class swap and rebuild leave no orphans
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);
        Check(Count<Wing>() == 0, "to battleship: wings gone");
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier);
        Priv(H, "RebuildShips"); await Wait(0.1);
        int craft = (int)(Me.Stats["fighter_count"] + Me.Stats["bomber_count"]);
        Check(Count<Wing>() == craft && Count<PlayerShip>() == 1, $"rebuild leaves no orphan wings ({Count<Wing>()} of {craft})");

        // ── camera: wheel zoom, the free camera (Y), its tether ──
        {
            var sh = Me;
            void Wheel(MouseButton b) { Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex = b, Pressed = true, Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) }); }
            for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) Wheel(MouseButton.WheelDown); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, Hub.DefaultZoom / Hub.ZoomOutMax), $"wheel out stops at 33% further out ({H.ZoomLevel:0.000})");
            for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) Wheel(MouseButton.WheelUp); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, Hub.DefaultZoom * Hub.ZoomInMax), $"wheel in stops at 1.5x ({H.ZoomLevel:0.000})");
            H.SetZoom(Hub.DefaultZoom * 0.8f); float kept = H.ZoomLevel;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) });
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Y); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.FreeCamera, "Y frees the camera");
            var cam0 = H.CameraPosition;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Right); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Right);
            Check(H.CameraPosition.X - cam0.X > 800, $"arrow keys move it ({H.CameraPosition.X - cam0.X:0} u in 1 s)");
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Right); await Wait(6.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Right);
            float tether = H.CameraPosition.DistanceTo(sh.ViewPosition);
            Check(Mathf.Abs(tether - sh.MyArt.CameraRange) < 1f && sh.MyArt.CameraRange == 5000f, $"it stops 5000 u from the ship ({tether:0})");
            var cam1 = H.CameraPosition;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(2, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(2, 540)) });
            await Wait(0.5);
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) });
            Check(H.CameraPosition.X < cam1.X - 100f, "the mouse against the screen edge pans it too");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Y); await Wait(2.0);
            Check(!H.FreeCamera && H.CameraPosition.DistanceTo(sh.ViewPosition) < 30f && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, kept),
                  "Y again returns to the ship, keeping the zoom");
            H.SetZoom(Hub.DefaultZoom);
        }
