        // ── THE ENEMY TABLE: six rows, and every figure in them ──────────────────
        // (Behaviour checks elsewhere read Enemies.Of(kind); these are the literals behind them.)
        {
            EnemyDef E(int k) => Enemies.Of(k);
            Check(Enemies.All.Length == 6
                  && E(Enemies.Webifier).Hull == 25 && E(Enemies.Webifier).Dps == 1.0 && E(Enemies.Webifier).Reach == 100f
                  && E(Enemies.Gunship).Hull == 100 && E(Enemies.Gunship).Dps == 2.0 && E(Enemies.Gunship).Reach == 150f,
                  "the two the game is balanced on: a webifier of 25 hull and 1 x damage inside 100 u, a gunship of 100 and 2 x inside 150");
            Check(E(Enemies.Talon).Hull == 18 && E(Enemies.Talon).Dps == 1.4 && E(Enemies.Talon).Cruise == 130f
                  && E(Enemies.Pod).Hull == 60 && E(Enemies.Pod).Dps == 0.7 && E(Enemies.Pod).Cruise == 78f
                  && E(Enemies.Cross).Hull == 130 && E(Enemies.Cross).Dps == 2.6 && !E(Enemies.Cross).Missiles
                  && E(Enemies.Lancerkin).Hull == 150 && E(Enemies.Lancerkin).Reach == 260f && E(Enemies.Lancerkin).Missiles,
                  "and the owner's four: a talon (18 hull, 1.4 x, 130 u/s), a pod (60, 0.7, 78), a cross (130, 2.6, no missiles), a lancerkin (150, standing 260 u off, with them)");
            Check(Enemies.OfWay(EnemyWay.Pin).Length == 3 && Enemies.OfWay(EnemyWay.Standoff).Length == 3
                  && Enemies.All.All(e => e.Texture != null && e.Texture.StartsWith("res://")),
                  "three that pin and three that stand off, each with its own hull drawn");
            // ── AN ENEMY'S MISSILE IS ITS OWN ROW ──
            // The figures were Raider consts, so both rows that throw a missile threw the SAME
            // missile: the Lancerkin, which stands 260 u off against the gunship's 150, fired the
            // gunship's 500 u missile and could not be given one of its own. Given one here, the
            // gunship's must not move -- which is the thing a shared const could never manage. The
            // row is put back before the Check, and no frame runs in between.
            {
                var kin = E(Enemies.Lancerkin); var gun = E(Enemies.Gunship);
                float keepRange = kin.MissileRange; double keepEvery = kin.MissileEvery, keepDamage = kin.MissileDamage;
                kin.MissileRange = 900f; kin.MissileEvery = 5.0; kin.MissileDamage = 44;
                bool apart = kin.MissileRange == 900f && kin.MissileEvery == 5.0 && kin.MissileDamage == 44
                             && gun.MissileRange == 500f && gun.MissileEvery == 12.0 && gun.MissileDamage == 42;
                kin.MissileRange = keepRange; kin.MissileEvery = keepEvery; kin.MissileDamage = keepDamage;
                Check(apart && Enemies.All.Count(x => x.Missiles) == 2
                      && kin.MissileRange == 500f && kin.MissileEvery == 12.0 && kin.MissileDamage == 42 && kin.BlastRadius == 90f
                      && gun.BlastRadius == 90f && Math.Abs(Raider.MissileFlight - 12) < 1e-9,
                      $"the missile is each row's own: 42 damage inside 500 u every 12 s in a 90 u blast, 12 s out -- and a lancerkin given 900 u / 5 s / 44 leaves the gunship's 500 u / 12 s / 42 exactly where they were (back at {kin.MissileRange:0} u)");
            }
            // a wave draws from the table, so the kinds rotate rather than being written in
            var p1 = H.SpawnPatrol(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -3400));
            var p2 = H.SpawnPatrol(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -3600));
            await Wait(0.1);
            var w1 = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == p1).ToList();
            var w2 = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == p2).ToList();
            Check(w1.Count > 0 && w2.Count > 0 && w1.Select(r => r.Kind).Distinct().Count() == 2
                  && w1.Select(r => r.Kind).First() != w2.Select(r => r.Kind).First(),
                  $"one patrol of each way, and the next wave draws different kinds ({string.Join(",", w1.Select(r => Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Name).Distinct())} then {string.Join(",", w2.Select(r => Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Name).Distinct())})");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == p1 || r.Patrol == p2).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(99999);
            await Wait(0.3);

            // ── A WAVE IS A ROW (Waves.All) ─────────────────────────────────
            // Both of these are sent by the SAME builder that sent the two patrols above -- the
            // only builder there is (Raids.Send) -- and both were impossible before it: the old
            // Hub.SpawnPatrol drew ONE pin kind and ONE standoff kind a wave, and took `heavy` as
            // a bool. The escort's late waves are the rows that ask for them.
            {
                var was = H.Raiders.Select(r => r.NetId).ToHashSet();
                H.HuntWave(Me, 6);                                    // an even wave from the seventh on: hunt_mixed
                await Wait(0.05);
                var mixed = H.Raiders.Where(r => !was.Contains(r.NetId)).ToList();
                var pins = mixed.Where(r => !r.Heavy).ToList();
                Check(mixed.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 0 && pins.Count >= 5
                      && pins.Select(r => r.Kind).Distinct().Count() == 2
                      && pins.GroupBy(r => r.Kind).Min(g => g.Count()) == 2
                      && pins.All(r => Math.Abs(r.MaxHull - 0.5 * Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Hull * r.Strength) < 1e-9),
                      $"a wave MIXES TWO PINNER KINDS -- the rotation's next row, two of them, at half hull ({string.Join(" + ", pins.GroupBy(r => r.Kind).Select(g => $"{g.Count()} {Enemies.Of(g.Key).Name}"))})");
                foreach (var r in mixed) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
                await Wait(0.2);

                was = H.Raiders.Select(r => r.NetId).ToHashSet();
                H.HuntWave(Me, 9);                                    // an odd wave from the tenth on: hunt_twin
                await Wait(0.05);
                var twin = H.Raiders.Where(r => !was.Contains(r.NetId)).ToList();
                var stand = twin.Where(r => r.Heavy).ToList();
                Check(stand.Count == 2 && twin.Count(r => !r.Heavy) >= 3
                      && stand.Any(r => r.Kind == Enemies.Gunship),
                      $"...and a wave fields TWO STANDOFFS -- one drawn from the rotation and the gunship named outright ({string.Join(" + ", stand.Select(r => Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Name))})");
                foreach (var r in twin) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
                await Wait(0.2);
            }

            // ── A THIRD HOST-SPAWNED REPLICATED KIND IS A ROW, NOT A THIRD COPY ──
            // The raiders and the deployed turrets were the same six methods written twice: mint
            // an id from a NetIds space, add, tell the world, guard for idempotence, remove with
            // or without a burst. Registering a kind here adds NOTHING to Hub -- the row says its
            // space, what bag holds it and how one is built, and Hub.Spawn / Hub.Down take it.
            {
                var nfm = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
                int raiders0 = H.Raiders.Count, turrets0 = H.Deployed.Count;
                int extra = Spawns.All.Count;
                Spawns.All.Add(new SpawnKind {
                    Id = "Probe", Space = NetIds.Menu, Burst = 0f,
                    Bag = k => new SpawnSet<SpawnProbe>(k),
                    Make = (h, s) => new SpawnProbe { NetId = s.NetId, Position = s.At },
                    Seed = n => new SpawnSeed(((SpawnProbe)n).NetId, n.Position, 0, 0, 0),
                });
                var probeAt = VaryNear(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -5400), 400f);
                var probe = H.Spawn(extra, probeAt, 0, 0, 0) as SpawnProbe;
                bool born = probe != null && probe.GetParent() == H && probe.Position == probeAt
                            && probe.NetId > NetIds.Menu && probe.NetId < NetIds.Menu + 10000
                            && probe.Name.ToString() == $"Probe_{probe.NetId}";
                H.Down(extra, probe, burst: false, 0f);
                bool buried = probe != null && probe.IsQueuedForDeletion();
                // ...and the six each kind used to need are GONE, with exactly two spawn RPCs left
                // on Hub for every kind there will ever be. NetDeployHulls is in the list too: the
                // 10 Hz HULL packet was the turrets' own, and is now one packet for every kind that
                // fills in SpawnKind.Hull (Hub.NetHulls), so a further kind adds no RPC either.
                int spawnRpcs = typeof(Hub).GetMethods(nfm).Count(mi => mi.Name.StartsWith("NetSpawn"));
                bool noCopies = new[] { "NetRaiderSpawn", "AddRaider", "DropRaider", "NetRaiderGone",
                                        "NetDeploy", "AddDeployed", "DropDeployed", "RemoveDeployed", "NetDeployGone",
                                        "NetDeployHulls" }
                                .All(nm => typeof(Hub).GetMethod(nm, nfm) == null);
                Spawns.All.RemoveAt(extra);                           // the table back as the game ships it
                Check(born && buried && spawnRpcs == 2 && noCopies
                      && H.Raiders.Count == raiders0 && H.Deployed.Count == turrets0,
                      $"a FURTHER host-spawned replicated kind took the generic path with no new method: its own id space ({probe?.NetId}), "
                      + $"built, named and dropped by Hub.Spawn/Hub.Down, {spawnRpcs} spawn RPCs on Hub and none of the per-kind methods left ({noCopies})");
            }
        }

        // ── THE NINE NEW CLASSES ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // Every class in the table flies, with the hull, mounts and bar its row says; then the
        // one thing each of the nine is FOR.
        {
            foreach (var cd in Classes.All.Where(x => x.Ready).ToList())
            {
                await SetClass(cd.Id);
                var nc = Me; var sheet = nc.Stats;
                int nMain = Nodes<Turret>(nc).Count(t => !t.PointDefense), nPd = Nodes<Turret>(nc).Count(t => t.PointDefense);
                Check(nc.Class == cd.Id && Math.Abs(nc.MaxHp - sheet["hull"]) < 1e-6
                      && nMain == (int)sheet["main_count"] && nPd == (int)sheet["pd_count"]
                      && Abilities.For(cd.Id).Length == cd.Abilities.Length + 6
                      && Classes.Art(cd.Id).Texture != null,
                      $"{cd.Name} flies: {sheet["hull"]:0} hull, {nMain} main and {nPd} PD mounts, {cd.Abilities.Length} abilities and six open slots");
            }

            // ── FREIGHTER: three turrets out, one collected, and a bubble that soaks 400 ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightHauler);
            // EMPTY SPACE to work in: the practice fighters at (900, 550) are small craft, so a
            // deployed turret would lock onto one and never kill it, and the belt, the wreck and the
            // portal all have traffic. South-east and far out is clear of every one of them.
            var fr = Me; var yonder = VaryNear(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2600, 2400), 500f);
            fr.Position = yonder; fr.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                fr.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0;
                fr.UseAbility("deploy", 0);
                fr.Position += Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(150f, 240f);
                await Wait(0.1);
            }
            Check(fr.TurretsOut == 3 && H.Deployed.Count(t => t.OwnerId == fr.OwnerId) == 3,
                  $"T drops a turret where you are, and a fourth is refused: three out ({fr.TurretsOut})");
            {   // ONE TICK A FRAME, not two. The deploy cooldown used to be run down by a line of
                // its own outside the loop that runs the slots down; the loop reaches every row
                // now, so that line is gone. Two ticks would spend the 6 s between drops in 3.
                fr.Sl("deploy").Cool = 6.0;
                double cool0 = fr.Sl("deploy").Cool, clock0 = fr.Clock;
                await Wait(1.0);
                double offCool = cool0 - fr.Sl("deploy").Cool, offClock = fr.Clock - clock0;
                Check(Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.FreightHauler)["deploy_cooldown"] - 6) < 1e-9
                      && Math.Abs(offCool - offClock) < 0.05,
                      $"the 6 s between drops counts down a second a second, not two ({offCool:0.00} s off it in {offClock:0.00} s of clock)");
                fr.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0;
            }
            var one = H.Deployed.First();
            // its gun is its OWNER's sheet, read live -- so the pilot's points and its gear reach
            // every turret it has out. (A bare freighter's 6 a shot is a literal where the table is proved.)
            Check(Math.Abs(one.Hp - fr.Stats["deploy_hull"]) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(one.Spec(true).Damage - fr.Stats["deploy_damage"]) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(one.Spec(true).Interval - fr.Stats["deploy_interval"]) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(one.Spec(true).Range - (float)fr.Stats["deploy_range"]) < 1e-3
                  && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.FreightHauler)["deploy_damage"] - 6) < 1e-9,
                  $"a dropped turret fires its owner's sheet: {one.Spec(true).Damage:0.0} a shot every {one.Spec(true).Interval:0.00} s inside {one.Spec(true).Range:0} u, on {one.Hp:0} hull");
            fr.Position = one.Position + new Vector2(90, 0); await Wait(0.15);
            fr.UseAbility("collect", 0); await Wait(0.15);
            Check(fr.TurretsOut == 2, $"C over one picks it up again ({fr.TurretsOut} left out)");
            fr.Position = yonder + new Vector2(0, -900); await Wait(0.15);       // clear of all three
            fr.UseAbility("collect", 0); await Wait(0.15);
            Check(fr.TurretsOut == 2 && fr.FailNote("collect") == "NOT OVER ONE", "and from 400 u away it refuses, saying why");
            // a raider walks into one: the turret shoots it, and the raider shoots back
            var tgt = H.Deployed.First();
            var standAt = VaryNear(tgt.Position, 420f);                          // inside its 500 u, any direction
            var rr = H.SpawnRaider(standAt); rr.Hp = 400;
            double rh0 = rr.Hp;
            // held where it appeared: this is a check on the GUN, not on how long a raider with
            // business elsewhere happens to stay in reach of it
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && rr.Hp == rh0; i++)
            { rr.Position = standAt; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(rr.Hp < rh0,
                  $"a turret left standing shoots what comes near it ({rh0 - rr.Hp:0.0} damage at {standAt.DistanceTo(tgt.Position):0} u, aiming at {(tgt.Aimed == null ? "nothing" : "it")})");
            rr.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            foreach (var dpl in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl);
            await Wait(0.1);

            fr.UseAbility("bubble", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(fr.BubbleUp && Math.Abs(fr.BubbleLeft - 400) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(fr.BubbleRadius - 260f) < 1e-3,
                  $"F raises a bubble of 400 over 260 u ({fr.BubbleLeft:0})");
            double hull0 = fr.Hp;
            fr.Hit(150, fr.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:bubble1");
            Check(Math.Abs(fr.Hp - hull0) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(fr.BubbleLeft - 250) < 1e-6,
                  $"a 150 blow comes out of the bubble, not the hull ({fr.BubbleLeft:0} left, hull {fr.Hp:0})");
            fr.Hit(300, fr.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:bubble2");
            Check(fr.BubbleLeft == 0 && Math.Abs(fr.Hp - (hull0 - 50)) < 1e-6,
                  $"and when the pool is spent the rest lands on the hull ({fr.Hp:0} of {hull0:0})");

            // ── A WRECK FIRES NOTHING, AND PRESSES NOTHING BUT REBOARD ──
            // Both halves are the HOST's rule, so both are poked the way a guest reaches them: the
            // trigger comes off the wire (a guest's 20 Hz report is built before the host's 10 Hz
            // word of its death arrives, so it re-armed a cleared trigger for a round trip, and a
            // client that never cleared it fired out of stasis for ever), and a press arrives at
            // DoAbility, where Refuse -- the owner's courtesy -- has no say.
            var doAbility = typeof(PlayerShip).GetMethod("DoAbility", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
            foreach (var dpl0 in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl0);
            await Wait(0.1);
            fr.Restore(0, false, 0);                       // a wreck, its stasis already run out
            fr.Trigger = true;                             // ...as the wire would set it
            bool wreckTrigger = fr.Trigger;
            fr.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0;
            doAbility.Invoke(fr, new object[] { "deploy", 0 });
            await Wait(0.15);
            int outWhileDead = fr.TurretsOut;
            doAbility.Invoke(fr, new object[] { "reboard", 0 });
            await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!wreckTrigger && outWhileDead == 0 && fr.Alive && Math.Abs(fr.Hp - fr.MaxHp * 0.33) < 0.5,
                  $"a wreck fires nothing and drops nothing: the wire's trigger reads {wreckTrigger} on it and a T from its own peer makes {outWhileDead} turrets -- and reboard, the one ability declared for a wreck, still climbs aboard at a third of the hull ({fr.Hp:0} of {fr.MaxHp:0})");
            fr.Restore(fr.MaxHp, true, 0); fr.Trigger = false;
            await Wait(0.1);

            // ── A WAVE LEAVES A MISSILE IN FLIGHT ALONE ──
            // The wave, the EMP and the echo read the raw hostile list, where an interceptable
            // seeker also lives. So they deleted missiles (counting each as a point-defence
            // intercept), and the wave THREW one its full push -- on the host only, while every
            // guest flew its own copy along the old path: two peers, one damaging missile, a
            // thousand units apart.
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightBastion);
            var bn = Me; bn.Position = yonder; bn.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            int took0 = Shot.Intercepted;
            Combat.LaunchTorpedo(bn.Position + new Vector2(0, -140), Vector2.Down, 40f, 900f, 5, bn.NetId, 0.4f,
                                 heavy: false, hostile: true);
            await Wait(0.1);
            var seeker = Nodes<Shot>().FirstOrDefault(s => s.HostileFire);
            var seekerWas = seeker?.Position ?? Vector2.Zero;
            bn.Sl("shockwave").Cool = 0; bn.UseAbility("shockwave", 0);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(seeker != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(seeker) && seeker.Position.DistanceTo(seekerWas) < 60f
                  && Shot.Intercepted == took0 && Math.Abs(bn.Stats["wave_push"] - 1000) < 1e-6,
                  $"a 1000 u shockwave does not throw a missile in flight, or count it as an intercept: it moved {seeker?.Position.DistanceTo(seekerWas):0} u of its own and {Shot.Intercepted - took0} were taken");
            if (seeker != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(seeker)) seeker.QueueFree();
            // The ring this raised must be gone before the effects block below counts what is up:
            // the checks between here and there are table reads, and pass no game time at all.
            await Wait(0.9);

            // ── TENDER: everything it owns fires twice as fast ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightTender);
            var td = Me; td.Position = yonder; td.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            td.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0; td.UseAbility("deploy", 0); await Wait(0.15);
            var tt = H.Deployed.First();
            double gun0 = td.Spec(false).Interval, drop0 = tt.Spec(true).Interval;
            td.UseAbility("overdrive", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Math.Abs(td.Spec(false).Interval - gun0 / 2) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(tt.Spec(true).Interval - drop0 / 2) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(td.FireRate - 2f) < 1e-6,
                  $"overdrive halves the reload of its gun AND of every turret it has out ({gun0:0.00} -> {td.Spec(false).Interval:0.00} s)");
            foreach (var dpl in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl);

            // ── EVERY EFFECT IS A ROW, AND EVERY PEER GETS IT ──
            // The shockwave, the EMP and the echo used to be drawn as spokes of LASER FLASHES,
            // because that was the only drawing that reached a guest, and the echo's burst was
            // added straight to the host's own tree where nobody else ever saw it.
            {
                // ── A CLAIMED LEVEL BUYS ONLY WHAT IT COULD HAVE PAID FOR ──
                // The host has no way to check a guest's level, so what a claim may SPEND is
                // capped. The affordability gate used to weigh the RAW wire level and the clamp
                // landed after it, on the stored field: a peer claiming two billion bought every
                // row maxed on the host's own copy of its ship -- +300 hull and four times the
                // damage on a level-1 pilot. And a claim it could not pay for was refused WHOLE,
                // so an honest pilot past the cap arrived anywhere as a stock hull.
                int Spent(int[] b) { int s = 0; for (int i = 0; i < b.Length; i++) for (int k = 0; k < b[i]; k++) s += Progression.Cost(k); return s; }
                var greedy = Progression.Afford(new[] { 60, 60, 60, 60 }, 2000000000);
                var honest = Progression.Afford(new[] { 0, 13, 0, 0 }, 92);          // 13 hull levels cost 1+2+...+13 = 91
                var past   = Progression.Afford(new[] { 0, 60, 0, 0 }, 100000);
                double greedyHull = Progression.Flats(greedy, ShipClass.Battleship).GetValueOrDefault("hull");
                Check(Progression.MaxSpendLevel == 100 && Spent(greedy) <= 99 && greedyHull <= 65
                      && Spent(honest) == 91 && honest[1] == 13
                      && Spent(past) <= 99 && past[1] == 13,
                      $"a claim buys only what its level could have paid for, and no more than 99 points on anyone's host: every row maxed at level two billion comes to {Spent(greedy)} points and +{greedyHull:0} hull, not +300; an honest 13 hull levels at level 92 survive whole; and a pilot past the cap keeps {past[1]} of its 60");

                // ── A PILOT'S WEAPONS POINTS REACH WHAT THE CLASS CALLS ITS WEAPONS ──
                // The damage upgrade used to name ONE stat for every class in the game -- torpedoes
                // for a carrier, main guns for everything else -- so a sniper's railgun, a warden's
                // hunters, a warrior's EMP and a freighter's deployed turrets took nothing from it.
                {
                    var keepBought = (int[])Character.Bought.Clone();
                    int[] two = new int[Progression.All.Length];
                    two[System.Array.FindIndex(Progression.All, u => u.Id == "damage")] = 2;   // two Weapons levels
                    // A SHARE, so two levels is 6% of whatever that weapon is on that hull -- the
                    // railgun's 150, the hunters' 45, the EMP's 60. The class still says WHICH
                    // stats; what changed is that the figure compounds instead of being a step.
                    double Rise(ShipClass c, string stat)
                        => new ShipStats(c, Progression.Shares(two, c))[stat] - new ShipStats(c)[stat];
                    double Six(ShipClass c, string stat) => new ShipStats(c)[stat] * 0.06;
                    Check(Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.HeavySniper, "rail_damage") - Six(ShipClass.HeavySniper, "rail_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.HeavyWarden, "hunter_damage") - Six(ShipClass.HeavyWarden, "hunter_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior, "emp_damage") - Six(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior, "emp_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.FreightHauler, "deploy_damage") - Six(ShipClass.FreightHauler, "deploy_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.LightEcho, "main_damage") - Six(ShipClass.LightEcho, "main_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.HeavySniper, "rail_damage") - 9) < 1e-9,
                          $"two Weapons levels are 6% of each weapon the class declares: the sniper's railgun +{Rise(ShipClass.HeavySniper, "rail_damage"):0.0} of its 150, the warden's hunters +{Rise(ShipClass.HeavyWarden, "hunter_damage"):0.0}, the warrior's EMP +{Rise(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior, "emp_damage"):0.0}, a dropped turret +{Rise(ShipClass.FreightHauler, "deploy_damage"):0.00}");
                    // ...and change NOTHING a saved pilot already had on the three it could reach
                    Check(Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.Battleship, "main_damage") - Six(ShipClass.Battleship, "main_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Rise(ShipClass.Battleship, "pd_damage") == 0
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.Carrier, "torpedo_damage") - Six(ShipClass.Carrier, "torpedo_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Rise(ShipClass.Carrier, "fighter_damage") == 0
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.Destroyer, "main_damage") - Six(ShipClass.Destroyer, "main_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Rise(ShipClass.Destroyer, "missile_damage") == 0,
                          $"and the three original hulls take it where they always did -- 6% of the gun or the torpedoes (+{Rise(ShipClass.Battleship, "main_damage"):0.0} on the battleship) -- and nothing at all on the fighters, the missiles or the point defence, whose rows are declared at zero and stay there");
                    // the gear that lifts "every weapon" reaches the same list, and nothing else
                    Check(string.Join(",", Classes.EveryDamageStat())
                          == "deploy_damage,emp_damage,fighter_damage,hunter_damage,main_damage,missile_damage,rail_damage,torpedo_damage"
                          && Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => c.Damage.Count > 0)
                          && Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => c.Damage.Keys.All(k => new ShipStats(c.Id)[k] > 0)),
                          $"eight weapon stats in the whole game, every flyable class names at least one, and every one it names is a row on its own sheet");

                    // ── AND THE K WINDOW'S DAMAGE BLOCK IS A ROW PER SYSTEM (ClassDef.Weapons) ──
                    // Its total added four named figures -- main, missile, broadside, point defence
                    // -- so the sniper read 7.50 DPS beside a railgun worth 37.50, and the two
                    // point-defence lines were drawn on the five classes that mount none.
                    var snL = new ShipStats(ShipClass.HeavySniper);
                    Check(Math.Abs(snL.SustainedDps - 45.0) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(snL.DamageLines.First(l => l.Label.StartsWith("Railgun")).Dps - 37.5) < 1e-6
                          && snL.DamageLines.Count() == 2 && !snL.DamageLines.Any(l => l.Label.Contains("Point defence")),
                          $"the sniper's total counts its railgun: the gun 6/0.8 = 7.5 and the rail 150/(3+1) = 37.5 -- 45 DPS, not 7.50, and no point-defence line on a hull that mounts none ({snL.SustainedDps:0.00})");
                    Check(Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.HeavyWarden).SustainedDps - (12 / 0.6 + 6 * 45 / 14.0 + 5 / 0.5)) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.FreightHauler).SustainedDps - 49.0) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior).SustainedDps - (2 * 9 / 0.7 + 60 / 18.0)) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.LightEcho).SustainedDps - (5 / 0.35 + (5 / 0.35) * 5 / 15.0)) < 1e-6,
                          $"...and every other signature weapon counts too: the warden 20 + 19.29 + 10, the freighter's gun 12 + three dropped turrets 36 + 1 = 49, the warrior's EMP 60 every 18 s, the echo repeating five seconds of its own damage every 15");
                    Check(Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier).SustainedDps - (3 * (2.5 / 0.35) * (15.0 / 18.0) + 4 * 2 * 137.5 / 27.5 + 3 * 0.5 / 0.5 * 0.5)) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier).DamageLines.First(l => l.Label.StartsWith("Bomber")).Dps - 40.0) < 1e-6,
                          $"the carrier's torpedoes are in its total at last: 8 x 137.5 = 1100 over a 27.5 s run (20 s out and back, 1.5 s launching, 6 s rearming) = 40 DPS, beside 17.86 from the fighters and 1.5 from the point defence ({new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier).SustainedDps:0.00})");
                    Check(Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => c.Weapons.Length > 0)
                          && Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => new ShipStats(c.Id).DamageLines.All(l => l.Dps > 0 && l.Sustained)),
                          "every flyable class declares what it kills with, and every line it declares is a rate above zero");

                    // ── AND NO BLURB WRITES A FIGURE ITS OWN ROWS CARRY ──
                    // "three volleys", "400 damage", "six hunter-seekers", "1.2 s": all of them are
                    // live rows that gear and pilot points move, so the first gear family written
                    // for one of those systems made the words beside it lie.
                    Check(!Classes.All.Any(c => c.Blurb.Any(char.IsDigit))
                          && !Classes.All.SelectMany(c => Abilities.For(c.Id)).Any(a => a.Blurb.Any(char.IsDigit)),
                          "no class or ability blurb writes a number: every figure is on the STATS tab, where gear and points move it");
                    // ── EVERY UPGRADE ROW SAYS WHAT IT MOVES ──
                    // What a row moved lived in a SECOND private table plus `if (id == "damage")`,
                    // so a row missing from that table was fully purchasable and moved nothing at
                    // all. One level of every row, on every flyable class, has to land on the
                    // sheet -- and the three that name their own stats move them by the figures
                    // the design asks for: a degree of rudder, 5 hull, 1 u/s.
                    {
                        (string stat, double per)[] Moved(string id)
                            => Progression.StatsFor(Progression.All.First(u => u.Id == id), ShipClass.Battleship).ToArray();
                        var silent = new List<string>();
                        int[] oneLevel = new int[Progression.All.Length];
                        for (int i = 0; i < Progression.All.Length; i++)
                        {
                            System.Array.Clear(oneLevel); oneLevel[i] = 1;
                            foreach (var cd in Classes.All.Where(x => x.Ready))
                            {
                                // BOTH SIDES: a row's figures are amounts (hull, rudder, engines)
                                // or shares (weapons, reach, cooling, gunnery), and a row that
                                // moves nothing on either side is a row that cannot be bought.
                                var moved = ShipStats.Sum(Progression.Flats(oneLevel, cd.Id),
                                                          Progression.Shares(oneLevel, cd.Id));
                                if (!Progression.StatsFor(Progression.All[i], cd.Id).Any() || moved.Values.All(v => v == 0))
                                    silent.Add($"{Progression.All[i].Id}/{cd.Id}");
                            }
                        }
                        // SEVEN ROWS: the four it opened with, and the owner's three -- reach,
                        // cooling and gunnery. Every one of them must move something on every
                        // flyable class, which is what makes them worth a point on any hull.
                        var reachOf = Moved("reach"); var coolOf = Moved("cool"); var rofOf = Moved("rof");
                        Check(Progression.All.Length == 7 && silent.Count == 0
                              && Moved("turn").Length == 1 && Moved("turn")[0].stat == "turn_rate"
                              && Math.Abs(Moved("turn")[0].per - Mathf.DegToRad(1f)) < 1e-9
                              && Moved("hull")[0] == ("hull", 5.0) && Moved("speed")[0] == ("max_speed", 1.0)
                              && reachOf.Any(t => t.stat == "main_range" && Math.Abs(t.per - 0.01) < 1e-9)
                              && Progression.All.Count(u => u.Share) == 4 && Progression.All.First(u => u.Id == "damage").Share
                              && coolOf.Length == 1 && coolOf[0].stat == "cooldown_share" && Math.Abs(coolOf[0].per + 0.005) < 1e-9
                              && rofOf.Any(t => t.stat == "main_interval" && Math.Abs(t.per + 0.005) < 1e-9),
                              $"all 7 upgrade rows move at least one stat on each of the {Classes.All.Count(x => x.Ready)} flyable classes -- a degree of rudder, 5 hull, 1 u/s, the weapons the class names, 1% of its reach, half a percent off its cooldowns and half a percent off its gun's cycle ({(silent.Count == 0 ? "none silent" : string.Join(" ", silent))})");
                    }
                    // ── WHAT A TARGET IS CALLED, AND WHAT COMING FOR IT IS WORTH ──
                    // Both were type tests in Hub: `Selected is TargetDummy td` for the HUD's
                    // target line and `quarry is Hauler h ? h.Payout : 0` to size an escort, so a
                    // second kind with a name or a load of its own meant a second arm on each.
                    // They are contracts on IHittable and IRaidTarget now, with the default that
                    // everything nameless and empty-handed takes.
                    Check(((IHittable)Me).Label == $"#{Me.NetId}"
                          && H.Yard.Gatherers.All(g => ((IRaidTarget)g).Payout == 0),
                          $"a target names itself and says what it is worth: this ship is {((IHittable)Me).Label}, and a miner is worth 0 credits to the raid that comes for it");
                    System.Array.Copy(keepBought, Character.Bought, keepBought.Length);
                }

                // ── EVERYTHING THAT FLIES IS ONE FLYER WITH A ROW ──
                // Three classes (Shell, Slug, Torpedo) with one copy each of the same sweep,
                // lifetime, cosmetic-copy rule and hit. Six rows and one node now, and ONE launch
                // on the wire where there were three.
                Check(Shots.All.Length == 6
                      && !Shots.Of(Shots.Shell).AtPlayers && Shots.Of(Shots.Shell).Sweep == 6f && Shots.Of(Shots.Shell).Burst == 0
                      && Shots.Of(Shots.Slug).AtPlayers && Shots.Of(Shots.Scrap).AtPlayers
                      && Shots.Of(Shots.Slug).Burst == 0.25 && Shots.Of(Shots.Scrap).Look == ShotLook.Shard
                      && Shots.Of(Shots.Missile).Heavy && !Shots.Of(Shots.Torpedo).Heavy
                      && Shots.Of(Shots.Seeker).Interceptable && !Shots.Of(Shots.Missile).Interceptable
                      && Shots.All.Count(x => x.Smoke) == 3,
                      "six kinds of shot, each a row: a shell, a boss's slug and its scrap, a torpedo, a heavy missile, and the seeker point defence exists for");
                // AND EVERY EFFECT AND EVERY WARNING IS ONE NODE WITH A ROW. Telegraph was a
                // second node for the same sentence -- a shape, at a place, for a time, with a
                // sound -- with a `bool Line` where this has a shape and an RPC of its own beside
                // the one that already carried effects. Two warning rows, a lane and a zone; how
                // long, how wide and what it sounds like are the MOVE's and come with the raise,
                // because one boss move plays its cue with no warning at all.
                var warnLane = Fx.Of(Fx.WarnLane); var warnZone = Fx.Of(Fx.WarnZone);
                Check(Fx.All.Length == 9 && warnLane.Warn && warnZone.Warn
                      && warnLane.Shape == FxShape.Lane && warnZone.Shape == FxShape.Zone
                      && warnLane.Sound == null && warnZone.Sound == null
                      && new[] { Fx.Burst, Fx.Lost, Fx.Rebuilt, Fx.Wave, Fx.Emp, Fx.Echo, Fx.Rail }.All(i => !Fx.Of(i).Warn)
                      && Fx.All.Count(x => x.Warn) == 2 && Fx.World == 0 && Fx.Flash == 0.35,
                      $"nine rows of Fx.All, seven effects and two warnings: a red lane and a red zone, whose clock and sounds are the raise's ({Fx.All.Length} rows, {Fx.All.Count(x => x.Warn)} warnings)");
                // the sweep the shell and the rock share: a step longer than a hull is how a shot
                // passes through it between frames
                {
                    int seen = 0;
                    bool struck = Shots.Sweep(Vector2.Zero, new Vector2(60, 0), 6f, _ => { seen++; return false; });
                    bool stops = Shots.Sweep(Vector2.Zero, new Vector2(60, 0), 6f, _ => true);
                    int once = 0;
                    Shots.Sweep(Vector2.Zero, new Vector2(600, 0), 0f, _ => { once++; return false; });
                    Check(seen == 10 && !struck && stops && once == 1,
                          $"Shots.Sweep walks a 60 u step in ten 6 u tests, stops at the first touch, and tests only the end point when a row asks for no sweep ({seen}, {once})");
                }

                Check(Fx.All.Length == 9 && Fx.Of(Fx.Wave).Shape == FxShape.Ring
                      && Fx.Of(Fx.Emp).Shape == FxShape.Spokes && Fx.Of(Fx.Echo).Shape == FxShape.Spokes
                      && Fx.Of(Fx.Rail).Shape == FxShape.Bar && Fx.Of(Fx.Burst).Shape == FxShape.Burst
                      && Fx.Of(Fx.Rebuilt).Tint != Fx.Of(Fx.Lost).Tint,
                      "the effects, each a row: a burst, a fleet craft lost and rebuilt, a wave, an EMP, an echo and a railgun's bar -- and the two warnings a boss draws, which were their own node until they became rows beside them");
                int fxBefore = Nodes<FxNode>().Count();
                Fx.Raise(Fx.Wave, fr.Position + new Vector2(0, -600), 400f);
                await Wait(0.1);
                var raised = Nodes<FxNode>().Where(f => f.Id == Fx.Wave).ToList();
                Check(raised.Count == 1 && Nodes<FxNode>().Count() == fxBefore + 1 && Math.Abs(raised[0].Radius - 400f) < 1e-3,
                      $"Fx.Raise puts one up in the live world, at the reach it was given ({raised.Count} up)");
                await Wait(1.0);
                Check(!Nodes<FxNode>().Any(f => f.Id == Fx.Wave), "and it takes itself away when its life is up");
            }

            // ── BASTION: everything within 1000 u thrown a thousand units clear ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightBastion);
            var bs = Me; bs.Position = yonder; bs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var near = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(300, 0)); near.Hp = 2000;
            var far = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(1600, 0)); far.Hp = 2000;
            await Wait(0.1);
            float nd0 = near.Position.DistanceTo(yonder), fd0 = far.Position.DistanceTo(yonder);
            bs.UseAbility("shockwave", 0); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float nd1 = near.Position.DistanceTo(yonder), fd1 = far.Position.DistanceTo(yonder);
            Check(Nodes<FxNode>().Any(f => f.Id == Fx.Wave), "the shockwave puts its ring up where it went off");
            Check(nd1 - nd0 > 950 && nd1 - nd0 < 1050 && Math.Abs(fd1 - fd0) < 60,
                  $"the shockwave throws what is within 1000 u a thousand units clear, and nothing beyond it ({nd1 - nd0:0} u / {fd1 - fd0:0} u)");
            near.TakeDamage(99999); far.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── SNIPER: 3 s locked in place, then 150 down a 2500 u line ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavySniper);
            var sn = Me; var dTgt = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            // One Weapons point on this hull is +1 on the gun AND +7.5 on the railgun. The L window
            // printed a single "next +1" for the pair, because it read the row's Per and not what
            // the purchase actually does (Progression.StatsFor).
            Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.15);
            var spw = Nodes<PilotWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            var sRow = spw == null ? null : Nodes<Label>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(spw).First(r => r.Name == "Pilot_damage")).First();
            // A SHARE READS AS A PERCENTAGE: +3% of each weapon the class names, where the window
            // used to advertise a step of +1 the purchase never actually gave.
            Check(sRow != null && sRow.Text.Contains("Damage per shot") && sRow.Text.Contains("+3% next")
                  && sRow.Text.Contains("Railgun · Damage") && !sRow.Text.Contains("+0.03"),
                  $"the sniper's Weapons row prints what a point really buys -- 3% of its gun AND 3% of its railgun, as a percentage rather than three hundredths of a damage ({sRow?.Text.Replace('\n', '|')})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.1);
            float railAt = Vary(900f, 2200f);                     // anywhere inside its 2500 u reach
            sn.Position = dTgt.Position + new Vector2(0, railAt); sn.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; sn.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            double dmg0 = dTgt.Total;
            sn.UseAbility("railgun", 0); await Wait(0.2);
            var wasAt = sn.Position;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.A); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.A);
            Check(sn.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled) && sn.Position.DistanceTo(wasAt) < 1f,
                  $"the railgun charges with the hull locked: W and A move it nowhere ({sn.Position.DistanceTo(wasAt):0.00} u)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && dTgt.Total == dmg0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(dTgt.Total - dmg0 - sn.Stats["rail_damage"]) < 0.01 && !sn.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled)
                  && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.HeavySniper)["rail_damage"] - 150) < 1e-9,
                  $"then its railgun's whole {sn.Stats["rail_damage"]:0.0} lands {railAt:0} u away, on the line, and the hull is free again ({dTgt.Total - dmg0:0.0})");

            // ── WARRIOR: two and a half times its speed, half the damage, then the EMP ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior);
            var wr = Me; wr.Position = yonder; wr.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var e1 = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(yonder, 200f)); e1.Hp = 500;        // inside the EMP's 260 u
            var e2 = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(800f, 1200f)); e2.Hp = 500;   // well outside its 260 u
            var e2Home = e2.Position;
            await Wait(0.1);
            double top0 = wr.Stats["max_speed"];
            wr.UseAbility("rush", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            double wh0 = wr.Hp;
            wr.Hit(100, wr.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:rush");
            Check(wr.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && Math.Abs(wh0 - wr.Hp - 50) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(wr.SpeedMult - 2.5f) < 1e-6,
                  $"the rush: 2.5x top speed ({top0 * wr.SpeedMult:0} u/s) and a 100 blow lands for {wh0 - wr.Hp:0}");
            double e10 = e1.Hp, e20 = e2.Hp;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && e1.Hp == e10; i++) { e2.Position = e2Home; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(Math.Abs(e10 - e1.Hp - wr.Stats["emp_damage"]) < 0.01 && e1.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled) && e2.Hp == e20
                  && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior)["emp_damage"] - 60) < 1e-9,
                  $"and it ends in an EMP: 60 to what is within 260 u ({e1.Position.DistanceTo(wr.Position):0} u) and it is held; nothing at {e2.Position.DistanceTo(wr.Position):0} u ({e10 - e1.Hp:0})");
            e1.TakeDamage(99999); e2.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── WARDEN: point defence with no window, and six hunters on six targets ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavyWarden);
            var wd = Me; wd.Position = yonder; wd.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            Check(wd.PdOnline && !wd.PdActive && wd.PdRing == 0f && Math.Abs(wd.Spec(true).Damage - 5.0) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(wd.Stats["pd_interval"] - 0.5) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(wd.Stats.PdDuty - 1) < 1e-9,
                  $"the warden's point defence is on with nothing pressed -- 5 a shot every 0.5 s, on all of the time ({wd.Stats.PdSustainedDps:0.0} DPS sustained)");
            // and it KILLS: one light raider inside its 420 u, nothing else firing, dead in seconds.
            // (At the 0.25 a shot it was first written with, this took 50 s and the class's whole
            // reason for existing did nothing a pilot could see.)
            {
                var pdMark = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(wd.Position, 380f));       // inside 420 u, any bearing
                double pdStart = wd.Clock;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && pdMark.Alive; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(!pdMark.Alive && wd.Clock - pdStart < 6,
                      $"...and it kills a light raider on its own in {wd.Clock - pdStart:0.0} s, with nothing pressed");
            }
            var marks = new List<Raider>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { var m = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(300 + 60 * i, -200 + 90 * i)); m.Hp = 3000; marks.Add(m); }
            await Wait(0.2);
            int torp0 = Missiles().Count();
            wd.UseAbility("hunters", 0); await Wait(0.2);
            var fired = Missiles().Where(t => !t.HostileFire && t.TargetId != 0).ToList();
            Check(fired.Count >= 6 - torp0 && fired.Select(t => t.TargetId).Distinct().Count() >= 4,
                  $"six hunter-seekers away, each on a target of its own ({fired.Count} out, {fired.Select(t => t.TargetId).Distinct().Count()} targets)");
            foreach (var m in marks) m.TakeDamage(99999);
            await Wait(0.3);

            // ── DART: untouchable through the roll, then faster and firing quicker ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.LightDart);
            var dt2 = Me; dt2.Position = yonder; dt2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            double dh0 = dt2.Hp, rof0 = dt2.Spec(false).Interval;
            dt2.UseAbility("roll", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            dt2.Hit(80, dt2.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:roll");
            Check(dt2.Statuses.Has(Status.Evading) && Math.Abs(dt2.Hp - dh0) < 1e-6,
                  "through the barrel roll nothing touches it at all");
            await Wait(1.4);
            Check(!dt2.Statuses.Has(Status.Evading) && Math.Abs(dt2.SpeedMult - 1.6f) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(dt2.Spec(false).Interval - rof0 / 1.25) < 1e-9,
                  $"and it comes out of it 60% faster, firing a quarter quicker ({rof0:0.000} -> {dt2.Spec(false).Interval:0.000} s)");
            {   // ...and BOTH multipliers are the rows', not two `if`s naming slot ids by string:
                // with EVERY one of the dart's slots running at once, the only two stats that
                // move them are the roll's own -- 1.25x the rate of fire and 1.6x the speed.
                foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(ShipClass.LightDart)) dt2.Sl(ab.Id).Left = 5.0;
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(!dt2.Statuses.Has(Status.Evading) && Math.Abs(dt2.FireRate - 1.25f) < 1e-6
                      && Math.Abs(dt2.SpeedMult - 1.6f) < 1e-6,
                      $"with every one of its slots running, only the roll's own two stats move them ({dt2.FireRate:0.00}x rate, {dt2.SpeedMult:0.00}x speed)");
                foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(ShipClass.LightDart)) dt2.Sl(ab.Id).Left = 0;
            }

            // ── ECHO: what it deals for five seconds, all at once at the end ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.LightEcho);
            var ec = Me; ec.Position = yonder; ec.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var mark = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(150, 0)); mark.Hp = 4000;
            var near2 = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(260, 0)); near2.Hp = 4000;
            await Wait(0.1);
            ec.UseAbility("echo", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            ec.NoteDealt(90, mark.Position);                       // as a shell of its own would
            Check(Math.Abs(ec.Sl("echo").Own - 90) < 1e-6, $"the echo remembers what it deals ({ec.Sl("echo").Own:0})");
            double m0 = mark.Hp, n0 = near2.Hp;
            var markHome = mark.Position; var nearHome = near2.Position;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && ec.Sl("echo").Left > 0; i++)
            { mark.Position = markHome; near2.Position = nearHome; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Math.Abs(m0 - mark.Hp - 90) < 0.01 && Math.Abs(n0 - near2.Hp - 90) < 0.01,
                  $"and puts all of it down where the last of it landed, on everything within 220 u ({m0 - mark.Hp:0})");
            mark.TakeDamage(99999); near2.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── WRAITH: five seconds nothing hostile can pick it ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.LightWraith);
            var wt = Me; wt.Position = yonder; wt.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var hunter = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(yonder, 500f)); hunter.Hp = 4000;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && hunter.Target != wt; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(hunter.Target == wt, "a raider comes for the wraith");
            wt.UseAbility("stealth", 0);
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && hunter.Target == wt; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(wt.Statuses.Has(Status.Untargetable) && hunter.Target != wt && Targeting.Hidden(wt),
                  "and the moment it goes dark the raider lets go of it");
            await Wait(5.2);
            Check(!wt.Statuses.Has(Status.Untargetable), "five seconds later it is visible again");
            // AND THE HUNT COMES BACK. A raider re-decides only when its target stops being valid,
            // so the miner it took while the wraith was dark stayed valid for ever: one press of
            // stealth used to hand the whole wave to the base's fleet permanently.
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && hunter.Target != wt; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(hunter.Target == wt,
                  $"...and the raider takes the wraith back the moment it can see it again (now after {(hunter.Target == wt ? "it" : Raider.Up(hunter.Target) ? "something else" : "nothing")})");
            hunter.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── EVERY TIMED ROW TICKS, WHATEVER CLASS CARRIES IT ────────────────────
            // A timed ability was a row everywhere except the tick: PlayerShip kept a string[]
            // of the nine ids that had timers, so a tenth added as a row compiled, bound, drew
            // on the bar and its Left never moved, with nothing to say so. Put 5 s of Left and
            // 5 s of Cool on EVERY slot of every flyable class and watch all of them fall. This
            // FAILS the moment one row is dropped from the tick again.
            {
                var stuck = new List<string>();
                int rows = 0;
                foreach (var cd in Classes.All.Where(x => x.Ready).ToList())
                {
                    await SetClass(cd.Id);
                    var tk = Me; tk.Position = yonder; tk.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(cd.Id)) { tk.Sl(ab.Id).Left = 5.0; tk.Sl(ab.Id).Cool = 5.0; rows++; }
                    await Wait(0.5);
                    foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(cd.Id))
                        if (tk.Sl(ab.Id).Left > 4.7 || tk.Sl(ab.Id).Cool > 4.7) stuck.Add($"{cd.Name}.{ab.Id}");
                    foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(cd.Id)) { tk.Sl(ab.Id).Left = 0; tk.Sl(ab.Id).Cool = 0; }
                }
                Check(stuck.Count == 0 && rows >= 12 * 6,
                      $"every ability row of every class runs its own timers down: 5 s put on all {rows} of them, none above 4.7 s half a second later ({(stuck.Count == 0 ? "none stuck" : string.Join(", ", stuck))})");
            }

            await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);

            {   // ── A STATUS'S SHARE IS A ROW, AND ITS GAP IS SWEPT ─────────────────
                // Guarded read Stats["rush_guard"] -- a row only the warrior carries -- out of
                // the one path every class's damage goes through, so Hardened on any other hull
                // was a status that did nothing at all.
                var gd = Me; gd.Position = yonder; gd.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.3);
                Check(gd.Stats["rush_guard"] == 0, "a battleship's sheet has no rush_guard row of its own at all");

                // The per-source gap kept one entry per attacker that had EVER touched the hull,
                // for the life of the world. Forty in one frame, and none of them a second later.
                var lastHitBy = (Dictionary<string, double>)typeof(PlayerShip)
                    .GetField("_lastHitBy", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(gd);
                for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) gd.Hit(1, gd.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), $"test:swept{i}");
                int keptGap = lastHitBy.Count, tallied = gd.DamageBySource.Count;
                await Wait(1.2);                                       // well past the 0.52 s gap
                int swept = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) if (!lastHitBy.ContainsKey($"test:swept{i}")) swept++;
                Check(keptGap >= 40 && swept == 40 && tallied >= 40 && gd.DamageBySource.Count >= 40,
                      $"forty sources in one frame leave forty entries in the 0.52 s gap and none of them 1.2 s later ({keptGap} held, {swept} of 40 swept), and the tally the HUD reads still has all forty ({gd.DamageBySource.Count})");
                double gap0 = gd.Hp;
                gd.Hit(10, gd.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:gap");
                gd.Hit(10, gd.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:gap");
                Check(Math.Abs(gap0 - gd.Hp - 10) < 1e-6,
                      $"...and the gap itself still holds: the same source twice inside 0.52 s lands once, for 10 ({gap0 - gd.Hp:0})");

                // HARDENED on a hull with no row of its own: half of it, which is what Hardened
                // has always meant. (The warrior's own rush_guard row is 0.5, checked above.)
                gd.ApplyStatus(Status.Hardened, 0.4); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                double hd0 = gd.Hp;
                gd.Hit(100, gd.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:hardrow");
                Check(gd.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && Math.Abs(hd0 - gd.Hp - 50) < 1e-6,
                      $"a 100 blow on a HARDENED battleship -- a class with no rush_guard row -- lands for 50 ({hd0 - gd.Hp:0})");
                await Wait(0.6);
                // and EVADING, the same table's other row, on a hull that cannot roll
                gd.ApplyStatus(Status.Evading, 0.4); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                double ev0 = gd.Hp;
                gd.Hit(100, gd.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:evadrow");
                Check(!gd.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && Math.Abs(gd.Hp - ev0) < 1e-6,
                      $"and an EVADING one takes none of that 100 at all ({ev0 - gd.Hp:0})");
                await Wait(0.6);
            }

            {   // ── FIVE STATUSES ON THE WIRE, AND ALL FIVE COME BACK ───────────────
                // Shielded was a row nothing set, read or implemented, and it still rode the
                // wire in StatusSet.Bits. The values that remain ARE the wire format: 1 2 4 8 16.
                var put = new StatusSet();
                put.Apply(Status.Pinned, 1); put.Apply(Status.Disabled, 1); put.Apply(Status.Untargetable, 1);
                put.Apply(Status.Hardened, 1); put.Apply(Status.Evading, 1);
                int bits = put.Bits;
                var got = new StatusSet(); got.FromBits(bits);
                Check(bits == 31 && System.Enum.GetValues(typeof(Status)).Length == 6
                      && (int)Status.Pinned == 1 && (int)Status.Disabled == 2 && (int)Status.Untargetable == 4
                      && (int)Status.Hardened == 8 && (int)Status.Evading == 16
                      && got.Has(Status.Pinned) && got.Has(Status.Disabled) && got.Has(Status.Untargetable)
                      && got.Has(Status.Hardened) && got.Has(Status.Evading),
                      $"five statuses and no sixth: 1 2 4 8 16 pack into {bits} and all five come back out of it");
            }
        }

        // ── buildings open menus; READY -> autopilot -> portal -> ARENA -> home ──
        // (Late in the run on purpose: the trip replaces the whole hub scene, so every
        //  node reference taken before it -- dummies, ships -- is stale afterwards.)
        {
            var tioHome = Me.Position;
            Me.Position = Hub.TioPos + new Vector2(260, 0); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.5);
            Click(Scr(Hub.TioPos)); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.TioOpen && Nodes<TioWindow>().Any(), "left-click on the TIO opens WARP TO TARGET");
            var tw = Nodes<TioWindow>().First();
            Check(!Nodes<Button>(tw).Any(b => b.Name == "Warp"), "no WARP button: READY drives the mission");
            H.Yard.Credits = 1000;
            // start well away from where the portal opens, so the autopilot has a real trip
            Me.Position = H.MissionPortalPos + new Vector2(300, 700); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.3);
            Nodes<Button>(tw).First(b => b.Name == "Ready").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.AllReady && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.Opening && Me.AutopilotTo == H.MissionPortalPos,
                  "READY: the party (just me) is ready, the portal starts opening, and my ship flies itself there");
            await Wait(Hub.PortalOpenTime + 0.2);
            var mp = H.GetNodeOrNull<Node2D>("MissionPortal");
            var tio = H.GetNode<Sprite2D>("TIO"); float halfW = tio.GetRect().Size.X * tio.Scale.X / 2f;
            Check(H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen && mp != null && mp.Position.X > Hub.TioPos.X + halfW && mp.Position.Y < Hub.TioPos.Y - Hub.TioHeight / 2f,
                  "after 3 s the portal opens off the TIO's top-right corner");
            // THE MARK THAT WAS PICKABLE AND INVISIBLE: the open portal is one row of the scope
            // table now, so the glyph and the pick are the same row or neither exists.
            var mpMark = H.ScopeMarks().Where(smk => smk.Name == "MISSION PORTAL").ToList();
            Check(mpMark.Count == 1 && mpMark[0].At.DistanceTo(H.MissionPortalPos) < 0.01f && mpMark[0].Pick > 0f
                  && mpMark[0].Size.X > 0f && mpMark[0].Colour.A > 0f,
                  $"the open mission portal is 1 scope mark with a glyph of its own ({mpMark.Count} row, pick {(mpMark.Count == 1 ? mpMark[0].Pick : -1):0} u)");
            float d0 = Me.Position.DistanceTo(H.MissionPortalPos);
            // The base as it LEAVES, marked: the only thing that can put this figure on disk is the
            // save on the way out (the 30 s autosave is held off). That save used to write nothing --
            // the sector had already become the arena -- and a quit out there lost the difference.
            typeof(Yard).GetField("_saveCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H.Yard, 1e9);
            // ...and a hauler with a load waiting on its pad: the trip must take it home, not lose it
            H.Yard.Hauler.ResetToPad(); H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo = 300;
            double heldAtLeaving = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 45 && !Hub.InArena; i++)
            {
                if (H?.Yard != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Yard))
                {
                    H.Yard.Credits = 424242;
                    heldAtLeaving = H.Yard.Stock("ore") + H.Yard.Stock("salvage") + H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo + H.Yard.Gatherers.Sum(g => g.Cargo);
                }
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var tripRec = typeof(Yard).GetField("_trip", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static).GetValue(null);
            // The trip's stock is one figure per resource row now, not two named fields.
            var tripBag = tripRec?.GetType().GetProperty("Stock").GetValue(tripRec) as System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, double>;
            double tripStock = tripBag == null ? -1 : Gathering.Resources.Sum(r => tripBag.GetValueOrDefault(r));
            Check(Math.Abs(tripStock - heldAtLeaving) < 1.0, $"leaving for the arena, the loads come home first: the base set aside holds the hauler's 300 too ({tripStock:0} of {heldAtLeaving:0})");
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Hub.InArena, $"the capital-ship autopilot flew {d0:0} u to the portal and the party went through");
            var leftWith = new ConfigFile(); leftWith.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
            Check(Math.Abs((double)leftWith.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - 424242) < 1e-6,
                  $"leaving for the arena writes the base to disk on the way out ({(double)leftWith.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0} of 424242)");
            var boss = H.Boss;
            Check(boss != null && boss.Alive && boss.Type.Id == "silver_lancer" && boss.Type.Name == "SILVER LANCER" && H.Yard == null && !Nodes<TargetDummy>().Any() && Combat.Hostiles.Contains(boss),
                  "the arena at level 1: the Silver Lancer, and no base or dummies");
            // THE LEVEL'S OWN COPY of every move. At level 1 the scale is exactly 1, so the boss
            // flies the build's rows unaltered -- which is also how this proves the copy is made
            // from them rather than instead of them.
            {
                var flown = boss.Type.Moves.First(m => m.Windup > 0 && m.Reach > 0);
                var slot = boss.S(flown.Id);
                double q1 = Missions.Quicken(Missions.Level);
                Check(Math.Abs(q1 - 1.0) < 1e-9 && slot != null
                      && Math.Abs(slot.M.Windup - flown.Windup / q1) < 1e-6
                      && Math.Abs(slot.M.Reach - flown.Reach * q1) < 0.01
                      && Math.Abs(slot.M.Live - flown.Live) < 1e-9,
                      $"the boss flies the level's own copy of its rows: at level {Missions.Level} the scale is x{q1:0.000}, so its {flown.Id} winds up in {slot?.M.Windup:0.00} s and reaches {slot?.M.Reach:0} u -- its row's own figures");
            }
            // ITS MOVES ARE ROWS, so what follows reads the SLOT a row drives, by that row's id.
            // A beam's phases: Opening -- its escorts are out; Winding -- the red line is charging;
            // Firing -- it is burning down that line. These were five private fields and a
            // reflection handle each.
            Boss.Slot BeamSlot() => boss.S("beam");
            bool EscortsOut() => BeamSlot().At == Boss.Phase.Opening;
            bool BeamCharging() => BeamSlot().At == Boss.Phase.Winding;
            bool BeamLive() => BeamSlot().At == Boss.Phase.Firing;
            bool Ramming() => boss.S("ram").At == Boss.Phase.Firing;
            // -- THE MOVES ARE A TABLE, AND THESE ARE ITS FIGURES --------------------------
            // Every number the two bosses fight with, against the literals of the design rather
            // than against the code's own constants. The behaviour checks below read the rows;
            // this is the one place that says what a row must SAY.
            {
                var lan = Missions.Bosses[0]; var dra = Missions.Bosses[1];
                BossMove L(string id) => lan.Moves.First(m => m.Id == id);
                BossMove D(string id) => dra.Moves.First(m => m.Id == id);
                Check(lan.Moves.Select(m => m.Id).SequenceEqual(new[] { "guns", "trident", "beam", "ram", "wave" })
                      && dra.Moves.Select(m => m.Id).SequenceEqual(new[] { "gun", "shotgun", "throw" }),
                      $"a boss's moves are a table of rows, in the order a frame resolves them ({string.Join(",", lan.Moves.Select(m => m.Id))} / {string.Join(",", dra.Moves.Select(m => m.Id))})");
                // its art, its shape and what it closes on are the row's too, as EnemyDef carries
                // a raider's -- they were abstract members of a boss class each
                Check(lan.Hull == 760 && lan.Length == 360f && lan.HalfWidth == 70f && lan.Sprite == "res://boss_raider.png"
                      && dra.Hull == 700 && dra.Length == 420f && dra.HalfWidth == 90f && dra.Sprite == "res://boss_drake.png"
                      && lan.HoldOff == 650f && lan.CloseSpeed == 30f && Math.Abs(lan.TurnRate - 0.3f) < 1e-6
                      && dra.HoldOff == 650f && dra.CloseSpeed == 30f && Math.Abs(dra.TurnRate - 0.3f) < 1e-6,
                      "a boss's hull, art and approach are its row's: 760 / 360 / 70 and 700 / 420 / 90, both closing to 650 u at 30 u/s and turning at 0.3 rad/s");
                var gn = L("guns");
                Check(gn.Way == MoveWay.Bolt && gn.Every == 1.2 && gn.First == 2.0 && gn.Damage == 3.6 && gn.Find == 900f
                      && gn.Waits == MoveWait.Nothing && !gn.Busy && !gn.Super && gn.Source == "boss:guns"
                      && Math.Abs(gn.Damage / gn.Every - 3) < 1e-9,
                      $"the guns: {gn.Damage} every {gn.Every} s (3 DPS) at the nearest ship within 900 u, and they wait on nothing");
                var tr = L("trident");
                Check(tr.Way == MoveWay.Shoot && tr.Shot == Shots.Seeker && tr.Every == 15 && tr.First == 13.5
                      && tr.Count == 3 && tr.Spread == 25f && tr.Damage == 15 && tr.Speed == 135f && tr.Range == 1920f
                      && tr.Turn == 1.4f && tr.Size == 2f && tr.Muzzle == 14f && tr.Cue == "boss_trident"
                      && tr.Waits == MoveWait.Nothing && !tr.Busy && tr.Source == "boss:missiles",
                      $"the trident: {tr.Count} seekers at -25 / 0 / +25 degrees, 15 each, 135 u/s out to 1920 u, twice the size");
                var bm = L("beam");
                Check(bm.Way == MoveWay.Beam && bm.Every == 30 && bm.First == 6.0 && bm.Windup == 6.0 && bm.Live == 3.0
                      && bm.Tick == 0.25 && bm.Damage == 50 && bm.Reach == 10000f && bm.Width == 70f
                      && bm.Busy && bm.Super && bm.Waits == MoveWait.Itself && bm.Source == "boss:beam"
                      && bm.Cue == "boss_beam_charge" && bm.Strike == "boss_beam"
                      && bm.Escorts == 2 && bm.EscortKind == Enemies.Webifier && bm.EscortAngle == 45f
                      && bm.EscortOut == 130f && bm.EscortHull == 3 && bm.WebGrace == 1.0 && bm.WebSlack == 1.0
                      && bm.ArmMax == 5.0 && bm.ArmMax < bm.Windup,
                      $"the beam: wind-up {bm.Windup} s, live {bm.Live} s judged every {bm.Tick} s for {bm.Damage}, 70 u wide down 10000 u; two escorts 45 degrees off at 3 hull, never stalling it past 5 s");
                var rm = L("ram");
                Check(rm.Way == MoveWay.Dash && rm.Every == 30 && rm.First == 21.0 && rm.Windup == 1.5
                      && rm.Damage == 40 && rm.Reach == 900f && rm.Speed == 1200f && rm.Width == 140f
                      && rm.Busy && rm.Super && rm.Shifts && rm.Waits == MoveWait.Everything && rm.Source == "boss:charge",
                      $"the ram: wind-up {rm.Windup} s, {rm.Reach} u at {rm.Speed} u/s, {rm.Damage} on contact -- and a cadence of its OWN ({rm.Every} s, where it read the beam's constant by accident)");
                var wv = L("wave");
                Check(wv.Way == MoveWay.Ring && wv.Every == 17.0 && wv.First == 10.0 && wv.Windup == 1.8
                      && wv.Damage == 45 && wv.Reach == 340f && wv.Busy && !wv.Super && !wv.Shifts
                      && wv.Waits == MoveWait.Movers && wv.Source == "boss:wave" && wv.Strike == "boss_shockwave",
                      $"the shockwave: every {wv.Every} s, wind-up {wv.Windup} s, {wv.Damage} within {wv.Reach} u -- the period and the damage were bare literals in a method body");
                var dg = D("gun"); var sg = D("shotgun"); var th = D("throw");
                Check(dg.Way == MoveWay.Shoot && dg.Shot == Shots.Slug && dg.Every == 2.5 && dg.First == 2.0
                      && dg.Find == 1100f && dg.Damage == 6 && dg.Speed == 220f && dg.Range == 1300f
                      && dg.Radius == 7f && dg.Waits == MoveWait.Nothing && !dg.Busy && dg.Source == "boss:gun",
                      $"the Drake's main gun: {dg.Damage} a shell (2.4 DPS) every {dg.Every} s at 220 u/s within 1100 u, through its own specials");
                Check(sg.Way == MoveWay.Shoot && sg.Shot == Shots.Scrap && sg.Every == 15 && sg.First == 17.0
                      && sg.Warp == 1.0 && sg.Standoff == 600f && sg.WarpRing == 117f && sg.WarpSound == "drake_warp"
                      && sg.Windup == 1.875 && sg.Count == 7 && sg.Spread == 10f && sg.Width == 28f
                      && sg.Damage == 18.75 && sg.Speed == 480f && sg.Range == 1400f && sg.Radius == 9f
                      && sg.Busy && sg.Super && sg.Shifts && sg.Waits == MoveWait.Everything && sg.Source == "boss:scrap",
                      $"the scrap shotgun: a {sg.Warp} s ring, a warp to {sg.Standoff} u off, {sg.Windup} s of fan, then {sg.Count} pieces of {sg.Damage} at {sg.Spread} degrees apart");
                Check(th.Way == MoveWay.Throw && th.Every == 30 && th.First == 6.0 && th.Windup == 7.5
                      && th.Flight == 1.6 && th.Damage == 250 && th.Reach == 1800f && th.Width == 180f
                      && th.Radius == 90f && th.Offset == 220f && th.Busy && th.Super
                      && th.Waits == MoveWait.Everything && th.Cue == "drake_tractor" && th.Strike == "drake_throw",
                      $"the asteroid throw: a {th.Radius} u body {th.Offset} u off the flank, a {th.Windup} s lane {th.Reach} u long, {th.Flight} s of flight, {th.Damage} to a ship in it");
                // ...AND A BOSS'S MOVES COME FROM ITS ROW, NOT FROM ITS CLASS. There is one Boss
                // class now: given the Lancer's hull with the DRAKE'S Moves, the boss built from
                // that row schedules the Drake's moves and knows nothing of the Lancer's.
                var mixed = new Missions.BossType { Id = "mixed", Name = "MIXED", Hull = 100, Sprite = lan.Sprite,
                                                    Length = lan.Length, HalfWidth = lan.HalfWidth, Moves = dra.Moves };
                var proof = new Boss { Hub = H, Type = mixed, Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, -6000) };
                AddChild(proof);                                  // _Ready builds its slots from the row
                Check(boss.GetType() == typeof(Boss) && proof.GetType() == typeof(Boss)
                      && proof.S("beam") == null && proof.S("ram") == null && proof.S("throw") != null
                      && proof.S("throw").Due == 6.0 && proof.S("shotgun").Due == 17.0
                      && proof.Length == 360f && Math.Abs(proof.MaxHp - 100) < 1e-6,
                      $"a boss's moves come from its ROW, not its class: one Boss class, and the Lancer's hull handed the Drake's rows runs the Drake's moves ({string.Join(",", proof.Type.Moves.Select(m => m.Id))})");
                proof.QueueFree();
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            Check(Met("boss"), "THE BOSS: met on entering the arena");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
            var bb = Nodes<Button>().FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "BaseButton");
            Check(bb != null && !bb.IsVisibleInTree(), "in the arena the BASE button is hidden");
            bb?.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);                 // and even pressed, there is nothing to open
            Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0, "neither B nor the button opens a base menu out there");
            Check(Music.CombatZone, "the arena counts as combat for the music");
            Me.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; Me.Rotation = 0;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(0.1);                           // point defence up
            Check(Math.Abs(boss.MaxHp - 760) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(boss.S("guns").M.Damage / boss.S("guns").M.Every - 3) < 1e-9, "the boss: 760 hull at level 1 (solo); its guns 3 DPS");
            // stand in the beam on purpose with a thick hull (test only), and watch the rhythm
            // the boss's health bar: a chunk per hit, bouncing, whitening, fading; bursts merge
            var bar = Nodes<QuarryBar>().First();
            Check(bar.Visible, "in the arena the boss's health bar shows");
            double hpA = boss.Hp;
            boss.TakeDamage(60); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var ck = bar.Chunks.LastOrDefault();
            Check(ck != null && Math.Abs(ck.To - hpA / boss.MaxHp) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(ck.From - (hpA - 60) / boss.MaxHp) < 1e-6,
                  "a hit leaves a chunk: exactly the slice of the bar it took");
            await Wait(0.06);
            float off = QuarryBar.Offset(ck.T), wh = QuarryBar.Whiteness(ck.T);
            Check(Math.Abs(off) > 1f && wh > 0f && wh < 1f, $"the chunk bobs ({off:0.0} px) and is turning white ({wh:0.00})");
            Check(QuarryBar.Offset(QuarryBar.BounceFor) == 0 && QuarryBar.Alpha(QuarryBar.Life) <= 0.001 && QuarryBar.Whiteness(QuarryBar.Life * 0.5) >= 0.999,
                  "the bounce dies away; white by half-life; gone by the end");
            await Wait(QuarryBar.Life + 0.1);
            Check(bar.Chunks.Count == 0, "and then it is gone from the bar");
            // one small hit a frame for three frames (50 ms): inside the 60 ms window, so one chunk
            // (the bar reads the hull once a frame, so hits within ONE frame could never test this)
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) { boss.TakeDamage(1); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(bar.Chunks.Count == 1, $"a burst of small hits, frame after frame, shares one chunk ({bar.Chunks.Count})");
            var me2 = Me; me2.MaxHp = 5000; me2.TakeDamage(-(5000 - me2.Hp));
            double tBeam = -1, tCharge = -1, tVolley = -1, arenaT = 0; int trident = 0; float spread = 0;
            // THE LIVE BEAM, 3 s: it must hold the line it drew, and that line must still be drawn.
            // The boss used to turn after the pilot the whole time, with the telegraph already gone
            // -- an invisible line sweeping the arena. 2.2 s in, the pilot is moved clear to the
            // side (after five 50-hull ticks, which the damage check below counts on): a boss that
            // turns would now swing after it.
            double liveT = 0; float liveRot0 = 0, liveTurn = 0; bool liveDrawn = false, sidestep = false;
            async void Watch()
            {
                while (Hub.InArena && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(boss))
                {
                    arenaT += 1.0 / 60;
                    if (BeamLive())
                    {
                        if (liveT == 0) liveRot0 = boss.Rotation;
                        liveT += 1.0 / 60;
                        liveTurn = Math.Max(liveTurn, Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(liveRot0, boss.Rotation)));
                        if (liveT > 2.0 && Nodes<FxNode>(boss).Any(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane)) liveDrawn = true;
                        if (liveT > 2.2 && !sidestep) { sidestep = true; me2.Position += Vector2.Right.Rotated(boss.Rotation) * 400f; }
                    }
                    // The beam's place in the rhythm is where it COMMITS -- its escorts going out.
                    // Its red line comes later, when their web pins the pilot, so timing the rhythm
                    // off the telegraph would measure that wait, not the rhythm.
                    if (tBeam < 0 && H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort)) tBeam = arenaT;
                    foreach (var tl in Nodes<FxNode>().Where(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane))
                        if (tCharge < 0 && Math.Abs(tl.Radius - 140f) < 0.1f) tCharge = arenaT;   // the ram's lane, 140 u
                    if (tVolley < 0 && boss.S("trident").Fired > 0)
                    {
                        tVolley = arenaT;
                        var big = Missiles().Where(x => x.HostileFire && x.Size == 2f).ToList();
                        trident = big.Count;
                        if (big.Count == 3) spread = big.Max(x => x.Dir.Angle()) - big.Min(x => x.Dir.Angle());
                    }
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                }
            }
            Watch();
            // The death beam OPENS WITH ITS ESCORTS, well before any red line: two fragile lights
            // 45 degrees to port and starboard of the nose, shivering on the spot while they come
            // round onto the pilot. The beam starts charging once their web has pinned the pilot.
            var escorts = new System.Collections.Generic.List<Raider>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && escorts.Count < 2; i++) { escorts = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            var bnose = Vector2.Up.Rotated(boss.Rotation);
            var escAng = escorts.Select(r => Mathf.RadToDeg(bnose.AngleTo(r.Position - boss.Position))).OrderBy(x => x).ToList();
            Check(escorts.Count == 2 && Math.Abs(escAng[0] + 45) < 12 && Math.Abs(escAng[1] - 45) < 12
                  && escorts.All(r => r.Target == me2 && r.Hp > 0 && r.Hp <= 3 * Missions.S(Missions.Level) + 1e-6),
                  $"two escorts launch at the pilot, 45 degrees to port and starboard ({string.Join(", ", escAng.Select(x => x.ToString("0")))}), fragile");
            Check(escorts.All(r => r.Shivering), "the escorts shiver at the launch point before the run in");
            // ── A BOSS'S ESCORTS ARE NOT THE LANCER'S: one more every ten levels, to five ──
            // The count was the row's alone, so every boss that ever calls craft in would have had
            // to grow its own. It belongs to the move table: a move that calls nothing still calls
            // nothing, and the fan spreads across the same arc whatever the count, so five need no
            // new formation.
            var callsThem = Missions.ForLevel(1).Moves.First(m => m.Escorts > 0);
