            var callsNone = Missions.ForLevel(1).Moves.First(m => m.Escorts == 0);
            Check(Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 1) == 2 && Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 10) == 2
                  && Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 11) == 3 && Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 21) == 4
                  && Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 31) == 5 && Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 99) == 5
                  && Boss.EscortsAt(callsNone, 99) == 0,
                  $"a move's escorts grow with the level and stop at five: {Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 1)} at level 1, {Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 11)} at 11, {Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 21)} at 21, {Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 31)} at 31, {Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 99)} at 99 -- and a move that calls none still calls none");
            // ESCORTS OUT, THE BOSS ALREADY HOLDS ITS POSITION -- and turns only to face the pilot. Pushed
            // out past its 650 u standoff (where it would otherwise close at 30 u/s) and twisted 0.6 rad
            // off the pilot first, so both "it held" and "it turned" are real claims.
            {
                var armHome = boss.Position;
                boss.Position += (boss.Position - me2.Position).Normalized() * 700f;
                var armAt = boss.Position;
                boss.Rotation += 0.6f;
                float Off() => Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, (me2.Position - boss.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f));
                float armOff0 = Off();
                await Wait(0.35);
                float armOff1 = Off();
                Check(EscortsOut() && !BeamCharging() && boss.Locked && boss.Position.DistanceTo(armAt) < 0.01f && armOff0 - armOff1 > 0.07f,
                      $"escorts out, the boss holds its position already ({boss.Position.DistanceTo(armAt):0.000} u) and turns only to face the pilot ({armOff0:0.000} -> {armOff1:0.000} rad)");
                boss.Position = armHome;
            }
        // ON THE WIRE. Boosting and Shivering are read by _Draw, and both were host-only: a guest
        // drew neither the escorts' triple-length plume nor any raider's boost plume. They ride
        // the raider packet as two bits now. Bit values as literals, not Raider.FlagShiver -- a
        // check that reads the constant cannot catch the constant changing under it.
        //
        // An escort is boost-ARMED while it shivers (the boost is not spent sitting still), so
        // both bits are set here and only the shiver bit clears when it goes. Asserting the pair
        // BEFORE and AFTER is what makes this a check on the bits rather than on one snapshot:
        // a NetFlags stuck at any constant value fails one half or the other.
        var shiverFlags = escorts.Select(r => r.NetFlags).ToList();
        Check(escorts.All(r => (r.NetFlags & 2) != 0),
              $"a shivering escort says so on the wire ({string.Join(",", shiverFlags)})");
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && escorts.Any(r => r.Shivering); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var runFlags = escorts.Where(r => r.Alive).Select(r => r.NetFlags).ToList();
        Check(runFlags.Count > 0 && runFlags.All(f => (f & 2) == 0) && runFlags.Any(f => (f & 1) != 0),
              $"and once it goes, the shiver bit clears and the boost bit stands ({string.Join(",", runFlags)})");
            Check(!Nodes<FxNode>().Any(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane), "no beam line yet: the escorts go first, the charge follows their web");
            // THE CHARGE STARTS ON THE PIN -- not on a guess made at launch. Pinned now, well before the
            // web was predicted to land, it charges at once (the old boss waited out its estimate).
            if (EscortsOut())
            {
                double predicted = BeamSlot().Predict;
                me2.ApplyStatus(Status.Pinned, 0.3);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(BeamCharging() && BeamSlot().Cause == "pinned" && BeamSlot().ArmedFor < predicted - 0.2,
                      $"the charge starts the moment the pilot is pinned, before the web was predicted to land ({BeamSlot().Cause} at {BeamSlot().ArmedFor:0.00} s, predicted {predicted:0.00} s)");
            }
            else Check(BeamCharging() && BeamSlot().Cause == "pinned",
                       $"the charge starts the moment the pilot is pinned ({BeamSlot().Cause} at {BeamSlot().ArmedFor:0.00} s)");
            // the bar under the boss's hull resets as a super commits and counts the 15 s to the next
            Check(boss.SuperFill < 0.15 && Math.Abs(boss.NextSuperIn - 15) < 1.5,
                  $"the super-move bar resets as the beam commits, counting the 15 s to the ram ({boss.NextSuperIn:0.0} s, fill {boss.SuperFill:0.00})");
            FxNode beam = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && beam == null; i++) { beam = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane && Math.Abs(t.Radius - 70f) < 0.1f); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(beam != null && boss.WarningsPending > 0 && beam.Warn && BeamSlot().M.Width == 70f,
                  "the death beam is telegraphed: a red lane 70 u wide -- one warning row of Fx.All -- before it fires");
            Check(Math.Abs(beam.Time - 6.0) < 1e-6 && BeamSlot().M.Windup == 6 && Math.Abs(beam.Hold - 3.0) < 1e-6 && BeamSlot().M.Live == 3.0,
                  "the beam charges 6 s -- its row's wind-up, drawn for exactly that long -- and its lane stays lit the 3 s it burns");
            // the warning is parented to the boss, so it swings with the hull instead of being
            // pinned to the spot the boss stood on: the line drawn IS the line fired
            Check(beam.GetParent() == boss && beam.Anchor == boss.NetId,
                  "the beam's warning rides the hull rather than the world: the raise carries the hull's own id");
            // Push it out past its 650 u standoff BEFORE measuring. Inside that range it would
            // not close anyway, so watching for drift there proves nothing -- a mutant that
            // ignored the lock completely still passed this. Out here it wants to close at
            // 30 u/s, which is 22 u over the window below, so holding still is a real claim.
            var bossWasAt = boss.Position;
            boss.Position += (boss.Position - me2.Position).Normalized() * 700f;
            var lockAt = boss.Position;
            double standoff = boss.Position.DistanceTo(me2.Position);
            await Wait(0.75);
            Check(BeamCharging() && boss.Locked && standoff > 650 && boss.Position.DistanceTo(lockAt) < 0.01f,
                  $"the boss is locked in place while the beam charges, {standoff:0} u out where it would otherwise close (drifted {boss.Position.DistanceTo(lockAt):0.000} u)");
            boss.Position = bossWasAt;   // put it back: left out there its tridents take so long to
                                         // arrive that the point-defence check below times out
            // Reach, not trigger: this is how far the beam carries, whatever started its charge (the
            // 30 s rhythm, the nearest pilot, the escorts' web). Compared with
            // the spec's number as a literal, never with BeamLength itself, and checked on the
            // real segment so the drawn indicator and the hit line are both covered.
            var (segA, segB) = boss.Segment("beam");
            var farOut = segA + (segB - segA).Normalized() * 5000f;
            Check(BeamSlot().M.Reach == 10000f && Math.Abs((beam.To - beam.Position).Length() - 10000f) < 1f
                  && Combat.DistToSegment(farOut, segA, segB) < 1f,
                  $"beam and indicator both reach 10000 u -- a point 5000 u out is inside it, where the old 1800 u fell short (indicator {(beam.To - beam.Position).Length():0} u)");
            // HARD LOCKED while it charges -- with the pilot FREE: the old boss froze too once the pilot
            // was pinned, and tracked it otherwise, so only a free pilot tells the two apart. Point
            // defence clears the escorts and the web lets go; then, twisted well off the pilot, the boss
            // does not turn back. A boss that tracked would close 0.15 rad of the 0.6 in the 0.5 s.
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && (me2.Pinned || H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort)); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            boss.Rotation += 0.6f;
            float heldAt = boss.Rotation;
            await Wait(0.5);
            Check(BeamCharging() && !me2.Pinned && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, heldAt)) < 1e-4f,
                  $"hard locked while the beam charges, the pilot free: twisted off it, the boss does not turn back (pinned {me2.Pinned}, turned {Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, heldAt):0.0000} rad in 0.5 s)");
            // The twist was a probe, and the aim is locked, so put it back on the pilot by hand: the
            // beam below has to be the real shot, and the damage check at the end of this scenario is
            // the only thing that proves the locked line still lands.
            boss.Rotation = (me2.Position - boss.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f;
            int down0 = Shot.Intercepted;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && Shot.Intercepted == down0; i++)
            {
                if (!Me.PdActive && Me.PdReady) Key(global::Godot.Key.Q);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            Check(Shot.Intercepted > down0, "point defence shoots the boss's missiles down");
            Check(!H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort), "point defence, switched on, kills the beam's escorts");
            var aMissile = Missiles().FirstOrDefault(t => t.HostileFire);
            Check(Turret.PdPriority(boss) == 2 && (aMissile == null || Turret.PdPriority(aMissile) == 0), "point defence takes missiles before anything else");
            var tiny = new TinyTarget();
            Check(Turret.PdPriority(tiny) == 1 && Turret.PdPriority(tiny) < Turret.PdPriority(boss)
                  && Targeting.PointDefence.Allows(tiny) && !Targeting.PointDefence.Allows(boss),
                  "then small craft (by their tag, not their size), then everything else -- and a boss is never a PD target");
            FxNode ring = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 18 && ring == null; i++) { ring = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(ring != null && ring.Radius == 340f && boss.S("wave").M.Reach == 340f && ring.Anchor == Fx.World,
                  "the shockwave is telegraphed: a red zone 340 u across, on the ground, before it lands");
            // without point defence, a pair of escorts reaches the pilot and pins it inside the charge
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Me.PdActive; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            boss.LaunchEscorts("beam", me2);
            bool pinnedInTime = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < (int)(60 * BeamSlot().M.Windup) && !pinnedInTime; i++) { pinnedInTime = me2.Pinned; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(pinnedInTime, "left alone (no point defence), the escorts pin the pilot before the charge ends");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(999);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 25 && (tCharge < 0 || Ramming() || tVolley < 0); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            GD.Print($"  boss rhythm: beam {tBeam:0.0} s, trident {tVolley:0.0} s, charge {tCharge:0.0} s; trident of {trident}, spread {Mathf.RadToDeg(spread):0} deg");
            // the watcher starts a moment after the boss's clock, so compare the gaps, not the times
            Check(tBeam > 0 && Math.Abs(tCharge - tBeam - 15) < 0.4 && Math.Abs(tVolley - tBeam - 7.5) < 0.4,
                  "the rhythm: the charge 15 s after the beam, a trident half-way between");
            Check(trident == 3 && Math.Abs(Mathf.RadToDeg(spread) - 50) < 3, "a trident: three double-size missiles, the outer two 25 degrees off the middle");
            double beamDmg = me2.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("boss:beam", out var bd) ? bd : 0, chargeDmg = me2.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("boss:charge", out var cd) ? cd : 0;
            // live 3 s, checked every 0.25 s for 50 -- one source lands at most once per 0.52 s, and
            // ticks fall on frames, so two ticks are sometimes 0.533 s apart: 5 to 7 hits of 50
            Check(beamDmg >= 200 && beamDmg <= 350 && Math.Abs(beamDmg % 50) < 1e-6, $"the beam, live 3 s at 50 a tick, through the 0.52 s guard: {beamDmg / 50:0} hits ({beamDmg:0})");
            Check(Math.Abs(chargeDmg - 40) < 1e-6, $"the charge rams for 40 ({chargeDmg:0})");
            Check(sidestep && liveTurn < 0.01f, $"the live beam holds the line it drew, with the pilot gone from it (turned {Mathf.RadToDeg(liveTurn):0.00} deg)");
            Check(liveDrawn, "and the line is still drawn 2 s into its 3 s burn");
            Check(Music.I == null || Math.Abs(boss.S("trident").M.Speed - 135f) < 1e-3, "trident missiles fly at 135 u/s (10% slower)");
            // THE FALLBACKS, each on a beam started by hand. The escorts shot down: the charge still comes,
            // but not before their web would have landed. Escorts that neither pin nor die (the pilot far
            // off): they cannot stall it past 5 s. And the ram waits out a beam rather than snapping round
            // in the middle of one.
            {
                // A MOVE IS ARMED BY ITS ROW'S CLOCK, so each fallback is started by putting that
                // clock on the point of coming round -- no reflection, and a renamed field can no
                // longer pass silently.
                bool RamLine() => Nodes<FxNode>().Any(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane && Math.Abs(t.Radius - 140f) < 0.1f) || boss.S("ram").At != Boss.Phase.Idle;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && (boss.Locked || RamLine()); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                me2.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                BeamSlot().Due = 0.01;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && !EscortsOut(); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                double predictD = BeamSlot().Predict;
                foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(999);
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !BeamCharging(); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(BeamCharging() && BeamSlot().Cause == "escorts down" && BeamSlot().ArmedFor >= predictD - 0.02 && BeamSlot().ArmedFor < predictD + 0.1,
                      $"the escorts shot down, the beam still charges -- once their web would have landed, not before ({BeamSlot().Cause} at {BeamSlot().ArmedFor:0.00} s, web due {predictD:0.00} s)");
                // the ram falls due in the middle of this beam: it waits for the beam to end, then comes
                boss.S("ram").Due = 0.01;
                bool ramDuringBeam = false;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && (BeamCharging() || BeamLive()); i++)
                { ramDuringBeam |= RamLine(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                bool ramAfter = false;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && !ramAfter; i++) { ramAfter = RamLine(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                Check(!ramDuringBeam && ramAfter, $"the ram, due mid-beam, waits for the beam to end and then comes (during: {ramDuringBeam}, after: {ramAfter})");
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && (boss.Locked || RamLine()); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                // the pilot 3000 u off: the escorts need longer than 5 s to reach it
                me2.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 3000); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                BeamSlot().Due = 0.01;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !BeamCharging(); i++) { me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                Check(BeamCharging() && BeamSlot().Cause == "overdue" && Math.Abs(BeamSlot().ArmedFor - 5.0) < 0.05,
                      $"escorts that neither pin nor die cannot stall it: it charges 5 s after they launch ({BeamSlot().Cause} at {BeamSlot().ArmedFor:0.00} s)");
                foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(999);
                me2.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            }
            // the kill: shared EXP for it and the mission, the bounty's credits, then home
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; Character.BossCleared.Clear();
            // (hints off meanwhile: a card starting mid-way would move the batched save these checks time)
            Character.HintsOff = true;
            // clear of where the crates will fall, with the boss's escorts out (they must die with it)
            Me.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 1500); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            boss.LaunchEscorts("beam", Me); await Wait(0.1);
            int escortsOut = H.Raiders.Count;
            int HoldOnDisk() { var c = new ConfigFile(); if (c.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg") != Error.Ok || !c.HasSection("gear_hold")) return 0;
                               return c.GetSectionKeys("gear_hold").Sum(k => (int)c.GetValue("gear_hold", k, 0)); }
            int UnclaimedOnDisk() => ((string)OnDisk("loot", "unclaimed", "")).Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Length;
            int hold0 = Character.GearHold.Values.Sum(), disk0 = HoldOnDisk();
            var bossAt = boss.Position;
            // killed mid-wind-up, a lane on its hull and a zone on the ground: its warnings go with
            // it. Raised here rather than by any boss move, because what is being proved is the
            // WARNING -- the raise's anchor puts the lane on the hull and leaves the zone on the
            // ground, and a boss that is down clears both (Fx.ClearWarnings).
            Fx.Warn(new FxRaise { Id = Fx.WarnLane, At = new Vector2(0, -100), To = new Vector2(0, -3000), Size = 70f, Time = 10.0, Anchor = boss.NetId });
            Fx.Warn(new FxRaise { Id = Fx.WarnZone, At = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 600), To = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 600), Size = 300f, Time = 10.0 });
            var warnings = Nodes<FxNode>().Where(t => t.Warn && t.Time == 10.0).ToList();
            bool ridesHull = warnings.Count(t => t.GetParent() == boss) == 1 && warnings.Count(t => t.GetParent() == H) == 1;
            boss.TakeDamage(boss.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(warnings.Count == 2 && ridesHull && warnings.All(t => !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(t)),
                  $"a warning goes where its raise anchors it -- one on the hull, one on the ground -- and a boss killed mid-wind-up takes both with it: no beam charging over a DEFEATED boss ({warnings.Count(t => GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(t))} of {warnings.Count} left)");
            var dropped = H.Crates.ToList();
            Check(dropped.Count == 2 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 2 && UnclaimedOnDisk() == 2
                  && dropped.All(c => c.Position.DistanceTo(bossAt) < 300 && Equipment.ById(c.Item) is { Kit: false, Rarity: Rarity.Common }),
                  $"a level-1 boss drops this pilot 2 Common crates where it died, on disk at once ({dropped.Count} crates, {UnclaimedOnDisk()} on disk)");
            Check(escortsOut >= 2 && H.Raiders.Count == 0, $"the boss's escorts die with it ({escortsOut} out, {H.Raiders.Count} left)");
            // NO CLOCK any more: the failure timer is not armed on a win, and a RETURN button appears
            double solEndT = (double)typeof(Hub).GetField("_arenaEndT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(H);
            Check(solEndT < 0 && H.MissionWon && !H.IReturned && H.ReturnTotal == 1 && Nodes<ReturnButton>().Any(),
                  $"after the kill: a RETURN button and no clock sending you home (_arenaEndT {solEndT:0.0}, ready {H.ReturnReady}/{H.ReturnTotal})");
            Priv(H, "ReceiveLoot", new[] { "no_such_part", "chip_basic", "fr_flak_1", "fr_flak_2", "fr_flak_3" }, bossAt + new Vector2(0, 400), 1);
            Check(H.Crates.Count == 4 && H.Crates.Skip(2).Select(c => c.Item).SequenceEqual(new[] { "fr_flak_1", "fr_flak_2" }),
                  $"a drop off the wire: no unknown part, no kit, and no more than a boss gives -- 2 at level 1 ({string.Join(",", H.Crates.Skip(2).Select(c => c.Item))})");
            // FLY OVER A CRATE: in the hold at once; on disk 2.5 s after the LAST pickup
            var crA = H.Crates[0]; var crB = H.Crates[1];
            Me.Position = crA.Position - Vector2.Right.Rotated(Me.Rotation) * (Me.MyArt.HalfWidth + 38f); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            await Wait(0.5);
            bool besideLeft = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crA) && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == hold0;
            Check(besideLeft, "a ship beside a crate, not over it, leaves it where it lies");
            Me.Position = crA.Position; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crA); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            bool tookA = !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crA) && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == hold0 + 1;
            await Wait(1.5);
            Me.Position = crB.Position; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crB); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.5); int diskMid = HoldOnDisk();
            await Wait(1.3); int diskAfter = HoldOnDisk();
            Check(tookA && diskMid == disk0 && diskAfter == disk0 + 2 && UnclaimedOnDisk() == 2,
                  $"flying over a crate takes it into the hold at once; the file follows 2.5 s after the LAST pickup ({disk0}: {diskMid} 1.5 s after the second, {diskAfter} at 2.8 s)");
            // IN STASIS, THE POD COLLECTS: near is not enough -- it has to fly over the crate
            var crC = H.Crates[0]; var cAt = crC.Position;
            Me.TakeDamage(Me.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.3);
            var pod = (EscapePod)typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_pod", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Me);
            Me.Position = cAt + new Vector2(0, 900);
            pod.Position = cAt + new Vector2(60, 0); pod.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.0);
            bool stillThere = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crC) && crC.Position == cAt;
            pod.Position = cAt; pod.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crC); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(!Me.Alive && stillThere && !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crC),
                  $"a pilot in stasis collects with its escape pod: 60 u off leaves the crate where it lies, flying over it takes it");
            Check(Hub.InArena, "five seconds and more after the kill, the party is still in the arena");
            var crD = H.Crates[0];
            Check(H.MissionWon && Character.Level == 1 && Character.Exp == 650, $"the kill of a level-1 boss pays a level-1 pilot 300 + 250 first clear + 100 = 650 (exp {Character.Exp})");
            var onKill = new ConfigFile(); onKill.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
            Check(Math.Abs((double)onKill.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - (424242 + 2000)) < 1e-6,
                  $"the bounty is on disk at the kill, not on the trip home: a quit out there kept the EXP and lost the credits ({(double)onKill.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0})");
            // no clock: RETURN takes you home; solo, the one present pilot is enough
            H.SetMyReturn(true);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && Hub.InArena; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(!Hub.InArena && H.Yard != null, "pressing RETURN takes the pilot home (solo: no one else to wait for)");
            // the crate left lying came home too -- into the hold -- and its node stayed behind
            Check(!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crD) && Count<LootCrate>() == 0 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0 && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == hold0 + 4,
                  $"the crate left behind comes home in the hold, its node gone with the arena ({Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() - hold0} of 4 parts)");
            Character.HintsOff = false;
            await Wait(Raids.Delay + 0.5);
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0, "a won mission brings no raid");
            // exactly the credits set aside at departure + the 2000 bounty (a hauler sale before
            // departure is part of what was set aside, so a fixed window was fragile)
            Check(Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - Yard.TripStartCredits - 2000) < 1e-6, $"the bounty's 2000 credits are paid at home ({Yard.TripStartCredits:0} + 2000 = {H.Yard.Credits:0})");
            // the literal 1/20 asked for -- not Yard.AwayShare, or the check could never fail
            double want = H.Yard.FleetRate(GatherKind.Miner) * Yard.LastAway / 20.0;
            Check(Yard.LastAway > 1 && Yard.LastAwayGained("ore") > 0 && Math.Abs(Yard.LastAwayGained("ore") - want) < 1e-6,
                  $"while away the base earned 1/20 of its rate ({Yard.LastAwayGained("ore"):0.0} ore for {Yard.LastAway:0} s)");
            // levels: the win cleared level 1; docking at the TIO selects level 2
            Check(Character.BossCleared.TryGetValue("silver_lancer", out var cleared) && cleared.Contains(1), "the win records level 1 as cleared");
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.S(2) - Missions.LevelStep) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.S(3) - Missions.LevelStep * Missions.LevelStep) < 1e-9,
                  "each level is stronger, multiplicatively (the level-3 Lancer: its row x the level scale)");
            H.OpenTio(); await Wait(0.1);
            var tw2 = Nodes<TioWindow>().First();
            Check(Missions.Level == 2, "docking at the TIO selects the newest unlocked level (2)");
            Check(Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelUp").Disabled, "no level beyond the newest unlocked");
            Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelDown").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Missions.Level == 1, "the host can pick an easier, cleared level");
            Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelUp").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Missions.Level == 2, "and back up to the newest");
            // ONE SIDE WINDOW AT A TIME: they share a spot. B over the open TIO used to leave both up,
            // one drawn over the other; now it takes the spot, and B again gives it back empty.
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Count<TioWindow>() == 0 && Count<BasePanel>() == 1, $"B with the TIO open: the base takes the spot ({Count<TioWindow>()} TIO, {Count<BasePanel>()} base)");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0 && Count<TioWindow>() == 0, "and B again closes it");
            H.OpenTio(); await Wait(0.1);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            // a wipe: every ship in stasis at once -> the mission fails, home with nothing
            H.EnterSector(Hub.SectorKind.Arena); await Wait(1.5);
            // THE DRAKE BASTION: level 2 is its. Checked here, in the arena the wipe below fails.
            {
                var dk = H.Boss;
                var me3 = Me; me3.MaxHp = 5000; me3.TakeDamage(-(5000 - me3.Hp)); me3.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me3.Rotation = 0f;
                var dspr = dk?.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().FirstOrDefault();
                Check(dk != null && dk.Type.Id == "drake_bastion" && dk.Type.Name == "DRAKE BASTION" && Math.Abs(dk.MaxHp - 700 * Missions.S(2)) < 1e-6
                      && dspr != null && dspr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("boss_drake.png") && Math.Abs(dspr.Texture.GetHeight() * dspr.Scale.Y - 420f) < 0.5f,
                      $"level 2 is the Drake Bastion: 700 hull x the level's scale, 420 u long ({dk?.Type.Name}, {dk?.MaxHp:0})");
                if (dk != null)
                {
                    // ITS CLOCKS: a throw every 30 s from 6 s, the shotgun every 15 s from 17 s -- 11 s after a
                    // throw starts, clear of its 9.1 s (7.5 s of warning and 1.6 s of flight), so the two never
                    // run back to back (at the trident's own 13.5 s it fell inside the first throw)
                    double gap = dk.S("shotgun").Due - dk.S("throw").Due;
                    Check(Math.Abs(gap - 11) < 1e-6 && 11 > 7.5 + 1.6 && 30 - 11 - 15 > 0,
                          $"its specials never run back to back: the shotgun 11 s after each throw starts, clear of the throw's 9.1 s ({gap:0.00} s)");
                    // its own clocks out of the way: each special below is started by hand, alone
                    dk.S("throw").Due = 999.0; dk.S("shotgun").Due = 999.0;
                    // MAIN GUN: a slow shell every 2.5 s (2.4 DPS at 6 a shell), dodged by moving
                    me3.Position = dk.Position + new Vector2(0, 700); await Wait(0.1);
                    var shellSeen = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Shot>(Slugs());
                    var shellAt = new System.Collections.Generic.List<double>(); Shot shell = null; double clock = 0;
                    // EVERY FIRED SHOT IS ITS OWN SOURCE (Shots.SourceKey), so a weapon's tally is
                    // its family name, then "#", then the body's number. Summed by family here --
                    // and "boss:gun" is matched WHOLE as well, so the Lancer's "boss:guns", one
                    // character away, can never be counted as this boss's gun.
                    double Fired(string fam) => me3.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key == fam || kv.Key.StartsWith(fam + "#")).Sum(kv => kv.Value);
                    double gun0 = Fired("boss:gun");
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 7 && shellAt.Count < 2; i++)
                    {
                        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); clock += GetProcessDeltaTime();
                        foreach (var s in Slugs().Where(s => s.Kind == Shots.Slug && shellSeen.Add(s))) { shellAt.Add(clock); shell = s; }
                    }
                    float sspd = 0;
                    if (shell != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(shell)) { var sp0 = shell.Position; for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); sspd = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(shell) ? (shell.Position - sp0).Length() / (10f / 60f) : 0; }
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && Fired("boss:gun") == gun0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    double gunHit = Fired("boss:gun") - gun0;
                    Check(shellAt.Count == 2 && Math.Abs(shellAt[1] - shellAt[0] - 2.5) < 0.05 && Math.Abs(sspd - 220) < 4 && Math.Abs(gunHit - 6 * Missions.S(2)) < 1e-6,
                          $"its main gun: a slow shell (220 u/s) every 2.5 s, 6 a hit -- 2.4 DPS before the level's scale (here {6 * Missions.S(2):0.00}) ({sspd:0} u/s, {(shellAt.Count == 2 ? shellAt[1] - shellAt[0] : -1):0.00} s apart, {gunHit:0.00})");
                    // THE SCRAP SHOTGUN (started by hand): a warpRing where it will land -- 600 u from the pilot --
                    // then, held still, a fan of seven red lines for 1.875 s (1.25 x the Lancer's 1.5 s), then
                    // seven pieces of scrap down them: the same fixed fan, 10 degrees apart
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && dk.Locked; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    me3.Position = dk.Position + new Vector2(0, 1400); me3.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    dk.S("shotgun").Due = 0.01;
                    FxNode warpRing = null;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && warpRing == null; i++) { warpRing = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && dk.S("shotgun").At != Boss.Phase.Winding; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    var landed = dk.Position; float warpOff = landed.DistanceTo(me3.Position);
                    // by the move's OWN wind-up, which the level shortens (1.875 at level 1)
                    var fan = Nodes<FxNode>().Where(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane && Math.Abs(t.Time - dk.S("shotgun").M.Windup) < 1e-3).ToList();
                    var scrapSeen = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Shot>(Slugs());
                    await Wait(1.0);
                    bool heldStill = dk.Position.DistanceTo(landed) < 0.01f && dk.Locked;
                    var scrap = new System.Collections.Generic.List<Shot>();
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && scrap.Count < 7; i++) { scrap.AddRange(Slugs().Where(s => s.Kind == Shots.Scrap && scrapSeen.Add(s))); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    float aimTo = (me3.Position - landed).Angle();
                    var offs = scrap.Select(s => Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(aimTo, s.Dir.Angle()))).OrderBy(a => a).ToList();
                    bool fanned = offs.Count == 7 && offs.Zip(new[] { -30f, -20f, -10f, 0f, 10f, 20f, 30f }, (a, b) => Math.Abs(a - b) < 0.5f).All(x => x);
                    // 606, not 600: this is the LEVEL-2 Drake, and a boss gains 1% a level on its
                    // reach (Missions.Quicken), so its 600 u standoff is 600 x 1.01.
                    // The FIGURES ARE THE BOSS'S OWN, scaled for its level -- 1% a level on wind-ups
                    // and reach, proved against literals where that scale is defined. What is
                    // checked here is the SHAPE: a ring, a standoff, seven lines, seven pieces.
                    var shot = dk.S("shotgun").M;
                    Check(warpRing != null && Math.Abs(warpRing.Time - shot.Warp) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(warpOff - shot.Standoff) < 6 && fan.Count == 7 && heldStill && fanned
                          && dk.S("shotgun").Fired == 1,
                          $"the scrap shotgun: a warp to its own standoff off the pilot ({warpOff:0} u of {shot.Standoff:0}, ring {warpRing?.Time:0.000} of {shot.Warp:0.000}), a fan of {fan.Count} lines for {shot.Windup:0.000} s held still ({heldStill}), then 7 pieces at -30..+30 in 10s ({string.Join(" ", offs.Select(a => a.ToString("0")))}), counted once ({dk.S("shotgun").Fired})");
                    // EACH PIECE ITS OWN HIT, by construction rather than by hand. The fan used to
                    // number its seven source keys itself (boss:scrap:0..6) so the pieces would not
                    // swallow one another inside a hull's 0.52 s per-source gap; every fired shot
                    // carries its own identity now, so the fan says "boss:scrap" once. The piece at
                    // 0 degrees is aimed dead at the pilot (`fanned`, above), and a hull as broad as
                    // the 302 u battleship's takes its neighbours too -- so what the tally must show
                    // is a WHOLE number of one piece, at least one: every piece that lands felt in
                    // full, and not one of them swallowed.
                    double all0 = Fired("boss:scrap");
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    double allDmg = Fired("boss:scrap") - all0, pieces = allDmg / (18.75 * Missions.S(2));
                    Check(pieces >= 1 && Math.Abs(pieces - Math.Round(pieces)) < 1e-6,
                          $"the piece aimed at the pilot strikes it, and every piece that lands is felt in full -- 15 x1.25 = 18.75, x the level's scale ({18.75 * Missions.S(2):0.00} each) ({allDmg:0.000} taken, {pieces:0} of the 7 struck)");
                    // THE ASTEROID THROW (started by hand): a rock beside it in its tractor, a red lane of the
                    // rock's width for 7.5 s (1.25 x the Lancer's 6 s charge), the boss held still -- then the
                    // throw: slow, then very fast (the first half of the flight covers an eighth of the lane),
                    // 250 x the level's scale to a ship in the lane, and the rock breaks apart at the lane's end
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && dk.Locked; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    me3.Position = dk.Position + new Vector2(0, 700); me3.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    await Wait(0.2);
                    dk.S("throw").Due = 0.01;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 && dk.Rock == null; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    var rock = dk.Rock;
                    var lane = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane && Math.Abs(t.Radius - 180f) < 0.5f);
                    var drakeAt = dk.Position; var rockAt = rock?.Position ?? Vector2.Zero;
                    await Wait(3.0);
                    bool heldRock = rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock) && rock.Position.DistanceTo(rockAt) < 0.01f && !rock.Thrown
                                    && dk.Position.DistanceTo(drakeAt) < 0.01f && dk.Locked;
                    // 7.43 s and 1.58: the flight and the wind-up are the level-2 Drake's, one
                    // percent quicker than the row's 7.5 and 1.6 (Missions.Quicken). The 220 u the
                    // rock is held at is a reach, so it goes the other way: 220 x 1.01 = 222.
                    var heave = dk.S("throw").M;
                    Check(rock != null && lane != null && Math.Abs(lane.Time - heave.Windup) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(lane.Hold - heave.Flight) < 1e-3
                          && Math.Abs(rockAt.DistanceTo(drakeAt) - heave.Offset) < 1.5 && heldRock && dk.S("throw").Fired == 1,
                          $"the throw: a rock beside it in its tractor ({heave.Offset:0} u off), a lane of its width for {heave.Windup:0.00} s then {heave.Flight:0.00} s of flight, the boss and the rock held still ({rockAt.DistanceTo(drakeAt):0} u, lane {lane?.Time:0.00} s), counted once ({dk.S("throw").Fired})");
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock) && !rock.Thrown; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    var lane0 = rock?.From ?? Vector2.Zero; float laneLen = rock == null ? 1 : rock.From.DistanceTo(rock.To);
                    await Wait(0.8);
                    double half = rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock) ? rock.Position.DistanceTo(lane0) / laneLen : -1;
                    bool broke = false;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock); i++) { broke |= rock.Done; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    double rockDmg = me3.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault("boss:rock");
                    Check(Math.Abs(half - 0.125) < 0.04 && broke && Math.Abs(laneLen - heave.Reach) < 1 && Math.Abs(rockDmg - 250 * Missions.S(2)) < 1e-6,
                          $"thrown: half the flight covers an eighth of its {heave.Reach:0} u lane ({half:0.000}) -- the ease, whatever the level does to the length -- it strikes the pilot for its 250 at the level's scale ({rockDmg:0}), and breaks apart at the end ({broke})");
                }
            }
            // EVERY BOSS SPECIAL HAS ITS OWN SOUND, heard as it happens: the Lancer's beam (its charge, and
            // the growl as it fires), ram, shockwave and trident, fought above; the Drake's gun, warp, scrap
            // blast, tractor, throw and the rock breaking, here
            {
                var bossSounds = new[] { "boss_beam_charge", "boss_beam", "boss_ram", "boss_shockwave", "boss_trident",
                                         "drake_gun", "drake_warp", "drake_scrap", "drake_tractor", "drake_throw", "drake_rock" };
                var unheard = bossSounds.Where(n => GD.Load<AudioStream>($"res://sfx/{n}.wav") == null || Sfx.Played.GetValueOrDefault(n) == 0).ToList();
                Check(unheard.Count == 0, $"every boss special has its own sound, and each was heard in its fight (unheard: {string.Join(", ", unheard)})");
            }
            int exp1 = Character.Exp; int failLevel = Missions.Level;
            Me.TakeDamage(Me.Hp + 1);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Hub.InArena; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(!Hub.InArena && Character.Exp == exp1 && Me.Alive, "the whole party in stasis: the mission fails, home with no reward");
            // ...and the boss sends its raiders after you: 2 patrols (solo), from the map's edge, at the failed level's strength
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0, "the raid is not there at once (every guest's world loads first)");
            // measured the frame they appear: a raider that spots a miner boosts inward at once
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && H.Raiders.Count == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            double sL = Math.Pow(Missions.LevelStep, failLevel - 1);
            var raid = H.Raiders.ToList();
            GD.Print($"  raid: {raid.Count} raiders, level {failLevel} (x{sL:0.00}), {raid.Min(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos)):0}-{raid.Max(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos)):0} u out");
            Check(raid.Count == 8 && raid.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 2 && raid.Select(r => r.Patrol).Distinct().Count() == 2,
                  "a failed mission: the boss sends 2 patrols (3 lights and a heavy each)");
            Check(raid.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - Hub.RaidEdge) < 110f), "in from the edge of the base's map (3200 u out)");
            Check(raid.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Strength - sL) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(r.MaxHull - Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Hull * sL) < 1e-6),
                  $"at the failed boss's level: hull and damage x{sL:0.00} (LevelStep^(L-1), L = {failLevel})");
            foreach (var r in raid) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
            await Wait(0.3);
            // the fighter-class autopilot, flown on its own (no player fighter class yet)
            Vector2 fp = Vector2.Zero, fv = Vector2.Zero; float fh = 0f; var goal = new Vector2(800, -600);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12; i++)
            {
                var (turn, thrust) = Autopilot.Fighter(fp, fh, fv, goal, 300f, 30f);
                fh += turn * 4f / 60f; fv += Vector2.Right.Rotated(fh) * thrust * 600f / 60f; fv *= 0.985f;
                if (fv.Length() > 300f) fv = fv.Normalized() * 300f;
                fp += fv / 60f;
            }
            Check(fp.DistanceTo(goal) < 80f, $"the fighter-class autopilot reaches its point ({fp.DistanceTo(goal):0} u off)");
            Check(Settings.MusicOn, "music is on by default");

            // ── THE PIRATE BASE SIEGE: the first entry of RAIDS, the second thing a mission has
            //    ever been able to be, and a LADDER OF ITS OWN ─────────────────────────────────
            {
                Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1;
                Character.Unclaimed.Clear(); Character.Save();
                Check(Missions.KindOf(Missions.Bounty).Category == "BOSS" && Missions.KindOf(Missions.Siege).Category == "RAIDS"
                      && Missions.KindOf(Missions.Siege).Name == "PIRATE BASE SIEGE",
                      $"a mission kind is a ROW: RAIDS beside BOSS, the siege its first entry ({Missions.KindOf(Missions.Siege).Category} · {Missions.KindOf(Missions.Siege).Name})");
                // THE SELECTED LEVEL IS PER CATEGORY: stepping one never moves the other
                Missions.Kind = Missions.Bounty; Missions.Level = 2;
                Missions.Kind = Missions.Siege;  Missions.Level = 3;
                int raidSel = Missions.Level;
                Missions.Kind = Missions.Bounty; int bossSel = Missions.Level;
                Missions.Kind = Missions.Siege;
                Check(raidSel == 3 && bossSel == 2 && Missions.Level == 3,
                      $"the selected level is the CATEGORY's: RAIDS on 3 left BOSS on 2 ({raidSel} raids, {bossSel} boss)");

                H.EnterSector(Hub.SectorKind.Arena); await Wait(1.5);
                // well outside the base's own guns, and the pylons': nothing shoots at the pilot
                // while the numbers below are read, and where it sits is not one of them
                var sme = Me; sme.MaxHp = 500000; sme.TakeDamage(-(500000 - sme.Hp));
                sme.Position = VaryNear(Hub.ArenaCentre + new Vector2(0, 3400), 500f); sme.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                await Wait(0.2);
                var em = H.Emplacements.ToList();
                var pbase = em.FirstOrDefault(e => e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Base);
                var pylons = em.Where(e => e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Pylon).ToList();
                Check(em.Count == 5 && pbase != null && pylons.Count == 4 && H.Boss == null,
                      $"a siege builds a pirate base and FOUR pylons, and no boss at all ({em.Count} hulls, {pylons.Count} pylons, boss {(H.Boss == null ? "none" : "built")})");
                // 1200 u out on each diagonal -- the four ends of an invisible X, one per corner
                var pylonCorners = pylons.Select(p => (Math.Sign(p.Position.X - pbase.Position.X), Math.Sign(p.Position.Y - pbase.Position.Y))).ToHashSet();
                Check(pylons.All(p => Math.Abs(p.Position.DistanceTo(pbase.Position) - 1200) < 1f
                                   && Math.Abs(Math.Abs(p.Position.X - pbase.Position.X) - Math.Abs(p.Position.Y - pbase.Position.Y)) < 1f)
                      && pylonCorners.Count == 4,
                      $"four pylons 1200 u out on the diagonals, one to each corner ({string.Join(", ", pylons.Select(p => $"{p.Position.DistanceTo(pbase.Position):0}"))} u; {pylonCorners.Count} pylonCorners)");
                // TWICE THE BOSS OF THIS LEVEL, at the same scale -- one hull formula, not a second.
                // (The level's own scale is proved against its literal once, where it is defined.)
                double bossAt3 = Missions.ForLevel(3).Hull * Missions.HullMult(3, 1);
                Check(Math.Abs(pbase.MaxHp - 2 * bossAt3) < 1e-6,
                      $"the pirate base is 2x the boss of its own level: 2 x {bossAt3:0.0} = {pbase.MaxHp:0.0} at level 3");
                Check(pylons.All(p => Math.Abs(p.MaxHp - 400 * Missions.S(3)) < 1e-6),
                      $"each pylon starts at 400 hull and climbs the same level scale (level 3: 400 x {Missions.S(3):0.000} = {pylons[0].MaxHp:0})");

                // ITS GARRISON comes as an escort's hunters do -- the same one builder, a row of
                // Waves.All -- but at FULL hull and the mission's own strength: the escort halves
                // its hunters (Waves.HunterHull), and none of that reaches here.
                var siegeRow = Missions.KindOf(Missions.Siege);
                Check(siegeRow.WaveEvery > 0 && Missions.KindOf(Missions.Bounty).WaveEvery == 0,
                      $"the wave clock is the mission ROW's: a siege every {siegeRow.WaveEvery:0} s from {siegeRow.FirstWave:0} s, a bounty none");
                int raid0 = H.Raiders.Count;
                H.GarrisonWave(Hub.ArenaCentre, 0); await Wait(0.3);
                var wave = H.Raiders.Skip(raid0).ToList();
                double siegeLv = Math.Pow(Missions.LevelStep, 3 - 1);
                Check(wave.Count == 4 && wave.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 1 && Math.Abs(Waves.HunterHull - 0.5) < 1e-9
                      && wave.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Strength - siegeLv) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(r.MaxHull - Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Hull * siegeLv) < 1e-6),
                      $"a siege wave is 3 pinners and a standoff at FULL hull and x{siegeLv:0.00} -- not the escort's half ({wave.Count} raiders, {wave.Count(r => r.Heavy)} heavy)");
                foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) H.RaiderDown(r);
                await Wait(0.2);

                // THE SHIELD: the four pylons hold it up, and nothing touches the base until every
                // one of them is down. All in one frame -- a dropped hull leaves H.Emplacements at
                // the call, not at the frame's end.
                double baseHp0 = pbase.Hp;
                pbase.TakeDamage(500);
                bool allFour = pbase.Shielded && Math.Abs(pbase.Hp - baseHp0) < 1e-9;
                for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) pylons[i].TakeDamage(pylons[i].MaxHp * 2);
                pbase.TakeDamage(500);
                bool lastOne = pbase.Shielded && Math.Abs(pbase.Hp - baseHp0) < 1e-9
                               && H.Emplacements.Count(e => e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Pylon) == 1;
                Check(allFour && lastOne,
                      $"the base takes NOTHING while a pylon stands: 500 with four up and 500 with one, {baseHp0 - pbase.Hp:0} taken of 1000");
                pylons[3].TakeDamage(pylons[3].MaxHp * 2);
                pbase.TakeDamage(500);
                Check(!pbase.Shielded && Math.Abs(baseHp0 - pbase.Hp - 500) < 1e-6
                      && H.Emplacements.Count(e => e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Pylon) == 0,
                      $"all four pylons down, the base takes damage: 500 off {baseHp0:0} leaves {pbase.Hp:0}");
                await Wait(0.2);
                // ONLY THE BASE DROPS: four pylons have fallen and left nothing at all
                Check(H.CratesDropped == 0 && Count<LootCrate>() == 0 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0,
                      $"four pylons down and not one crate: only the base drops in a siege ({H.CratesDropped} crates, {Character.Unclaimed.Count} unclaimed)");
                var wreck = pbase.Position;
                pbase.TakeDamage(pbase.Hp + 1);
                await Wait(0.6);
                Check(H.MissionWon && H.CratesDropped == Loot.CratesFor(3) && Count<LootCrate>() == Loot.CratesFor(3)
                      && H.Crates.All(c => Math.Abs(c.Position.DistanceTo(wreck) - Loot.Ring) < 1f),
                      $"the base's fall ends the siege and drops ITS crates, on the {Loot.Ring:0} u ring ({H.CratesDropped} of {Loot.CratesFor(3)})");

                // ── TWO LADDERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
                // The siege clear opens the next RAID and leaves the bosses exactly where they
                // were; a boss clear then opens the next BOSS and leaves the raids where they are.
                Check(Missions.Cleared(Missions.Siege, 3) && Missions.HighestBeaten(Missions.Siege) == 3 && Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Siege) == 4
                      && Character.BossCleared.TryGetValue("siege", out var sieged) && sieged.Contains(3),
                      $"a siege clear counts on the RAID ladder: level 3 cleared, {Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Siege)} unlocked, filed under its own id");
                Check(!Missions.Cleared(Missions.Bounty, 3) && Missions.HighestBeaten(Missions.Bounty) == 0 && Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Bounty) == 1,
                      $"...and it unlocked NO boss level: the bounties are still at {Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Bounty)} with raids at {Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Siege)}");
                Progression.AwardClear(Missions.Bounty, 1);
                Check(Missions.Cleared(Missions.Bounty, 1) && Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Bounty) == 2
                      && !Missions.Cleared(Missions.Siege, 1) && Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Siege) == 4,
                      $"a boss clear opens the next BOSS and no raid ({Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Bounty)} boss, {Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Siege)} raids)");

                H.EnterSector(Hub.SectorKind.Home); await Wait(1.5);
                // the TIO's ◀ ▶ are capped by whichever ladder the selected operation climbs
                H.SelectMission(Missions.Siege); H.SelectLevel(99); int raidTop = Missions.Level;
                H.SelectMission(Missions.Bounty); H.SelectLevel(99); int bossTop = Missions.Level;
                Check(raidTop == 4 && bossTop == 2,
                      $"the level selector is capped by the OPERATION's own ladder: raids stop at {raidTop}, bosses at {bossTop}");
                Missions.Kind = Missions.Bounty; Missions.Level = 1;
                Character.Unclaimed.Clear();
            }

            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; Character.Save();
            Me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 330); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.5);
            Click(Scr(Hub.BasePos)); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Nodes<BasePanel>().Any(), "left-click on the base station opens the BASE menu");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        }

        // ── radar and the Esc menu ──
        {
            var radar = Nodes<Radar>().First();
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[Settings.RadarSize]), "the radar sits top right at the chosen size");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.EscMenuOpen && Hub.ControlsLocked, "Esc (nothing else open) opens the menu; the helm is locked");
            var menu = Nodes<EscMenu>().First();
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "LARGE").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Settings.RadarSize == 2 && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[2]), "LARGE radar: saved and applied");
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "SMALL").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[0]), "SMALL radar applied");
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "MEDIUM").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "50%").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(Settings.MusicVolume, 0.5f), "music volume set from the menu");
            // ...and the row LIGHTS the live step. The default used to be 0.6, which is not one of
            // the five the menu offers, so the match that picks the lit button found nothing and
            // the row opened with none of them pressed. Both rows are checked: a settings row where
            // nothing is selected looks broken whichever row it is.
            foreach (var g in new[] { "Radar", "Music" })
            {
                var opts = Nodes<Button>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(menu).First(r => r.Name == g)).ToList();
                Check(opts.Count > 1 && opts.Count(b => b.ButtonPressed) == 1,
                      $"the {g} row shows exactly one live choice ({opts.Count(b => b.ButtonPressed)} of {opts.Count} lit)");
            }
            // ...and the harness cap SURVIVES the menu re-applying the volume. Checked here, after
            // a menu has just written the audio settings, rather than at startup where nothing has
            // had a chance to overwrite it. 5% as a literal, never Settings.TestVolume: a check
            // that reads the value it is testing cannot catch the value being wrong.
            int mbus = AudioServer.GetBusIndex("Master");
            float lin = mbus < 0 ? -1f : (float)Mathf.DbToLinear(AudioServer.GetBusVolumeDb(mbus));
            Check(mbus >= 0 && !AudioServer.IsBusMute(mbus) && Mathf.Abs(lin - 0.05f) < 0.001f && Settings.Volume > 0.05f,
                  $"the harness runs at 5% master volume whatever the settings say ({lin * 100:0.0}%, setting {Settings.Volume * 100:0}%)");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!H.EscMenuOpen, "Esc closes the menu");
        }

        // ── music by mood: ambient, 20% combat on a selected enemy, 100% in combat ──
        {
            var sh = Me; var mus = Music.I;
            Check(mus != null, "the music player is running");
            H.ClearSelection();
            await Wait(9.0);
            Check(mus.Target == Music.Mood.Ambient && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.AmbientLevel, 1f) && mus.CombatLevel == 0f, "nothing selected: ambient only");
            H.SelectTarget(H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1"));
            await Wait(2.5);
            Check(mus.Target == Music.Mood.Alert && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.CombatLevel, 0.2f), $"enemy selected: combat music at 20% ({mus.CombatLevel:0.00})");
            Check(Math.Abs(Music.I.AmbientLevel - 0.35f) < 0.02, $"and the ambient at half its alert level under it (0.35: {Music.I.AmbientLevel:0.00})");
            sh.Hit(1, sh.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * 80f); await Wait(2.0);
            Check(sh.InCombat && mus.Target == Music.Mood.Combat && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.CombatLevel, 1f) && mus.AmbientLevel == 0f,
                  "taking damage: combat music at 100%");
            H.ClearSelection();
            await Wait(PlayerShip.CombatHold + 3.0);
            Check(!sh.InCombat && mus.Target == Music.Mood.Ambient, "8 s after the last damage, back to ambient");
            sh.TakeDamage(-1);
        }

        Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 0 && H != null, "Esc closes the K window first");
        H.ClearSelection();
        // a part dropped and not flown over when the pilot quits (as in a victory window)
        Character.Unclaimed.Add("drv_surge_2"); Character.Save(); int surge0 = Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("drv_surge_2");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
        Nodes<Button>(Nodes<EscMenu>().First()).First(b => b.Name == "Quit").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.3);
        Check(GetTree().CurrentScene is MainMenu, "QUIT TO MAIN MENU returns to the main menu");
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://Hub.tscn"); await Wait(0.4);
        Check(H != null && Count<PlayerShip>() == 1, "hub re-enters cleanly");
        Check(Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("drv_surge_2") == surge0 + 1 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0,
              "a part not flown over before a quit is in the hold the next time the pilot enters a world");

        // ── sound: the music 35% quieter; effects by the camera's distance and zoom ──
        Check(Math.Abs(Music.Master - 0.65f) < 1e-6, "the music plays 35% quieter than it started");
        Check(Math.Abs(Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1)) < 1e-4 && Sfx.VolumeAt(1000, 1) < Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1) - 3
              && Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 0.5f) < Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1) - 3 && float.IsNegativeInfinity(Sfx.VolumeAt(6000, 1)),
              "effects: 0 dB at the view's centre, quieter with distance and when zoomed out, silent far away");
        Check(new[] { "laser_light", "laser_fighter", "laser_boss", "laser_hit", "missile_whoosh", "impact_thunk", "cannon" }
                  .All(n => GD.Load<AudioStream>($"res://sfx/{n}.wav") != null),
              "all seven effects load");
        int P(string n) => Sfx.Played.TryGetValue(n, out var v) ? v : 0;
        GD.Print($"  sounds played: light laser {P("laser_light")}, fighter {P("laser_fighter")}, boss laser {P("laser_boss")}, hit {P("laser_hit")}, whoosh {P("missile_whoosh")}, thunk {P("impact_thunk")}, cannon {P("cannon")}");
        Check(P("laser_light") > 0 && P("laser_hit") > 0, "lasers buzz where they fire and crackle where they hit");
        Check(P("laser_boss") > 0, "the boss's lasers have their own, deeper buzz");
        Check(P("missile_whoosh") > 0 && P("impact_thunk") > 0, "missiles and torpedoes whoosh at launch and thunk when they hit");
        Check(P("laser_fighter") > 0, "a carrier's fighters have their own report, not the capital ship's");
        Check(P("cannon") > 0, "the battleship's guns have their own file, not the impact played quietly");

        // ── THE LEVEL IS IN THE FILE, and these read the files to prove it ──────────────
        // Every trim in Sfx is 0 now: a sound's loudness is a property of the sound, not of
        // whoever plays it. That only means anything if the files actually carry those levels,
        // so this measures them. Ratios as literals -- 0.65 is "35% quieter" as a person means
        // it -- never as Sfx constants, which no longer exist to read.
        Check(Math.Abs(Peak("res://sfx/laser_fighter.wav") / Peak("res://sfx/laser_light.wav") - 0.65) < 0.02,
              $"a carrier fighter's report is 35% quieter than a capital ship's, IN THE FILE ({Peak("res://sfx/laser_fighter.wav") / Peak("res://sfx/laser_light.wav"):0.000})");
        // The bomber's launch whoosh, 35% quieter than it used to be heard. The old file is in
        // retired/, and what is compared is the LEVEL IT PLAYS AT: the old file times its old
        // -8 dB trim, which is what the ear was actually getting.
        double oldWhoosh = Peak("res://retired/sfx/missile_whoosh.wav") * Math.Pow(10, -8 / 20.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(Peak("res://sfx/missile_whoosh.wav") / oldWhoosh - 0.65) < 0.02,
              $"a missile launch is 35% quieter than it was heard before ({Peak("res://sfx/missile_whoosh.wav") / oldWhoosh:0.000})");
        // ...and the impact is NOT, because it was already right. Same level as before, to the
        // sample: the old file at its old -2 dB trim.
        double oldThunk = Peak("res://retired/sfx/impact_thunk.wav") * Math.Pow(10, -2 / 20.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(Peak("res://sfx/impact_thunk.wav") / oldThunk - 1.0) < 0.02,
              $"the impact is exactly as loud as it was, because it was already right ({Peak("res://sfx/impact_thunk.wav") / oldThunk:0.000})");

        // leaks: leaving and re-entering the hub must not grow the orphan-node or object
        // counts (the first cycle warms caches; compare the next two against it)
        async Task Cycle()
        {
            GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://MainMenu.tscn"); await Wait(0.5);
            GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://Hub.tscn"); await Wait(1.0);
        }
        // Collect .NET garbage first, so the count shows real leaks, not objects merely
        // waiting for the collector (it runs whenever it likes).
        async Task<double> Settled()
        {
            for (int g = 0; g < 2; g++) { System.GC.Collect(); System.GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers(); }
            for (int f = 0; f < 3; f++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            return Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectCount);
        }
        await Cycle();
        double objA = await Settled(), orphA = Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectOrphanNodeCount);
        await Cycle(); await Cycle();
        double objB = await Settled(), orphB = Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectOrphanNodeCount);
        GD.Print($"  leak check: orphan nodes {orphA:0} -> {orphB:0}; objects {objA:0} -> {objB:0} (after collection)");
        Check(orphB <= orphA, "no orphan nodes accumulate across leaving and re-entering the hub");
        Check(objB <= objA + 20, "no objects accumulate across leaving and re-entering the hub");
        // THE LEVEL COMES WITH THE SECTOR: a peer sent into a world is told the level in the same call,
        // set before the world -- and an arena's boss -- is built from it, rather than left to the
        // mission report that follows. (Sent home again here, where the hub has just been rebuilt.)
        {
            int lv0 = Missions.Level;
            Priv(H, "NetSector", (int)Hub.SectorKind.Home, Missions.Kind, 7);   // the kind rides beside the level now
            int lvSet = Missions.Level;
            await Wait(1.0);                                   // the home world, rebuilt
            Missions.Level = lv0;
            Check(lvSet == 7 && H != null && !Hub.InArena, $"a peer sent into a world is told the level with it, before the world is built ({lvSet})");
        }

        // ── LAST, because it ends a session and that resets the fleet ────────────────────
        // A session can end without anything else moving. Quitting to the menu changes scene and
        // Yard._ExitTree writes on the way out; pressing PLAY OFFLINE, or a host closing the lobby
        // while everyone stands at home, changes nothing but the socket -- and nothing wrote the
        // character on that route, with the autosave up to thirty seconds away.
        //
        // Hosted first so there is a session to leave, and the figure is changed WHILE in it, so a
        // stale file cannot pass: the value on disk has to be the one from inside the session.
        // Placed at the very end on purpose -- going offline re-parks the base and resets the
        // ships, which quietly broke four later checks when this sat in the middle of the run.
        var yard = H.Yard;
        Net.I.Host(27124);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 && !Net.IsOnline; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        bool hosted = Net.IsOnline;
        yard.Credits = 4242;
        // a place held for a pilot that dropped, as the session ends
        var soloHeld = (System.Collections.IDictionary)Session.Places;   // Session.Held is a public record now
        soloHeld["ghost"] = new Session.Held { OldPeer = 515151, Name = "Ghost", Until = Time.GetTicksMsec() + 60000 };
        Priv(H, "RefreshAway");
        bool ghostInParty = H.PartyIds.Contains(515151);
        Net.I.GoOffline();
        await Wait(0.15);
        Check(ghostInParty && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.Idle && H.PartyIds.SequenceEqual(new[] { Net.LocalId }) && soloHeld.Count == 0,
              $"a session ended with a place held: the solo world's party is this pilot alone -- its portal is not kept shut ({string.Join(",", H.PartyIds)})");
        var afterLeaving = new ConfigFile();
        afterLeaving.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
        Check(hosted && Math.Abs((double)afterLeaving.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - 4242) < 1e-6,
              $"leaving a session writes this peer's character, with no scene change to do it for them ({(double)afterLeaving.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0})");
    }

    // ── multiplayer ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // ── a dedicated two-player arena run (its own port, after the three-player run) ──
    // Mission 1: both READY, both at the portal, into the arena together; the boss dies;
    // both home. Mission 2: into the arena again; the host drops -- the guest must land
    // home, offline, with its OWN base (a marker credit total set before it joined).
    async Task ArenaMp(string role)
    {
        const int Port = 27125;
        async Task ToArena(string who, int mission)
        {
            // the home world must be BUILT first: the scene swap is deferred, and for a frame
            // or two the old one is still current
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && (H == null || Hub.InArena || H.GetNodeOrNull("TIO") == null || !H.IsNodeReady()); i++)
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            H.SetMyReady(true);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && H.Mission != Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && !Hub.InArena; i++)
            {
                if (H != null && Me != null && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen) { Me.Position = H.MissionPortalPos; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            await RealTime(() => H != null && H.IsNodeReady() && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss) && Me != null, 5);
            Check(Hub.InArena, $"{who}: into the arena with the other pilot (mission {mission})");
        }
        if (role == "ahost")
        {
            Character.Name = "Arena Host";
            Net.I.Host(Port);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && Net.I.Players.Count < 2; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Net.I.Players.Count == 2, "arena host: the guest joined");
            await Wait(1.0);
            // ── TWO OF THE NINE, IN A SESSION ─────────────────────────────────────
            // Everything the nine classes put on the wire had only ever flown alone: the status
            // bits, the slot array a class names itself, a turret dropped into the world, an
            // effect, and a bubble that covers SOMEBODY ELSE. At home before the first arena trip
            // the host flies a WARRIOR and the guest a FREIGHTER.
            // Each process seeds its own varied geometry, so the rendezvous is a LITERAL and
            // everything varied is the HOST's -- the guest reads it off the wire.
            var mpSpot = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2400, 2400);   // clear of the belt, the wreck, the dummies and the lane
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior);
            var hw = Me; hw.AutopilotTo = null;
            Check(hw.Class == ShipClass.HeavyWarrior && Math.Abs(hw.MaxHp - 175) < 1e-6 && Abilities.For(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior).Length == 9,
                  $"arena host: hosting as a WARRIOR -- 140 x 1.25 for five chips = 175 hull, three abilities and six open slots ({hw.MaxHp:0})");
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine) is { Class: ShipClass.FreightHauler } gg
                                 && gg.Position.DistanceTo(mpSpot) < 60, Wan ? 25 : 15);
            var fg = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine);
            Check(fg != null && fg.Class == ShipClass.FreightHauler && Math.Abs(fg.MaxHp - 500) < 1e-6
                  && fg.Position.DistanceTo(mpSpot) < 60,
                  $"arena host: the guest refitted to a FREIGHTER and parked -- 400 x 1.25 = 500 hull ({fg?.Class}, {fg?.MaxHp:0}, {fg?.Position.DistanceTo(mpSpot):0} u off)");
            hw.Position = VaryNear(fg.Position, 230f); hw.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hw.Rotation = 0f;   // inside its 260 u, any bearing
            await Wait(0.3);
            // ITS TURRET, MADE BY THE HOST: the guest presses T; only the host may put one in the
            // world, and only the host may take it away again.
            await RealTime(() => H.Deployed.Count(t => t.OwnerId == fg.OwnerId) == 1, Wan ? 15 : 8);
            var t1 = H.Deployed.FirstOrDefault(t => t.OwnerId == fg.OwnerId);
            Check(t1 != null && H.Deployed.Count == 1 && fg.TurretsOut == 1
                  && Math.Abs(t1.Hp - 120) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(t1.Spec(true).Damage - fg.Stats["deploy_damage"]) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(fg.Stats["deploy_damage"] - 7.5) < 1e-9,   // 6 x 1.25, the guest's five chips
                  $"arena host: a guest's T reached the host -- one 120-hull turret firing the GUEST's sheet, {t1?.Spec(true).Damage:0.0} a shot, owned by the guest ({H.Deployed.Count} out)");
            H.Drop(fg, VaryNear(fg.Position, 420f), fg.Stats["deploy_hull"]);
            H.Drop(fg, VaryNear(fg.Position, 420f), fg.Stats["deploy_hull"]);
            await Wait(0.3);
            Check(fg.TurretsOut == 3, $"arena host: three of the guest's turrets standing ({fg.TurretsOut})");
            // WHICH ARM HOLDS WHICH SHIP IS THE HOST'S WORD. Put the first gatherer in arm 3 of the
            // base's 5 for the guest to read off the wire: the hologram bars are drawn from
            // Arm.Occupant on every peer, and nothing had ever written it anywhere but here.
            var armShip = H.Yard.Gatherers.FirstOrDefault();
            H.Yard.SetArm(armShip, 3);
            Check(armShip != null && H.Yard.ArmOf(armShip) == 3 && H.Yard.Arms[3].Occupant == armShip
                  && H.Yard.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null) == 1,
                  $"arena host: its first gatherer stands in arm 3 of the base's 5, and no other arm is taken ({(armShip == null ? -1 : H.Yard.ArmOf(armShip))}, {H.Yard.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null)} busy)");
            // 45 into the LAST of them (the first is the one shot off its base below): nothing
            // carried a turret's hull after the drop, so its owner watched a pristine turret take
            // a whole raid and then vanish in a burst.
            var hurtT = H.Deployed.Last(t => t.OwnerId == fg.OwnerId);
            hurtT.TakeDamage(45);
            Check(Math.Abs(hurtT.Hp - 75) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(hurtT.MaxHp - 120) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena host: 45 into one of the guest's turrets leaves 75 of 120 ({hurtT.Hp:0})");
            // THE BUBBLE IS OVER SOMEBODY ELSE. The pool is the GUEST's and the blow lands on the
            // HOST's hull: PlayerShip.ThroughBubbles reads Combat.Players, and until now no check
            // had ever had two pilots in it.
            await RealTime(() => fg.BubbleUp, Wan ? 15 : 8);
            Check(fg.BubbleUp && Math.Abs(fg.BubbleLeft - 400) < 1e-6 && hw.Position.DistanceTo(fg.Position) < 260f,
                  $"arena host: the guest's F raised its 400 bubble, and this hull is inside its 260 u ({hw.Position.DistanceTo(fg.Position):0} u)");
            double hwHull0 = hw.Hp;
            hw.Hit(150, hw.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:mpbubble");
            Check(Math.Abs(hw.Hp - hwHull0) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(fg.BubbleLeft - 250) < 1e-6,
                  $"...and a 150 blow on the HOST's hull comes out of the GUEST's pool ({fg.BubbleLeft:0} of 400 left, hull {hw.Hp:0} of {hwHull0:0})");
            // THE RUSH: a status only the host decides, and an EMP whose ring is an Fx row.
            hw.UseAbility("rush", 0);
            double rushLeft = hw.Sl("rush").Left, rushCool = hw.Sl("rush").Cool;
            Check(hw.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && Math.Abs(rushLeft - 2.5) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(rushCool - 18) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(hw.SpeedMult - 2.5f) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena host: the warrior's rush -- hardened, 2.5 s up, 18 s cooling, 2.5x top speed ({rushLeft:0.0} s, {rushCool:0.0} s)");
            // ...and one of the guest's turrets shot off its base, for the guest to see the gap and the burst
            var doomed = H.Deployed.First(t => t.OwnerId == fg.OwnerId);
            int doomedId = doomed.NetId;
            doomed.TakeDamage(1e12); await Wait(0.4);
            Check(H.Deployed.All(t => t.NetId != doomedId) && fg.TurretsOut == 2,
                  $"arena host: a turret shot off its base is gone here ({fg.TurretsOut} left out)");
            await RealTime(() => fg.TurretsOut == 1, Wan ? 20 : 12);
            Check(fg.TurretsOut == 1 && H.Deployed.Count == 1,
                  $"arena host: the guest's C picked one up again -- one of its three left standing ({fg.TurretsOut})");
            // THE RUSH MUST LAND BEFORE THE REFIT. A refit rebuilds the ship's slots, so a rush
            // still running is discarded with them and its EMP never fires -- which is right (it is
            // a different ship now), but it would leave the guest waiting for a ring that never came.
            await RealTime(() => !hw.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && hw.Sl("rush").Left <= 0, Wan ? 12 : 6);
            await Wait(0.4);
            foreach (var dpl in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl);   // the world clear for the arena
            await Wait(0.3);
            await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);                            // the hull the rest of this scenario flies
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine) is { Class: ShipClass.Destroyer }, Wan ? 20 : 12);
            await ToArena("arena host", 1);
            // Built after a scene change, so from what is REMEMBERED of each pilot, not a fresh
            // announcement. It used to be only names and colours: every boss a solo boss (it counted
            // the ships before there were any), and every guest at default gear.
            Check(Math.Abs(H.Boss.HullMult - 1.6) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(H.Boss.DamageMult - 1.2) < 1e-9,
                  $"arena host: a party of two faces a boss scaled for two (hull x1.6, damage x1.2; x{H.Boss.HullMult:0.00}, x{H.Boss.DamageMult:0.00})");
            var gship = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine);
            Check(gship != null && gship.Class == ShipClass.Destroyer && Math.Abs(gship.MaxHp - 287.5) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(gship.Stats["missile_mag"] - 6) < 1e-9,
                  $"arena host: the guest's destroyer and its own gear came into the arena with it (287.5 hull, a Salvo Rack III's 6 bursts; {gship?.Class}, {gship?.MaxHp:0.0}, {gship?.Stats["missile_mag"]:0})");
            // Long enough for the guest to watch the super-move bar move, and no longer: this
            // scenario has a second arena visit after it, and a stay stretched to fit a whole beam
            // cycle pushed that off the end of every wait that follows.
            await Wait(4.0);
            // the host in stasis at the moment of the kill: a pilot shot down in the fight still
            // helped win it, and gets its own crates -- only its own
            int ahHold0 = Character.GearHold.Values.Sum();
            Me.TakeDamage(Me.Hp + 1);
            H.Boss.TakeDamage(H.Boss.Hp + 1);
            await Wait(0.3);
            var ahCrates = H.Crates.ToList();
            Check(!Me.Alive && ahCrates.Count == 2 && Count<LootCrate>() == 2 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 2,
                  $"arena host: in stasis at the kill, still its own 2 crates, and none of the guest's ({ahCrates.Count} here, {Count<LootCrate>()} nodes)");
            // NO CLOCK: the party goes home only once EVERY present pilot presses RETURN. The guest
            // presses first (it collects, then readies); one pilot ready is not enough on its own.
            // (generously: the guest presses only after its own checks of the fight and its crates,
            // which can run past the kill)
            await RealTime(() => H.ReturnReady == 1, Wan ? 60 : 45);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Hub.InArena && H.ReturnReady == 1 && H.ReturnTotal == 2,
                  $"arena host: one pilot's RETURN is not enough -- the party waits ({H.ReturnReady} of {H.ReturnTotal})");
            H.SetMyReturn(true);                                  // now both (a stasis pilot readies too); home together
            await RealTime(() => !Hub.InArena, 30);
            await RealTime(() => H != null && H.Yard != null && H.IsNodeReady(), 5);   // the home world is built a frame after the sector changes
            Check(!Hub.InArena && Net.IsOnline, "arena host: the boss down, both pilots home -- still hosting");
            Check(Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == ahHold0 + 2 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0 && ahCrates.All(c => !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(c)),
                  "arena host: the crates it never reached came home in its hold; their nodes did not");
            await Wait(2.0);
